    50→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    51→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    52→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    53→    target = cleaned.lower()
    54→
    55→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    56→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    57→            return str(dim_key)
    58→    return slugify(cleaned)
    59→
    60→
    61→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    63→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    64→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    65→    return {
    66→        cleaned.lower(),
    67→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    68→        slugify(cleaned),
    69→        canonical.lower(),
    70→        slugify(canonical),
    71→    }

    88→            continue
    89→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    90→        [REDACTED](name)
    91→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    92→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    93→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    94→            if not key:
    95→                continue
    96→            aliases.add(key)
    97→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    98→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())

   100→        for alias in aliases:
   101→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   102→    return scores

   106→# Synthetic item builders

   108→
   109→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   110→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   111→
   112→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   113→    """
   114→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   115→    order_set = set(order)
   116→    present_ids = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   117→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   118→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   119→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   120→    present_names = {
   121→        name
   122→        for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS
   123→        if sid in present_ids
   124→    }
   125→    present_names.update(recorded_unconfirmed)
   126→    if not present_names:
   127→        return []
   128→
   129→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   130→    open_review_count = sum(
   131→        1 for f in issues.values()
   132→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   133→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   134→    )
   135→
   136→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   137→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   138→    for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
   139→        if name not in present_names:
   140→            continue
   141→        if name in confirmed:
   142→            continue
   143→
   144→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   145→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   146→        blocked_by = sorted(
   147→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps if dep in present_names and dep not in confirmed
   148→        )
   149→
   150→        only_stages = None if name == "commit" else name
   151→        cmd = triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=only_stages)
   152→
   153→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   154→            "id": sid,
   155→            "tier": 1,
   156→            "confidence": "high",
   157→            "detector": "triage",
   158→            "file": ".",
   159→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   160→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   161→            "detail": {
   162→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   163→                "stage": name,
   164→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   165→                "runner_commands": [
   166→                    {"label": label, "command": command}
   167→                    for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
   168→                ],
   169→                "manual_fallback": triage_manual_stage_command(name),
   170→            },
   171→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   172→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   173→        }
   174→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   175→        items.append(item)
   176→    return items
   177→
   178→
   179→def build_subjective_items(
   180→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   181→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   182→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   183→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   184→    if not dim_scores:
   185→        return []
   186→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   187→
   188→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   189→    if not subjective_entries:
   190→        return []
   191→    unassessed_dims = {
   192→        str(name).strip()
   193→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   194→            dim_scores
   195→        )
   196→    }
   197→
   198→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   199→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   200→    for issue in issues.values():
   201→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   202→            continue
   203→        if issue.get("detector") == "review":
   204→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   205→            if dim_key:
   206→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   207→
   208→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   209→    def _prepare_command(
   210→        cli_keys: list[str],
   211→        *,
   212→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   213→    ) -> str:
   214→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   215→        if cli_keys:
   216→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   217→        if force_review_rerun:
   218→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   219→        return command
   220→
   221→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   222→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   223→        if not name:
   224→            continue
   225→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   226→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   227→            continue
   228→
   229→        [REDACTED](name)
   230→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   231→        cli_keys = [
   232→            str(key).strip().lower()
   233→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   234→            if str(key).strip()
   235→        ]
   236→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   237→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   238→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   239→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   240→            name in unassessed_dims
   241→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   242→        )
   243→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   244→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   245→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   246→        if open_review > 0:
   247→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   248→        else:
   249→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   250→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   251→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   252→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   253→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   254→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   255→            "file": ".",
   256→            "confidence": "medium",
   257→            "summary": summary,
   258→            "detail": {
   259→                "dimension_name": name,
   260→                "dimension": dim_key,
   261→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   262→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   263→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   264→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   265→            },
   266→            "status": "open",
   267→        }
   268→        item["kind"] = "subjective_dimension"
   269→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   270→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   271→        items.append(item)
   272→    return items
   273→
   274→

1→"""Triage sync — inject/prune triage stage IDs based on review issue changes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
     7→    TRIAGE_IDS,
     8→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
     9→    QueueSyncResult,
    10→    confirmed_triage_stage_names,
    11→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix,
    12→    recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→
    18→from .defer_policy import (
    19→    DeferEscalationOptions,
    20→    DeferUpdateOptions,
    21→    should_escalate_defer_state,
    22→    update_defer_state,
    23→)
    24→from .triage_start_policy import decide_triage_start
    25→
    26→[REDACTED]
    27→_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD = "deferred_review_ids"
    28→[REDACTED]
    29→
    30→
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→# Helpers
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→
    35→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
    36→    state: StateModel,
    37→    meta: dict,
    38→) -> set[str]:
    39→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
    40→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    41→    active_ids = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
    42→    known_ids = triaged_ids | active_ids
    43→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state) - known_ids if known_ids else set()
    44→
    45→
    46→def _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta: dict) -> set[str]:
    47→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    48→    active_ids = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
    49→    return triaged_ids | active_ids
    50→
    51→
    52→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
    53→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
    54→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    55→        while sid in order:
    56→            order.remove(sid)
    57→
    58→
    59→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
    60→    order: list[str],
    61→    confirmed: set[str],
    62→    *,
    63→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    64→) -> list[str]:
    65→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
    66→
    67→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
    68→    Returns list of injected stage IDs.
    69→    """
    70→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
    71→    existing = set(order)
    72→    injected: list[str] = []
    73→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
    74→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
    75→            if skipped is not None:
    76→                skipped.pop(sid, None)
    77→            order.append(sid)
    78→            injected.append(sid)
    79→            existing.add(sid)
    80→    return injected
    81→
    82→
    83→def _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta: dict) -> None:
    84→    """Clear defer metadata once triage is no longer deferred."""
    85→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
    86→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
    87→
    88→
    89→def _store_triage_meta(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
    90→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
    91→
    92→
    93→def _inject_triage_result(
    94→    order: list[str],
    95→    confirmed: set[str],
    96→    *,
    97→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    98→    normalize: bool = False,
    99→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   100→    injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, confirmed, skipped=skipped)
   101→    if not injected:
   102→        return QueueSyncResult()
   103→    if normalize:
   104→        normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
   105→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=injected)
   106→
   107→
   108→def _mark_triage_deferred(
   109→    plan: PlanModel,
   110→    meta: dict,
   111→    *,
   112→    defer_state: dict,
   113→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   114→    meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   115→    meta["triage_recommended"] = True
   116→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
   117→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   118→    return QueueSyncResult(deferred=True)
   119→
   120→
   121→def _mark_triage_escalated(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   122→    meta[_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY] = True
   123→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   124→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   125→
   126→
   127→def _mark_triage_ready(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   128→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   129→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   130→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   131→
   132→
   133→def _prune_stale_present_stages(
   134→    *,
   135→    plan: PlanModel,
   136→    state: StateModel,
   137→    order: list[str],
   138→    meta: dict,
   139→    last_hash: str,
   140→    confirmed: set[str],
   141→    recorded_unconfirmed: set[str],
   142→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   143→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   144→    if not last_hash or confirmed or recorded_unconfirmed:
   145→        return result
   146→    if not _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta) and stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state):
   147→        return result
   148→    new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   149→    if new_since_triage:
   150→        return result
   151→    _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   152→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   153→    current_hash = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   154→    if current_hash:
   155→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   156→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   157→    result.pruned = list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
   158→    return result
   159→
   160→
   161→def _backfill_partial_triage_snapshot(
   162→    *,
   163→    plan: PlanModel,
   164→    state: StateModel,
   165→    meta: dict,
   166→    last_hash: str,
   167→) -> None:
   168→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   169→    has_completed_stage = any(
   170→        isinstance(v, dict) and v.get("confirmed_at")
   171→        for v in stages.values()
   172→    )
   173→    if not has_completed_stage or meta.get("triaged_ids") or last_hash:
   174→        return
   175→    current_review = sorted(stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state))
   176→    if current_review:
   177→        meta["triaged_ids"] = current_review
   178→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   179→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   180→
   181→
   182→def _defer_or_inject_triage(
   183→    *,
   184→    plan: PlanModel,
   185→    state: StateModel,
   186→    order: list[str],
   187→    meta: dict,
   188→    confirmed: set[str],
   189→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   190→    new_since_triage: set[str],
   191→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   192→    decision = decide_triage_start(
   193→        plan,
   194→        state,
   195→        policy=policy,
   196→        explicit_start=False,
   197→        attested_override=False,
   198→    )
   199→    if decision.action == "defer":
   200→        defer_state = update_defer_state(
   201→            meta.get(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY),
   202→            state=state,
   203→            deferred_ids=new_since_triage,
   204→            options=DeferUpdateOptions(
   205→                deferred_ids_field=_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   206→            ),
   207→        )
   208→        meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   209→        escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   210→            defer_state,
   211→            state=state,
   212→            options=DeferEscalationOptions(
   213→                deferred_ids_field=_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   214→            ),
   215→        )
   216→        if not escalated:
   217→            return _mark_triage_deferred(plan, meta, defer_state=defer_state)
   218→        _mark_triage_escalated(plan, meta)
   219→        return _inject_triage_result(
   220→            order,
   221→            confirmed,
   222→            skipped=plan.get("skipped", {}),
   223→            normalize=True,
   224→        )
   225→
   226→    _mark_triage_ready(plan, meta)
   227→    return _inject_triage_result(
   228→        order,
   229→        confirmed,
   230→        skipped=plan.get("skipped", {}),
   231→    )
   232→
   233→
   234→def _sync_hash_change(
   235→    *,
   236→    plan: PlanModel,
   237→    state: StateModel,
   238→    order: list[str],
   239→    meta: dict,
   240→    confirmed: set[str],
   241→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   242→    current_hash: str,
   243→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   244→    new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   245→    if not new_since_triage and not _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta):
   246→        new_since_triage = stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state)
   247→    if new_since_triage:
   248→        return _defer_or_inject_triage(
   249→            plan=plan,
   250→            state=state,
   251→            order=order,
   252→            meta=meta,
   253→            confirmed=confirmed,
   254→            policy=policy,
   255→            new_since_triage=new_since_triage,
   256→        )
   257→
   258→    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   259→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   260→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   261→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   262→    return QueueSyncResult()
   263→
   264→
   265→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   266→# Public API
   267→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   268→
   269→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   270→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   271→
   272→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   273→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   274→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   275→    resolved since then.
   276→
   277→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   278→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   279→    through the plan.
   280→    """
   281→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   282→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state)
   283→
   284→
   285→def compute_open_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   286→    """Return the set of currently-open review/concerns issue IDs."""
   287→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state)
   288→
   289→
   290→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   291→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   292→
   293→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   294→    """
   295→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   296→
   297→
   298→def sync_triage_needed(
   299→    plan: PlanModel,
   300→    state: StateModel,
   301→    *,
   302→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   303→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   304→    """Append triage stage IDs to back of queue when review issues change.
   305→
   306→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   307→
   308→    **Mid-cycle guard**: when the objective backlog still has work, triage
   309→    stages are NOT injected.  Instead, ``epic_triage_meta["triage_recommended"]``
   310→    is set so the UI can show a non-blocking banner.  Stages are injected
   311→    once the objective backlog drains (or on manual ``plan triage``). If
   312→    deferral repeats for multiple scans/days, triage escalates and stages are
   313→    injected in workflow/triage priority order with a forced-visibility marker.
   314→
   315→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   316→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   317→
   318→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   319→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   320→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   321→    """
   322→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   323→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   324→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   325→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   326→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   327→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   328→
   329→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   330→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   331→
   332→    current_hash = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   333→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   334→
   335→    if already_present:
   336→        return _prune_stale_present_stages(
   337→            plan=plan,
   338→            state=state,
   339→            order=order,
   340→            meta=meta,
   341→            last_hash=last_hash,
   342→            confirmed=confirmed,
   343→            recorded_unconfirmed=recorded_unconfirmed,
   344→        )
   345→
   346→    _backfill_partial_triage_snapshot(
   347→        plan=plan,
   348→        state=state,
   349→        meta=meta,
   350→        last_hash=last_hash,
   351→    )
   352→
   353→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   354→        return _sync_hash_change(
   355→            plan=plan,
   356→            state=state,
   357→            order=order,
   358→            meta=meta,
   359→            confirmed=confirmed,
   360→            policy=policy,
   361→            current_hash=current_hash,
   362→        )
   363→
   364→    return result
   365→
   366→
   367→__all__ = [
   368→    "compute_open_issue_ids",
   369→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   370→    "is_triage_stale",
   371→    "sync_triage_needed",
   372→]
   373→

1→"""Canonical queue snapshot for phase and visibility decisions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Iterable
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass

     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE

    33→)
    34→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    35→
    36→PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL = "review_initial"
    37→PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    38→PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    39→PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT = "review_postflight"
    40→PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT = "workflow_postflight"
    41→PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT = "triage_postflight"
    42→
    43→
    44→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    45→class QueueSnapshot:
    46→    """Canonical queue facts and partitions for one invocation."""
    47→
    48→    phase: str
    49→    all_objective_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    50→    all_initial_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    51→    all_postflight_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    52→    all_scan_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    53→    all_postflight_workflow_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    54→    all_postflight_triage_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    55→    execution_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    56→    backlog_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]

    59→    objective_execution_count: int
    60→    objective_backlog_count: int
    61→    subjective_initial_count: int
    62→    subjective_postflight_count: int
    63→    workflow_postflight_count: int
    64→    triage_pending_count: int
    65→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers: bool
    66→
    67→
    68→def _option_value(options: object | None, name: str, default: Any) -> Any:
    69→    if options is None:
    70→        return default
    71→    return getattr(options, name, default)
    72→
    73→
    74→def _resolved_scan_path(options: object | None, state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    75→    scan_path = _option_value(options, "scan_path", state.get("scan_path"))
    76→    if hasattr(scan_path, "__class__") and scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState":
    77→        return state.get("scan_path")
    78→    return scan_path
    79→
    80→
    81→def _is_fresh_boundary(plan: dict | None) -> bool:
    82→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    83→        return True
    84→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    85→    if not scores:
    86→        return True
    87→    return isinstance(scores, dict) and bool(scores.get("reset"))
    88→
    89→
    90→def _is_objective_item(item: WorkQueueItem, *, skipped_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    91→    return (
    92→        item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
    93→        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    94→        and item.get("id", "") not in skipped_ids
    95→    )
    96→
    97→
    98→def _review_issue_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    99→    return [
   100→        item for item in items
   101→        if item.get("detector", "") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
   102→    ]
   103→
   104→
   105→def _executable_review_issue_items(
   106→    plan: dict | None,
   107→    state: StateModel,
   108→    review_issue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   109→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   110→    """Hide raw review findings until triage is current for the live issue set."""
   111→    if not review_issue_items or not isinstance(plan, dict):
   112→        return review_issue_items
   113→
   114→    triage_snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(plan, state)
   115→    if triage_snapshot.has_triage_in_queue:
   116→        return []
   117→    if triage_snapshot.is_triage_stale:
   118→        return []
   119→    if not triage_snapshot.triage_has_run:
   120→        return []
   121→    return review_issue_items
   122→
   123→
   124→def _subjective_partitions(
   125→    state: StateModel,
   126→    *,
   127→    scoped_issues: dict[str, dict],
   128→    threshold: float,
   129→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   130→    candidates = build_subjective_items(state, scoped_issues, threshold=threshold)
   131→    initial = [item for item in candidates if item.get("initial_review")]
   132→    postflight = [item for item in candidates if not item.get("initial_review")]
   133→    return initial, postflight
   134→
   135→
   136→def _workflow_partitions(
   137→    plan: dict | None,
   138→    state: StateModel,
   139→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   140→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   141→        return [], [], []
   142→    scan_items = [
   143→        item
   144→        for item in (
   145→            build_deferred_disposition_item(plan),
   146→            build_run_scan_item(plan),
   147→        )
   148→        if item is not None
   149→    ]
   150→    postflight_workflow = [
   151→        item
   152→        for item in (
   153→            build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state),
   154→            build_import_scores_item(plan, state),
   155→            build_communicate_score_item(plan, state),
   156→            build_create_plan_item(plan),
   157→        )
   158→        if item is not None
   159→    ]
   160→    triage_items = build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)
   161→    return scan_items, postflight_workflow, triage_items
   162→
   163→
   164→def _phase_for_snapshot(
   165→    *,
   166→    fresh_boundary: bool,
   167→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   168→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   169→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   170→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   171→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   172→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   173→) -> str:
   174→    if fresh_boundary and initial_review_items:
   175→        return PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   176→    if objective_items:
   177→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   178→    if scan_items:
   179→        return PHASE_SCAN
   180→    if postflight_review_items:
   181→        return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   182→    if postflight_workflow_items:
   183→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   184→    if triage_items:
   185→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   186→    return PHASE_SCAN
   187→
   188→
   189→def _execution_items_for_phase(
   190→    phase: str,
   191→    *,
   192→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   193→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   194→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   195→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   196→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   197→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   198→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   199→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL:
   200→        return initial_review_items
   201→    if phase == PHASE_EXECUTE:
   202→        return objective_items
   203→    if phase == PHASE_SCAN:
   204→        deferred_items = [
   205→            item for item in scan_items
   206→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID
   207→        ]
   208→        if deferred_items:
   209→            return deferred_items
   210→        return [
   211→            item for item in scan_items
   212→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
   213→        ]
   214→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   215→        return postflight_review_items
   216→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   217→        return postflight_workflow_items
   218→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   219→        return triage_items
   220→    return []
   221→
   222→
   223→def build_queue_snapshot(
   224→    state: StateModel,
   225→    *,
   226→    options: object | None = None,
   227→    plan: dict | None = None,
   228→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   229→) -> QueueSnapshot:
   230→    """Build the canonical queue snapshot for the current state."""
   231→    context = _option_value(options, "context", None)
   232→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else (
   233→        plan if plan is not None else _option_value(options, "plan", None)
   234→    )
   235→    scan_path = _resolved_scan_path(options, state)
   236→    skipped_ids = set((effective_plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   237→    scoped_issues = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   238→    scope = _option_value(options, "scope", None)
   239→    chronic = bool(_option_value(options, "chronic", False))
   240→
   241→    all_issue_items = build_issue_items(
   242→        state,
   243→        scan_path=scan_path,
   244→        status_filter="open",
   245→        scope=scope,
   246→        chronic=chronic,
   247→    )
   248→    objective_items = [
   249→        item for item in all_issue_items
   250→        if _is_objective_item(item, skipped_ids=skipped_ids)
   251→    ]
   252→    planned_ids = _planned_objective_ids(
   253→        {item.get("id", "") for item in objective_items},
   254→        effective_plan,
   255→    )
   256→    planned_objective_items = [
   257→        item for item in objective_items
   258→        if item.get("id", "") in planned_ids
   259→    ]
   260→    review_issue_items = _review_issue_items(all_issue_items)
   261→    executable_review_items = _executable_review_issue_items(
   262→        effective_plan,
   263→        state,
   264→        review_issue_items,
   265→    )
   266→    initial_review_items, subjective_postflight_items = _subjective_partitions(
   267→        state,
   268→        scoped_issues=scoped_issues,
   269→        threshold=target_strict,
   270→    )
   271→    postflight_review_items = [*subjective_postflight_items, *executable_review_items]
   272→    scan_items, postflight_workflow_items, triage_items = _workflow_partitions(
   273→        effective_plan,
   274→        state,
   275→    )
   276→
   277→    fresh_boundary = _is_fresh_boundary(effective_plan)
   278→    phase = _phase_for_snapshot(
   279→        fresh_boundary=fresh_boundary,
   280→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   281→        objective_items=planned_objective_items,
   282→        scan_items=scan_items,
   283→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   284→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   285→        triage_items=triage_items,
   286→    )
   287→    execution_items = _execution_items_for_phase(
   288→        phase,
   289→        objective_items=planned_objective_items,
   290→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   291→        scan_items=scan_items,
   292→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   293→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   294→        triage_items=triage_items,
   295→    )
   296→
   297→    execution_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in execution_items}
   298→    backlog_items = [
   299→        item for item in (
   300→            [
   301→                *planned_objective_items,
   302→                *initial_review_items,
   303→                *subjective_postflight_items,
   304→                *review_issue_items,
   305→                *scan_items,
   306→                *postflight_workflow_items,
   307→                *triage_items,
   308→            ]
   309→        )
   310→        if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   311→    ]
   312→    objective_backlog_count = sum(
   313→        1 for item in planned_objective_items if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   314→    )
   315→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers = len(planned_objective_items) < len(objective_items)
   316→
   317→    return QueueSnapshot(
   318→        phase=phase,
   319→        all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items),
   320→        all_initial_review_items=tuple(initial_review_items),
   321→        all_postflight_review_items=tuple(postflight_review_items),
   322→        all_scan_items=tuple(scan_items),
   323→        all_postflight_workflow_items=tuple(postflight_workflow_items),
   324→        all_postflight_triage_items=tuple(triage_items),
   325→        execution_items=tuple(execution_items),
   326→        backlog_items=tuple(backlog_items),
   327→        objective_in_scope_count=len(objective_items),
   328→        planned_objective_count=len(planned_objective_items),
   329→        objective_execution_count=sum(
   330→            1
   331→            for item in execution_items
   332→            if item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   333→            and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   334→        ),
   335→        objective_backlog_count=objective_backlog_count,
   336→        subjective_initial_count=len(initial_review_items),
   337→        subjective_postflight_count=len(postflight_review_items),
   338→        workflow_postflight_count=len(postflight_workflow_items),
   339→        triage_pending_count=len(triage_items),
   340→        has_unplanned_objective_blockers=has_unplanned_objective_blockers,
   341→    )
   342→
   343→
   344→def coarse_phase_name(phase: str) -> str:
   345→    """Map internal queue phases to the persisted coarse lifecycle value."""
   346→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL or phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   347→        return "review"
   348→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   349→        return "workflow"
   350→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   351→        return "triage"
   352→    return phase
   353→
   354→
   355→__all__ = [
   356→    "PHASE_EXECUTE",
   357→    "PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL",
   358→    "PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT",
   359→    "PHASE_SCAN",
   360→    "PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT",
   361→    "PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT",
   362→    "QueueSnapshot",
   363→    "build_queue_snapshot",
   364→    "coarse_phase_name",
   365→]
   366→

1→"""Unified work-queue entrypoints for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.finalize import finalize_queue
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.inputs import resolve_queue_inputs
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import (
    10→    QueueBuildOptions,
    11→    QueueVisibility,
    12→    WorkQueueResult,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import group_queue_items
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.selection import select_queue_items
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→def build_work_queue(
    20→    state: StateModel,
    21→    *,
    22→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    23→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    24→    """Build the active canonical work queue for the current invocation."""
    25→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    26→        state,
    27→        options=options,
    28→        visibility=QueueVisibility.ALL,
    29→    )
    30→
    31→
    32→def _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    33→    state: StateModel,
    34→    *,
    35→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    36→    visibility: str = QueueVisibility.ALL,
    37→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    38→    """Build a ranked work queue from canonical queue partitions.
    39→
    40→    Open-status queue views are resolved from the canonical queue snapshot so
    41→    lifecycle phase, execution visibility, and backlog visibility all come
    42→    from one source of truth. Non-open status views remain issue-list queries.
    43→    """
    44→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    45→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = resolve_queue_inputs(opts, state)
    46→    items = select_queue_items(
    47→        state,
    48→        opts=opts,
    49→        plan=plan,
    50→        scan_path=scan_path,
    51→        status=status,
    52→        threshold=threshold,
    53→        visibility=visibility,
    54→    )
    55→    return finalize_queue(items, state=state, plan=plan, opts=opts)
    56→
    57→__all__ = [
    58→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
    59→    "QueueBuildOptions",
    60→    "QueueContext",
    61→    "QueueVisibility",
    62→    "WorkQueueResult",
    63→    "_build_work_queue_with_visibility",
    64→    "build_work_queue",
    65→    "collapse_clusters",
    66→    "group_queue_items",
    67→]
    68→

    30→def _workflow_action_item(
    31→    *,
    32→    item_id: str,

    34→    primary_command: str,

    52→def _confirm_attestation_hint(stage: str) -> str:
    53→    if stage == "observe":
    54→        return "I have thoroughly reviewed..."
    55→    if stage == "reflect":
    56→        return "My strategy accounts for..."
    57→    if stage == "organize":
    58→        return "This plan is correct..."
    59→    if stage == "enrich":
    60→        return "Steps are executor-ready..."
    61→    if stage == "sense-check":
    62→        return "Content and structure verified..."
    63→    return "..."
    64→
    65→
    66→def _runner_planning_tools(
    67→    *,
    68→    only_stages: str | None = None,
    69→    manual_fallback: str | None = None,
    70→) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    71→    tools = [
    72→        {"label": label, "command": command}
    73→        for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
    74→    ]
    75→    if manual_fallback:
    76→        tools.append({"label": "Manual fallback", "command": manual_fallback})
    77→    tools.append({"label": "Dashboard", "command": "desloppify plan triage"})
    78→    return tools
    79→
    80→
    81→def _triage_progression_target(plan: dict) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    82→    """Return current triage stage target and whether it requires confirmation."""
    83→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    84→    triage_stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {}) or {}
    85→    for stage, _sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    86→        stage_payload = triage_stages.get(stage)
    87→        if isinstance(stage_payload, dict) and stage_payload and not stage_payload.get("confirmed_at"):
    88→            return stage, True
    89→
    90→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    91→    for stage, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    92→        if sid not in order:
    93→            continue
    94→        return stage, False
    95→
    96→    if "triage::commit" in order:
    97→        return "commit", False
    98→
    99→    return None, False
   100→
   101→
   102→def _create_plan_primary_command(plan: dict) -> str:
   103→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
   104→    if stage is None:
   105→        return triage_run_stages_command()
   106→    if needs_confirm:
   107→        attestation = _confirm_attestation_hint(stage)
   108→        return (
   109→            f'desloppify plan triage --confirm {stage} '
   110→            f'--attestation "{attestation}"'
   111→        )
   112→    if stage == "commit":
   113→        return triage_run_stages_command()
   114→    return triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=stage)
   115→
   116→
   117→def _create_plan_planning_tools(plan: dict) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
   118→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
   119→    if needs_confirm:
   120→        return []
   121→    if stage == "commit":
   122→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   123→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("commit"),
   124→        )
   125→    if stage:
   126→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   127→            only_stages=stage,
   128→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command(stage),
   129→        )
   130→    return _runner_planning_tools(
   131→        only_stages="observe",
   132→        manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   133→    )
   134→
   135→
   136→def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   137→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` if queued."""
   138→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   142→
   143→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   144→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   145→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   146→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
   147→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   148→
   149→    return _workflow_action_item(
   150→        item_id=WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
   151→        summary=f"Score checkpoint: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   152→        detail={
   153→            "strict": strict,
   154→            "plan_start_strict": plan_start,
   155→            "delta": delta,
   156→            "planning_tools": _runner_planning_tools(
   157→                only_stages="observe",
   158→                manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   159→            ),
   160→        },
   161→        primary_command=triage_run_stages_command(only_stages="observe"),
   162→    )
   163→
   164→
   165→def build_create_plan_item(plan: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   166→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::create-plan`` if queued."""
   167→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   168→        return None
   169→
   170→    return _workflow_action_item(
   171→        item_id=WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   172→        summary="Create prioritized plan from review results",
   173→        detail={
   174→            "planning_tools": _create_plan_planning_tools(plan),
   175→        },

   177→    )
   178→

   180→def build_import_scores_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   181→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::import-scores`` if queued."""
   182→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   183→        return None
   184→    meta = pending_import_scores_meta(plan, state)
   185→    import_file = (meta.import_file if meta is not None else "").strip() or "issues.json"
   186→    quoted_import_file = shlex.quote(import_file)
   187→    packet_sha = meta.packet_sha256 if meta is not None else ""
   188→    explanation = (
   189→        "Review issues were imported but assessment scores were skipped "
   190→        "(untrusted source). Re-import the same batch with attestation to update dimension scores."
   191→    )
   192→    if import_file != "issues.json":
   193→        explanation += f" Expected import file: `{import_file}`."
   194→    if packet_sha:
   195→        explanation += f" Expected packet sha256: `{packet_sha}`."
   196→
   197→    return _workflow_action_item(
   198→        item_id=WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
   199→        summary="Import assessment scores with attestation",
   200→        detail={
   201→            "explanation": explanation,
   202→            "expected_import_file": import_file,
   203→            "packet_sha256": packet_sha,
   204→        },
   205→        primary_command=(
   206→            f"desloppify review --import {quoted_import_file} --attested-external "
   207→            '--attest "I validated this review was completed without awareness '
   208→            'of overall score and is unbiased."'
   209→        ),
   210→    )
   211→
   212→
   213→def build_communicate_score_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   214→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::communicate-score`` if queued."""
   215→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   216→        return None
   217→
   218→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   219→
   220→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   221→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   222→
   223→    # Use previous_plan_start_scores (stashed at rebaseline) for the delta
   224→    # so the user sees old-baseline → current, not current → current.
   225→    prev_start = (plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   226→    if prev_start is None:
   227→        prev_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   228→    delta = round(strict - prev_start, 1) if prev_start is not None else None
   229→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   230→
   231→    return _workflow_action_item(
   232→        item_id=WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
   233→        summary=f"Communicate updated score to user: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   234→        detail={
   235→            "strict": strict,
   236→            "previous_plan_start_strict": prev_start,
   237→            "delta": delta,
   238→        },
   239→        primary_command=(
   240→            f'desloppify plan resolve "{WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID}" '
   241→            '--note "Score communicated" --confirm'
   242→        ),
   243→    )
   244→
   245→
   246→def build_run_scan_item(plan: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   247→    """Build a synthetic item for the first post-flight scan step."""
   248→    if not postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   249→        return None
   250→
   251→    item = _workflow_action_item(
   252→        item_id=WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
   253→        summary="Run post-flight scan to refresh queue and surface follow-up review work.",
   254→        detail={
   255→            "phase": "postflight_scan",
   256→        },
   257→        primary_command="desloppify scan",
   258→    )
   259→    item["execution_visibility"] = "always"
   260→    return item
   261→
   262→
   263→def _temporary_skipped_ids(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   264→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   265→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
   266→        return []
   267→    deferred: list[str] = []
   268→    for issue_id, entry in skipped.items():
   269→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   270→            continue
   271→        if str(entry.get("kind", "temporary")) == "temporary":
   272→            deferred.append(str(issue_id))
   273→    deferred.sort()
   274→    return deferred
   275→
   276→
   277→def _deferred_cluster_breakdown(
   278→    plan: dict,
   279→    deferred_ids: list[str],
   280→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   281→    deferred_set = set(deferred_ids)
   282→    if not deferred_set:
   283→        return 0, 0
   284→
   285→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   286→    if not isinstance(clusters, dict):
   287→        return 0, len(deferred_ids)
   288→
   289→    covered_by_cluster: set[str] = set()
   290→    cluster_count = 0
   291→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   292→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   293→            continue
   294→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   295→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   296→            continue
   297→        matched = {str(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids if str(issue_id) in deferred_set}
   298→        if not matched:
   299→            continue
   300→        cluster_count += 1
   301→        covered_by_cluster.update(matched)
   302→
   303→    individual_count = len(deferred_set - covered_by_cluster)
   304→    return cluster_count, individual_count
   305→
   306→
   307→def build_deferred_disposition_item(plan: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   308→    """Build a synthetic item prompting deferred backlog disposition."""
   309→    deferred_ids = _temporary_skipped_ids(plan)
   310→    if not deferred_ids:
   311→        return None
   312→
   313→    count = len(deferred_ids)
   314→    cluster_count, individual_count = _deferred_cluster_breakdown(plan, deferred_ids)
   315→    cluster_label = "cluster" if cluster_count == 1 else "clusters"
   316→    individual_label = "item" if individual_count == 1 else "items"
   317→    reactivate_cmd = 'desloppify plan unskip "*"'
   318→    subset_reactivate_cmd = "desloppify plan unskip <cluster-or-id>"
   319→    inspect_cmd = "desloppify plan queue --include-skipped"
   320→    inspect_item_cmd = "desloppify show <cluster-or-id>"
   321→    backlog_cmd = 'desloppify plan backlog "*"'
   322→    subset_backlog_cmd = "desloppify plan backlog <cluster-or-id>"
   323→    wontfix_cmd = (
   324→        'desloppify plan skip --permanent "*" '
   325→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   326→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently." '
   327→        "--confirm"
   328→    )
   329→    subset_wontfix_cmd = (
   330→        "desloppify plan skip --permanent <cluster-or-id> "
   331→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   332→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently."'
   333→    )
   334→
   335→    item = _workflow_action_item(
   336→        item_id=WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
   337→        summary=(
   338→            "Deferred backlog decision required: "
   339→            f"{cluster_count} {cluster_label} + {individual_count} individual {individual_label} "
   340→            "must be reactivated, moved to backlog, or marked wontfix."
   341→        ),
   342→        detail={
   343→            "temporary_skipped_count": count,
   344→            "deferred_cluster_count": cluster_count,
   345→            "deferred_individual_count": individual_count,
   346→            "reactivate_command": reactivate_cmd,
   347→            "backlog_command": backlog_cmd,
   348→            "wontfix_command": wontfix_cmd,
   349→            "planning_tools": [
   350→                {
   351→                    "label": "Review deferred backlog",
   352→                    "command": inspect_cmd,
   353→                },
   354→                {
   355→                    "label": "Inspect a specific cluster or item",
   356→                    "command": inspect_item_cmd,
   357→                },
   358→                {
   359→                    "label": "Reactivate a subset",
   360→                    "command": subset_reactivate_cmd,
   361→                },
   362→                {
   363→                    "label": "Move a subset to backlog",
   364→                    "command": subset_backlog_cmd,
   365→                },
   366→                {
   367→                    "label": "Mark a subset as permanent wontfix",
   368→                    "command": subset_wontfix_cmd,
   369→                },
   370→            ],
   371→            "decision_options": [
   372→                {
   373→                    "label": "Reactivate deferred work",
   374→                    "command": reactivate_cmd,
   375→                },
   376→                {
   377→                    "label": "Move deferred work to backlog",
   378→                    "command": backlog_cmd,
   379→                },
   380→                {
   381→                    "label": "Convert deferred work to wontfix",
   382→                    "command": wontfix_cmd,
   383→                },
   384→            ],
   385→        },
   386→        primary_command=reactivate_cmd,
   387→    )
   388→    item["execution_visibility"] = "always"
   389→    return item
   390→

1→"""Canonical triage coverage and status snapshot helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import TRIAGE_IDS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import open_review_ids
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.playbook import TriageProgress, compute_triage_progress
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    11→
    12→
    13→def _normalized_issue_id_list(raw_ids: object) -> list[str]:
    14→    normalized: list[str] = []
    15→    seen: set[str] = set()
    16→    if not isinstance(raw_ids, list):
    17→        return normalized
    18→    for raw_id in raw_ids:
    19→        if not isinstance(raw_id, str):
    20→            continue
    21→        issue_id = raw_id.strip()
    22→        if not issue_id or issue_id in seen:
    23→            continue
    24→        seen.add(issue_id)
    25→        normalized.append(issue_id)
    26→    return normalized
    27→
    28→
    29→def _cluster_issue_ids(cluster: dict[str, object]) -> list[str]:
    30→    ordered: list[str] = []
    31→    seen: set[str] = set()
    32→
    33→    def _append(raw_ids: object) -> None:
    34→        if not isinstance(raw_ids, list):
    35→            return
    36→        for raw_id in raw_ids:
    37→            if not isinstance(raw_id, str):
    38→                continue
    39→            issue_id = raw_id.strip()
    40→            if not issue_id or issue_id in seen:
    41→                continue
    42→            seen.add(issue_id)
    43→            ordered.append(issue_id)
    44→
    45→    _append(cluster.get("issue_ids"))
    46→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps")
    47→    if isinstance(steps, list):
    48→        for step in steps:
    49→            if not isinstance(step, dict):
    50→                continue
    51→            _append(step.get("issue_refs"))
    52→    return ordered
    53→
    54→
    55→def plan_review_ids(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
    56→    """Return review/concerns IDs currently represented in queue_order."""
    57→    return [
    58→        issue_id
    59→        for issue_id in plan.get("queue_order", [])
    60→        if isinstance(issue_id, str)
    61→        and not issue_id.startswith("triage::")
    62→        and not issue_id.startswith("workflow::")
    63→        and (issue_id.startswith("review::") or issue_id.startswith("concerns::"))
    64→    ]
    65→
    66→
    67→def coverage_open_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    68→    """Return the frozen or live open review IDs covered by this triage run."""
    69→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    70→    active_ids = _normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids"))
    71→    if active_ids:
    72→        return set(active_ids)
    73→    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    74→    review_ids = open_review_ids(state)
    75→    if not has_completed_scan and not review_ids:
    76→        return set(plan_review_ids(plan))
    77→    return review_ids
    78→
    79→
    80→def active_triage_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel | None = None) -> set[str]:
    81→    """Return the frozen review issue set for the current triage run."""
    82→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    83→    active_ids = _normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids"))
    84→    if active_ids:
    85→        return set(active_ids)
    86→    if state is None:
    87→        return set()
    88→    return coverage_open_ids(plan, state)
    89→
    90→
    91→def _explicit_active_triage_issue_ids(plan: dict) -> set[str]:
    92→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    93→    return set(_normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids")))
    94→
    95→
    96→def live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel | None = None) -> set[str]:
    97→    """Return frozen triage IDs that are still open review issues in state."""
    98→    frozen_ids = active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    99→    if state is None or not frozen_ids:
   100→        return frozen_ids
   101→    return frozen_ids & open_review_ids(state)
   102→
   103→
   104→def undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel | None = None) -> list[str]:
   105→    """Return frozen triage issues still lacking cluster/skip/dismiss coverage."""
   106→    target_ids = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   107→    if not target_ids:
   108→        return []
   109→
   110→    covered_ids: set[str] = set()
   111→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   112→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict) or cluster.get("auto"):
   113→            continue
   114→        covered_ids.update(_cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   115→
   116→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   117→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
   118→        covered_ids.update(
   119→            issue_id for issue_id in skipped if isinstance(issue_id, str)
   120→        )
   121→
   122→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   123→    covered_ids.update(_normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get("dismissed_ids")))
   124→
   125→    dispositions = meta.get("issue_dispositions", {})
   126→    if isinstance(dispositions, dict) and isinstance(skipped, dict):
   127→        for issue_id, disposition in dispositions.items():
   128→            if (
   129→                isinstance(issue_id, str)
   130→                and isinstance(disposition, dict)
   131→                and disposition.get("decision_source") == "observe_auto"
   132→                and issue_id in skipped
   133→            ):
   134→                covered_ids.add(issue_id)
   135→
   136→    return sorted(issue_id for issue_id in target_ids if issue_id not in covered_ids)
   137→
   138→
   139→def triage_coverage(
   140→    plan: dict,
   141→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   142→) -> tuple[int, int, dict[str, dict]]:
   143→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage."""
   144→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   145→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
   146→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   147→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   148→            all_cluster_ids.update(_cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   149→    review_ids = list(open_review_ids) if open_review_ids is not None else plan_review_ids(plan)
   150→    organized = sum(1 for issue_id in review_ids if issue_id in all_cluster_ids)
   151→    return organized, len(review_ids), clusters
   152→
   153→
   154→def manual_clusters_with_issues(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   155→    """Return manual clusters that currently own at least one issue."""
   156→    return [
   157→        name
   158→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   159→        if isinstance(cluster, dict) and _cluster_issue_ids(cluster) and not cluster.get("auto")
   160→    ]
   161→
   162→
   163→def find_cluster_for(issue_id: str, clusters: dict[str, dict]) -> str | None:
   164→    """Return the owning cluster name for an issue ID, if any."""
   165→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   166→        if isinstance(cluster, dict) and issue_id in _cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   167→            return name
   168→    return None
   169→
   170→
   171→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   172→class TriageSnapshot:
   173→    """Single shared triage-status snapshot for command surfaces."""
   174→
   175→    progress: TriageProgress
   176→    frozen_issue_ids: frozenset[str]
   177→    live_open_ids: frozenset[str]
   178→    new_since_triage_ids: frozenset[str]
   179→    undispositioned_ids: frozenset[str]
   180→    organized_count: int
   181→    total_in_scope: int
   182→    has_triage_in_queue: bool
   183→    is_triage_stale: bool
   184→    cycle_active: bool
   185→    triage_has_run: bool
   186→
   187→
   188→def build_triage_snapshot(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> TriageSnapshot:
   189→    """Build a canonical triage snapshot from plan and state."""
   190→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   191→    triaged_ids = set(_normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get("triaged_ids")))
   192→    frozen_ids = _explicit_active_triage_issue_ids(plan) & open_review_ids(state)
   193→    live_open = open_review_ids(state)
   194→    known_ids = triaged_ids | frozen_ids
   195→    new_since = live_open - known_ids if known_ids else set()
   196→    in_scope_ids = coverage_open_ids(plan, state)
   197→    organized_count, total_in_scope, _clusters = triage_coverage(
   198→        plan,
   199→        open_review_ids=in_scope_ids,
   200→    )
   201→
   202→    return TriageSnapshot(
   203→        progress=compute_triage_progress(meta.get("triage_stages", {})),
   204→        frozen_issue_ids=frozenset(frozen_ids),
   205→        live_open_ids=frozenset(live_open),
   206→        new_since_triage_ids=frozenset(new_since),
   207→        undispositioned_ids=frozenset(undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)),
   208→        organized_count=organized_count,
   209→        total_in_scope=total_in_scope,
   210→        has_triage_in_queue=bool(set(plan.get("queue_order", [])) & TRIAGE_IDS),
   211→        is_triage_stale=bool(new_since),
   212→        cycle_active=bool(frozen_ids),
   213→        triage_has_run=bool(triaged_ids),
   214→    )
   215→
   216→
   217→__all__ = [
   218→    "TriageSnapshot",
   219→    "active_triage_issue_ids",
   220→    "build_triage_snapshot",
   221→    "coverage_open_ids",
   222→    "find_cluster_for",
   223→    "live_active_triage_issue_ids",
   224→    "manual_clusters_with_issues",
   225→    "plan_review_ids",
   226→    "triage_coverage",
   227→    "undispositioned_triage_issue_ids",
   228→]
   229→

1→"""Helpers for the persisted queue lifecycle phase.
     2→
     3→The persisted phase is the normal source of truth for CLI/debugging, but it is
     4→not trusted blindly. Queue assembly still re-resolves the active phase from the
     5→currently visible items as a safety net so stale saved state cannot strand the
     6→user in the wrong phase after out-of-band changes.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from typing import Iterable
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→
    16→[REDACTED]
    17→_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY = "lifecycle_phase"
    18→
    19→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    20→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW = "review"
    21→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW = "workflow"
    22→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE = "triage"
    23→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    24→VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES = frozenset(
    25→    {
    26→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    27→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    28→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    29→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    30→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    31→    }
    32→)
    33→
    34→
    35→def _refresh_state(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, object]:
    36→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    37→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    38→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    39→        refresh_state = {}
    40→        plan["refresh_state"] = refresh_state
    41→    return refresh_state
    42→
    43→
    44→def _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    45→    return not any(str(issue_id).startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
    46→
    47→
    48→def current_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel) -> str | None:
    49→    """Return the persisted lifecycle phase, falling back for legacy plans."""
    50→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    51→    if isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    52→        phase = refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY)
    53→        if isinstance(phase, str) and phase in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    54→            return phase
    55→    if postflight_scan_pending(plan):
    56→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
    57→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    58→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
    59→    return None
    60→
    61→
    62→def set_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel, phase: str) -> bool:
    63→    """Persist the current queue lifecycle phase."""
    64→    if phase not in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    65→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported lifecycle phase: {phase}")
    66→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
    67→    if refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY) == phase:
    68→        return False
    69→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = phase
    70→    return True
    71→
    72→
    73→def sync_lifecycle_phase(
    74→    plan: PlanModel,
    75→    *,
    76→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
    77→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
    78→    has_postflight_review: bool,
    79→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
    80→    has_triage: bool,
    81→    has_deferred: bool,
    82→) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    83→    """Resolve and persist the current lifecycle phase from queue-state facts."""
    84→    phase = resolve_lifecycle_phase(
    85→        plan,
    86→        has_initial_reviews=has_initial_reviews,
    87→        has_objective_backlog=has_objective_backlog,
    88→        has_postflight_review=has_postflight_review,
    89→        has_postflight_workflow=has_postflight_workflow,
    90→        has_triage=has_triage,
    91→        has_deferred=has_deferred,
    92→    )
    93→    return phase, set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
    94→
    95→
    96→def resolve_lifecycle_phase(
    97→    plan: PlanModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
   100→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
   101→    has_postflight_review: bool,
   102→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
   103→    has_triage: bool,
   104→    has_deferred: bool,
   105→) -> str:
   106→    """Resolve the lifecycle phase from explicit queue-state facts."""
   107→    if has_initial_reviews:
   108→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   109→    if has_objective_backlog:
   110→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   111→    if has_deferred or postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   112→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   113→    if has_postflight_review:
   114→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   115→    if has_postflight_workflow:
   116→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW
   117→    if has_triage:
   118→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE
   119→    persisted = current_lifecycle_phase(plan)
   120→    if persisted is not None:
   121→        return persisted
   122→    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   123→
   124→
   125→def postflight_scan_pending(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
   126→    """Return True when the current empty-queue boundary still needs a scan."""
   127→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
   128→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
   129→        return True
   130→    return not isinstance(refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY), int)
   131→
   132→
   133→def mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   134→    plan: PlanModel,
   135→    *,
   136→    scan_count: int | None,
   137→) -> bool:
   138→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current refresh cycle."""
   139→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   140→    try:
   141→        normalized_scan_count = int(scan_count or 0)
   142→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   143→        normalized_scan_count = 0
   144→    if refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY) == normalized_scan_count:
   145→        return False
   146→    refresh_state[_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY] = normalized_scan_count
   147→    return True
   148→
   149→
   150→def clear_postflight_scan_completion(
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    *,
   153→    issue_ids: Iterable[str] | None = None,
   154→) -> bool:
   155→    """Require a fresh scan after queue-changing work on real issues."""
   156→    if issue_ids is not None and not any(
   157→        _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids
   158→    ):
   159→        return False
   160→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   161→    if _POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY not in refresh_state:
   162→        return False
   163→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   164→    refresh_state.pop(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY, None)
   165→    return True
   166→
   167→
   168→__all__ = [
   169→    "clear_postflight_scan_completion",
   170→    "current_lifecycle_phase",
   171→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE",
   172→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW",
   173→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN",
   174→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE",
   175→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW",
   176→    "mark_postflight_scan_completed",
   177→    "postflight_scan_pending",
   178→    "resolve_lifecycle_phase",
   179→    "set_lifecycle_phase",
   180→    "sync_lifecycle_phase",
   181→    "VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES",
   182→]

1→"""Canonical stale/unscored subjective policy helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import all_subjective_entries
    11→
    12→_REVIEW_DETECTORS = ("review", "concerns")
    13→
    14→
    15→def open_review_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    16→    """Return IDs of open review/concerns issues from state."""
    17→    return {
    18→        fid
    19→        for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    20→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
    21→    }
    22→
    23→
    24→def _subjective_entry_id(
    25→    dimension_key: object,
    26→    *,
    27→    subjective_prefix: str,
    28→) -> str:
    29→    return f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(str(dimension_key))}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def _subjective_entries(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
    33→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    34→    if not dim_scores:
    35→        return []
    36→    return list(all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores))
    37→
    38→
    39→def _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
    40→    entries: list[dict],
    41→    *,
    42→    subjective_prefix: str,
    43→    predicate,
    44→) -> set[str]:
    45→    collected: set[str] = set()
    46→    for entry in entries:
    47→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    48→        if not dim_key or not predicate(entry):
    49→            continue
    50→        collected.add(_subjective_entry_id(dim_key, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix))
    51→    return collected
    52→
    53→
    54→def current_stale_ids(
    55→    state: StateModel,
    56→    *,
    57→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    58→) -> set[str]:
    59→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    60→    return _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
    61→        _subjective_entries(state),
    62→        subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
    63→        predicate=lambda entry: bool(entry.get("stale")),
    64→    )
    65→
    66→
    67→def _unscored_ids_from_assessments(
    68→    assessments: dict,
    69→    *,
    70→    subjective_prefix: str,
    71→) -> set[str]:
    72→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    73→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    74→        if not isinstance(payload, dict) or not payload.get("placeholder") or not dim_key:
    75→            continue
    76→        unscored.add(_subjective_entry_id(dim_key, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix))
    77→    return unscored
    78→
    79→
    80→def _unscored_ids_from_dimension_scores(
    81→    entries: list[dict],
    82→    *,
    83→    subjective_prefix: str,
    84→) -> set[str]:
    85→    return _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
    86→        entries,
    87→        subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
    88→        predicate=lambda entry: bool(entry.get("placeholder")),
    89→    )
    90→
    91→
    92→def current_unscored_ids(
    93→    state: StateModel,
    94→    *,
    95→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    96→) -> set[str]:
    97→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored."""
    98→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    99→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
   100→        return _unscored_ids_from_assessments(
   101→            assessments,
   102→            subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
   103→        )
   104→    return _unscored_ids_from_dimension_scores(
   105→        _subjective_entries(state),
   106→        subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
   107→    )
   108→
   109→
   110→def current_under_target_ids(
   111→    state: StateModel,
   112→    *,
   113→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   114→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
   115→) -> set[str]:
   116→    """Return under-target subjective IDs that are neither stale nor unscored."""
   117→    entries = _subjective_entries(state)
   118→    if not entries:
   119→        return set()
   120→
   121→    stale_ids = current_stale_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
   122→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
   123→
   124→    return {
   125→        item_id
   126→        for item_id in _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
   127→            entries,
   128→            subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
   129→            predicate=lambda entry: (
   130→                not entry.get("placeholder")
   131→                and not entry.get("stale")
   132→                and float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))) < target_strict
   133→            ),
   134→        )
   135→        if item_id not in stale_ids and item_id not in unscored_ids
   136→    }
   137→
   138→
   139→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   140→    """Hash open review/concerns issue IDs to detect triage-relevant changes."""
   141→    review_ids = sorted(open_review_ids(state))
   142→    if not review_ids:
   143→        return ""
   144→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   145→
   146→
   147→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   148→    """Return open review/concerns IDs that appeared since the last triage."""
   149→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   150→    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   151→    active = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
   152→    known = triaged | active
   153→    return open_review_ids(state) - known if known else set()
   154→
   155→
   156→def is_triage_stale(
   157→    plan: dict,
   158→    state: StateModel,
   159→) -> bool:
   160→    """Return True when genuinely new review issues appeared since last triage.
   161→
   162→    In-progress triage (confirmed stages + stage IDs in queue) is NOT
   163→    considered stale — the lifecycle filter in the work queue already
   164→    forces triage stages to the front.
   165→    """
   166→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   167→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   168→    active_ids = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
   169→    known = triaged_ids | active_ids
   170→    return bool(open_review_ids(state) - known)
   171→
   172→
   173→__all__ = [
   174→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   175→    "current_stale_ids",
   176→    "current_under_target_ids",
   177→    "current_unscored_ids",
   178→    "is_triage_stale",
   179→    "open_review_ids",
   180→    "review_issue_snapshot_hash",
   181→]
   182→
