    44→def _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    45→    """Clear all cycle state when --force-rescan is used.
    46→
    47→    Force-rescan means "start over" — the old cycle's triage stages,
    48→    workflow items, subjective dimensions, plan-start scores, and triage
    49→    metadata are all stale and must be removed.
    50→    """
    51→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    52→    synthetic = [item for item in order if any(item.startswith(p) for p in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)]
    53→    if not synthetic and not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    54→        return False
    55→    for item in synthetic:
    56→        order.remove(item)
    57→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    58→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
    59→    plan.pop("scan_count_at_plan_start", None)
    60→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    61→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    62→        meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
    63→    count = len(synthetic)
    64→    if count:
    65→        print(
    66→            colorize(
    67→                f"  Plan: force-rescan — removed {count} synthetic item(s) "
    68→                f"and reset cycle state.",
    69→                "yellow",
    70→            )
    71→        )
    72→    return True
    73→
    74→
    75→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    76→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    77→    return bool(
    78→        plan.get("queue_order")
    79→        or plan.get("overrides")
    80→        or plan.get("clusters")
    81→        or plan.get("skipped")
    82→    )
    83→
    84→
    85→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    86→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    87→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    88→        return False
    89→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    90→    if recon.resurfaced:
    91→        print(
    92→            colorize(
    93→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    94→                "cyan",
    95→            )
    96→        )
    97→    return bool(recon.changes)
    98→
    99→
   100→def _sync_subjective_dimensions_display(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
   101→    """Sync all subjective dimensions (unscored + stale + under-target) in plan queue."""
   102→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
   103→    if sync.resurfaced:
   104→        print(
   105→            colorize(
   106→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.resurfaced)} skipped subjective dimension(s) resurfaced — never reviewed.",
   107→                "yellow",
   108→            )
   109→        )
   110→    if sync.pruned:
   111→        print(
   112→            colorize(
   113→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
   114→                "cyan",
   115→            )
   116→        )
   117→    if sync.injected:
   118→        print(
   119→            colorize(
   120→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} subjective dimension(s) queued for review.",
   121→                "cyan",
   122→            )
   123→        )
   124→    return bool(sync.changes)

   126→
   127→def _sync_auto_clusters(
   128→    plan: dict[str, object],
   129→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   130→    *,
   131→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   132→    policy=None,
   133→) -> bool:
   134→    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""
   135→    return bool(auto_cluster_issues(
   136→        plan, state,
   137→        target_strict=target_strict,
   138→        policy=policy,
   139→    ))
   140→
   141→
   142→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   143→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   144→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   145→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   146→        return False
   147→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   148→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   149→        return False
   150→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   151→    if scores.strict is None:
   152→        return False
   153→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   154→        "strict": scores.strict,
   155→        "overall": scores.overall,
   156→        "objective": scores.objective,
   157→        "verified": scores.verified,
   158→    }
   159→    # New cycle — clear the communicate-score sentinel so it can fire again.
   160→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   161→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   162→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   163→    return True
   164→
   165→
   166→def _has_objective_cycle(
   167→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   168→    plan: dict[str, object],
   169→) -> bool | None:
   170→    """Return True when objective queue work exists and a cycle baseline should freeze."""
   171→    try:
   172→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   173→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   174→        )
   175→
   176→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   177→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   178→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "compute queue breakdown for plan-start seeding", exc)
   179→        return None
   180→    return breakdown.objective_actionable > 0
   181→
   182→
   183→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   184→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   185→) -> bool:
   186→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   187→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   188→        return False
   189→    # Don't clear while communicate-score is pending — the rebaseline just
   190→    # set plan_start_scores and the user hasn't seen the update yet.
   191→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   192→        return False
   193→
   194→    try:
   195→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   196→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   197→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   198→            score_display_mode,
   199→        )
   200→
   201→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   202→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   203→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   204→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   205→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   206→        return False
   207→    if not queue_empty:
   208→        return False
   209→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   210→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   211→    # Clear the cycle sentinel so communicate-score can be injected
   212→    # in the next cycle.
   213→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   214→    return True
   215→
   216→
   217→def _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(
   218→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   219→    plan: dict[str, object],
   220→) -> bool:
   221→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current empty-queue boundary."""
   222→    if build_deferred_disposition_item(plan) is not None:
   223→        return False
   224→    objective_cycle = _has_objective_cycle(state, plan)
   225→    if objective_cycle is not False:
   226→        return False
   227→    return mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   228→        plan,
   229→        scan_count=int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0),
   230→    )
   231→

   233→def _subjective_policy_context(
   234→    runtime: Any,
   235→    plan: dict[str, object],
   236→) -> tuple[float, object, bool]:
   237→    from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
   238→
   239→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   240→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   241→        runtime.state,
   242→        target_strict=target_strict,
   243→        plan=plan,
   244→    )
   245→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   246→    return target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed
   247→
   248→
   249→def _sync_subjective_and_log(
   250→    plan: dict[str, object],
   251→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   252→    *,
   253→    policy,
   254→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   255→) -> bool:
   256→    changed = _sync_subjective_dimensions_display(
   257→        plan,
   258→        state,
   259→        lambda p, s: sync_subjective_dimensions(
   260→            p,
   261→            s,
   262→            policy=policy,
   263→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   264→        ),
   265→    )
   266→    if changed:
   267→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_subjective", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   268→    return changed
   269→
   270→
   271→def _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   272→    plan: dict[str, object],
   273→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   274→    *,
   275→    target_strict: float,
   276→    policy,
   277→) -> bool:
   278→    changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   279→        plan,
   280→        state,
   281→        target_strict=target_strict,
   282→        policy=policy,
   283→    )
   284→    if changed:

   289→def _sync_triage_and_log(
   290→    plan: dict[str, object],

   292→    *,
   293→    policy=None,
   294→) -> bool:
   295→    triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   296→    if triage_sync.deferred:
   297→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   298→        if meta.get("triage_recommended"):
   299→            print(
   300→                colorize(
   301→                    "  Plan: review issues changed — triage recommended after current work.",
   302→                    "dim",
   303→                )
   304→            )
   305→        return False
   306→    if not triage_sync.changes:
   307→        return False
   308→    if triage_sync.injected:
   309→        print(
   310→            colorize(
   311→                "  Plan: planning mode needed — review issues changed since last triage.",
   312→                "cyan",
   313→            )
   314→        )
   315→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})

   318→
   319→def _sync_communicate_score_and_log(
   320→    plan: dict[str, object],
   321→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   322→    *,
   323→    policy,
   324→) -> bool:
   325→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   326→    current_scores = ScoreSnapshot(
   327→        strict=snapshot.strict,
   328→        overall=snapshot.overall,
   329→        objective=snapshot.objective,
   330→        verified=snapshot.verified,
   331→    )
   332→    communicate_sync = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   333→        plan, state, policy=policy, current_scores=current_scores,
   334→    )
   335→    if not communicate_sync.changes:
   336→        return False
   337→    append_log_entry(
   338→        plan,
   339→        "sync_communicate_score",
   340→        actor="system",
   341→        detail={"injected": True},
   342→    )

   344→
   345→
   346→def _sync_create_plan_and_log(
   347→    plan: dict[str, object],
   348→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   349→    *,
   350→    policy,
   351→) -> bool:
   352→    create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   353→    if not create_plan_sync.changes:
   354→        return False
   355→    if create_plan_sync.injected:
   356→        print(

   359→                "cyan",
   360→            )
   361→        )
   362→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})

   365→
   366→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(
   367→    plan: dict[str, object],
   368→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   369→) -> bool:
   370→    seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state)
   371→    if seeded:
   372→        append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   373→        return True
   374→    # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   375→    # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   376→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   377→    if cleared:
   378→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   379→    return cleared
   380→
   381→
   382→def _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(
   383→    plan: dict[str, object],
   384→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   385→) -> bool:
   386→    changed = _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(state, plan)
   387→    if changed:
   388→        append_log_entry(
   389→            plan,
   390→            "complete_postflight_scan",
   391→            actor="system",
   392→            detail={"scan_count": int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)},
   393→        )

   396→
   397→def _has_postflight_review_work(
   398→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   399→    *,
   400→    policy,
   401→) -> bool:
   402→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   403→    has_review_like_issue = any(
   404→        isinstance(issue, dict)
   405→        and issue.get("status") == "open"
   406→        and issue.get("detector") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
   407→        for issue in issues.values()
   408→    )
   409→    if has_review_like_issue:
   410→        return True
   411→    return bool(policy.stale_ids or policy.under_target_ids)
   412→
   413→
   414→def _has_postflight_workflow_items(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
   415→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   416→    return any(
   417→        item_id in order
   418→        for item_id in (
   419→            "workflow::import-scores",
   420→            "workflow::communicate-score",
   421→            "workflow::score-checkpoint",
   422→            "workflow::create-plan",
   423→        )
   424→    )
   425→
   426→
   427→def _has_triage_items(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
   428→    return any(
   429→        isinstance(item_id, str) and item_id.startswith("triage::")
   430→        for item_id in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   431→    )

   434→def _sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log(
   435→    plan: dict[str, object],

   437→) -> bool:
   438→    phase = coarse_phase_name(queue_context(state, plan=plan).snapshot.phase)
   439→    changed = set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
   440→    if changed:
   441→        append_log_entry(
   442→            plan,
   443→            "sync_lifecycle_phase",
   444→            actor="system",
   445→            detail={"phase": phase},
   446→        )
   447→    return changed

   449→
   450→def _sync_post_scan_without_policy(
   451→    *,
   452→    plan: dict[str, object],
   453→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   454→) -> bool:
   455→    """Run post-scan sync steps that do not require subjective policy context."""
   456→    return bool(_apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, state, reconcile_plan_after_scan))
   457→
   458→
   459→def _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   460→    """Return True when a plan cycle is active and queue items remain.
   461→
   462→    Extends ``is_mid_cycle`` (which checks ``plan_start_scores``) with an
   463→    additional queue-items guard — even if a cycle is nominally active, we
   464→    only skip destructive operations when work actually remains.
   465→
   466→    Mid-cycle scans (via --force-rescan or PHASE_TRANSITION gate) must NOT
   467→    regenerate clusters or inject triage stages — doing so wipes triage
   468→    state and reorders the queue, undoing prioritisation work.
   469→    """
   470→    if not is_mid_cycle(plan):
   471→        return False
   472→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   473→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   474→    return any(item not in skipped for item in order)
   475→
   476→
   477→def _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   478→    *,
   479→    plan: dict[str, object],
   480→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   481→    target_strict: float,
   482→    policy,
   483→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   484→    force_rescan: bool = False,
   485→) -> bool:
   486→    """Run post-scan sync steps that require policy/cycle context.
   487→
   488→    When running mid-cycle (plan_start_scores set, queue non-empty) or
   489→    via --force-rescan, skip auto-clustering and triage injection.  These
   490→    steps regenerate queue structure and issue IDs, which wipes triage
   491→    state and reorders the queue.  They only run at cycle boundaries
   492→    (pre-flight / post-flight).
   493→    """
   494→    dirty = False
   495→    mid_cycle = _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state) or force_rescan
   496→
   497→    if _sync_subjective_and_log(
   498→        plan,
   499→        state,
   500→        policy=policy,
   501→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   502→    ):
   503→        dirty = True
   504→    if not mid_cycle:
   505→        if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   506→            plan,
   507→            state,
   508→            target_strict=target_strict,
   509→            policy=policy,
   510→        ):
   511→            dirty = True
   512→    else:
   513→        print(
   514→            colorize(
   515→                "  Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration to "
   516→                "preserve queue state.",
   517→                "dim",
   518→            )
   519→        )
   520→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   521→        dirty = True
   522→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   523→        dirty = True
   524→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   525→        dirty = True
   526→    if not force_rescan:
   527→        if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, state):
   528→            dirty = True
   529→        if _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, state):
   530→            dirty = True
   531→    if _sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log(plan, state):
   532→        dirty = True
   533→    return dirty
   534→
   535→
   536→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   537→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   538→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   539→    try:
   540→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   541→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   542→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   543→        return
   544→
   545→    force_rescan = getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False)
   546→
   547→    # Force-rescan: clear all cycle state first.  The user explicitly chose
   548→    # to start over, so triage stages, workflow items, subjective dimensions,
   549→    # and plan-start scores from the old cycle are all stale.
   550→    dirty = _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan) if force_rescan else False
   551→
   552→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan=plan, state=runtime.state) or dirty
   553→
   554→    # Policy must be computed after reconciliation, which mutates plan
   555→    # (supersede/prune resolved issues) before policy reads it.
   556→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   557→        runtime,
   558→        plan,
   559→    )
   560→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   561→        plan=plan,
   562→        state=runtime.state,
   563→        target_strict=target_strict,
   564→        policy=policy,
   565→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   566→        force_rescan=getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False),
   567→    ) or dirty
   568→
   569→    if dirty:
   570→        try:
   571→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   572→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   573→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   574→
```

    71→def _has_postflight_review_work(state: dict, *, policy) -> bool:
    72→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
    73→    if any(
    74→        isinstance(issue, dict)
    75→        and issue.get("status") == "open"
    76→        and issue.get("detector") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
    77→        for issue in issues.values()
    78→    ):
    79→        return True
    80→    return bool(policy.stale_ids or policy.under_target_ids)
    81→
    82→
    83→def _has_deferred_disposition_work(plan: dict) -> bool:
    84→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    85→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
    86→        return False
    87→    return any(
    88→        isinstance(entry, dict) and str(entry.get("kind", "temporary")) == "temporary"
    89→        for entry in skipped.values()
    90→    )
    91→
    92→
    93→def _sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import(plan: dict, state: dict, *, policy) -> bool:
    94→    return sync_lifecycle_phase(
    95→        plan,
    96→        has_initial_reviews=bool(policy.unscored_ids),
    97→        has_objective_backlog=bool(policy.has_objective_backlog),
    98→        has_postflight_review=_has_postflight_review_work(state, policy=policy),
    99→        has_postflight_workflow=any(
   100→            item_id in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   101→            for item_id in (
   102→                "workflow::import-scores",
   103→                "workflow::communicate-score",
   104→                "workflow::score-checkpoint",
   105→                "workflow::create-plan",
   106→            )
   107→        ),
   108→        has_triage=any(
   109→            isinstance(item_id, str) and item_id.startswith("triage::")
   110→            for item_id in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   111→        ),
   112→        has_deferred=_has_deferred_disposition_work(plan),
   113→    )[1]

   199→def _build_import_sync_inputs(

   218→def _record_workflow_change(
   219→    mutations: _PlanImportMutations,
   220→    result,
   221→    *,
   222→    workflow_id: str,
   223→    injected: bool = True,
   224→) -> None:

   232→def _sync_review_delta(
   233→    mutations: _PlanImportMutations,
   234→    plan: dict,
   235→    state: dict,
   236→    *,
   237→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility,
   238→    sync_inputs: _ImportSyncInputs,
   239→) -> None:

   242→    mutations.import_result = sync_plan_after_review_import(
   243→        plan,

   251→def _sync_subjective_queue_after_import(
   252→    mutations: _PlanImportMutations,
   253→    plan: dict,
   254→    state: dict,
   255→    *,
   256→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility,
   257→    target_strict: float,

   261→        return
   262→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   263→    mutations.stale_sync_result = sync_subjective_dimensions(
   264→        plan,
   265→        state,
   266→        policy=policy,
   267→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   268→    )
   269→    if mutations.stale_sync_result.changes:
   270→        mutations.dirty = True
   271→
   272→    mutations.auto_cluster_changes = int(auto_cluster_issues(
   273→        plan,
   274→        state,
   275→        target_strict=target_strict,
   276→        policy=policy,
   277→    ))
   278→    if mutations.auto_cluster_changes:
   279→        mutations.dirty = True

   282→def _append_workflow_log_entries(
   283→    plan: dict,
   284→    *,
   285→    communicate_result,
   286→    import_scores_result,
   287→    create_plan_result,

   296→    if import_scores_result.changes:

   318→def _append_review_import_sync_log(
   319→    plan: dict,
   320→    diff: dict,
   321→    mutations: _PlanImportMutations,
   322→    *,
   323→    covered_ids: tuple[str, ...],
   324→) -> None:
   325→    if not (
   326→        mutations.import_result is not None
   327→        or mutations.workflow_injected_ids
   328→        or covered_ids
   329→    ):
   330→        return
   331→    import_result = mutations.import_result
   332→    stale_sync_result = mutations.stale_sync_result
   333→    append_log_entry(
   334→        plan,
   335→        "review_import_sync",
   336→        actor="system",
   337→        detail={
   338→            "trigger": "review_import",
   339→            "new_ids": sorted(import_result.new_ids) if import_result is not None else [],
   340→            "added_to_queue": (
   341→                import_result.added_to_queue if import_result is not None else []
   342→            ),
   343→            "workflow_injected_ids": mutations.workflow_injected_ids,
   344→            "triage_injected": (
   345→                import_result.triage_injected if import_result is not None else False
   346→            ),
   347→            "triage_injected_ids": (
   348→                import_result.triage_injected_ids if import_result is not None else []
   349→            ),
   350→            "triage_deferred": (
   351→                import_result.triage_deferred if import_result is not None else False
   352→            ),
   353→            "diff_new": diff.get("new", 0),
   354→            "diff_reopened": diff.get("reopened", 0),
   355→            "diff_auto_resolved": diff.get("auto_resolved", 0),
   356→            "stale_pruned_from_queue": (
   357→                import_result.stale_pruned_from_queue if import_result is not None else []
   358→            ),
   359→            "covered_subjective": list(covered_ids),
   360→            "stale_sync_injected": (
   361→                sorted(stale_sync_result.injected)
   362→                if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   363→            ),
   364→            "stale_sync_pruned": (
   365→                sorted(stale_sync_result.pruned)
   366→                if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   367→            ),
   368→            "auto_cluster_changes": mutations.auto_cluster_changes,
   369→        },
   370→    )
   371→
   372→
   373→def sync_plan_after_import(
   374→    state: dict,
   375→    diff: dict,
   376→    assessment_mode: str,
   377→    *,
   378→    request: PlanImportSyncRequest | None = None,
   379→) -> None:
   380→    """Apply issue/workflow syncs after import in one load/save cycle."""
   381→    try:
   382→        state_file = request.state_file if request is not None else None
   383→        config = request.config if request is not None else None
   384→        import_file = request.import_file if request is not None else None
   385→        import_payload = request.import_payload if request is not None else None
   386→
   387→        plan_path = None
   388→        target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   389→        if state_file is not None:
   390→            plan_path = plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
   391→        if not has_living_plan(plan_path):
   392→            return
   393→
   394→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   395→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   396→            state,
   397→            target_strict=target_strict,
   398→            plan=plan,
   399→        )
   400→        snapshot = score_snapshot(state)
   401→        current_scores = ScoreSnapshot(
   402→            strict=snapshot.strict,
   403→            overall=snapshot.overall,
   404→            objective=snapshot.objective,
   405→            verified=snapshot.verified,
   406→        )
   407→        trusted_score_import = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
   408→        communicate_result = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   409→            plan,
   410→            state,
   411→            policy=policy,
   412→            scores_just_imported=trusted_score_import,
   413→            current_scores=current_scores,
   414→        )
   415→        import_scores_result = sync_import_scores_needed(
   416→            plan,
   417→            state,
   418→            assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
   419→            import_file=import_file,
   420→            import_payload=import_payload,
   421→        )
   422→        create_plan_result = sync_create_plan_needed(
   423→            plan,
   424→            state,
   425→            policy=policy,
   426→        )
   427→
   428→        sync_inputs = _build_import_sync_inputs(diff, import_payload)
   429→        mutations = _PlanImportMutations()
   430→        _record_workflow_change(
   431→            mutations,
   432→            communicate_result,
   433→            workflow_id="workflow::communicate-score",
   434→        )
   435→        _record_workflow_change(
   436→            mutations,
   437→            import_scores_result,
   438→            workflow_id="workflow::import-scores",
   439→            injected=bool(getattr(import_scores_result, "injected", ())),
   440→        )
   441→        _record_workflow_change(
   442→            mutations,
   443→            create_plan_result,
   444→            workflow_id="workflow::create-plan",
   445→        )
   446→        _sync_review_delta(
   447→            mutations,
   448→            plan,
   449→            state,
   450→            policy=policy,
   451→            sync_inputs=sync_inputs,
   452→        )
   453→        _sync_subjective_queue_after_import(
   454→            mutations,
   455→            plan,
   456→            state,
   457→            policy=policy,
   458→            target_strict=target_strict,
   459→            sync_inputs=sync_inputs,
   460→        )
   461→
   462→        if mutations.dirty:
   463→            if _sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import(plan, state, policy=policy):
   464→                mutations.dirty = True
   465→            _append_workflow_log_entries(
   466→                plan,
   467→                communicate_result=communicate_result,
   468→                import_scores_result=import_scores_result,
   469→                create_plan_result=create_plan_result,
   470→            )
   471→            _append_review_import_sync_log(
   472→                plan,
   473→                diff,
   474→                mutations,
   475→                covered_ids=sync_inputs.covered_ids,
   476→            )
   477→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   478→
   479→        if mutations.import_result is not None:
   480→            _print_review_import_sync(
   481→                state,
   482→                mutations.import_result,
   483→                workflow_injected=bool(mutations.workflow_injected_ids),
   484→            )
   485→        _print_workflow_injected_message(mutations.workflow_injected_ids)
   486→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   487→        print(
   488→            colorize(
   489→                f"  Note: skipped plan sync after review import ({exc}).",
   490→                "dim",
   491→            )
   492→        )
   493→
   494→
   495→__all__ = ["PlanImportSyncRequest", "sync_plan_after_import"]
   496→
```

## Summary

I've read all the requested files and traced the two "queue empty, start PROCESS" patterns. Here's what I found:

---

## **TWO QUEUE-EMPTY TRIGGER PATTERNS**

### **PATTERN 1: PRE-FLIGHT PATH (Post-Scan Phase Transition)**
**Location:** `desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py` — function `reconcile_plan_post_scan()` and its helpers

**Gate/Trigger Flow:**

1. **Boundary condition:** After a scan completes, code runs `_mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready()` (line 217).
2. **Guard checks:**
   - No deferred disposition items pending: `build_deferred_disposition_item(plan) is not None` → return False
   - Has objective work: `_has_objective_cycle(state, plan)` must return exactly `False` (not `None`)
3. **Trigger fires:** When both guards pass, `mark_postflight_scan_completed()` records scan count in `plan["refresh_state"]["postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count"]`
4. **What gets started:**
   - **`workflow::run-scan`** — Built by `build_run_scan_item()` in synthetic_workflow.py (line 246)
     - Trigger: `postflight_scan_pending(plan)` returns True (line 125 in refresh_lifecycle.py)
     - This item enters queue when the previous scan's postflight boundary is complete
   - **`workflow::score-checkpoint`**, **`workflow::import-scores`**, **`workflow::communicate-score`**, **`workflow::create-plan`** — all synthetic workflow items get re-evaluated after this boundary

**Key logic:** The code calls `_sync_post_scan_with_policy()` (line 477) which:
- Seeds `plan_start_scores` at cycle start via `_seed_plan_start_scores()` (line 142)
- Clears `plan_start_scores` when queue fully drains via `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty()` (line 183)
- Seeds subjective dimensions via `sync_subjective_dimensions()`
- Auto-clusters via `auto_cluster_issues()`
- Then calls `sync_communicate_score_needed()`, `sync_create_plan_needed()`, `sync_triage_needed()` which all return `QueueSyncResult` objects with `.injected` flags

---

### **PATTERN 2: POST-FLIGHT PATH (Review Import / Workflow Sync)**
**Location:** `desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/plan_sync.py` — function `sync_plan_after_import()` (line 373)

**Gate/Trigger Flow:**

1. **Boundary condition:** After a `desloppify review --import` completes, plan sync runs.
2. **Decision point:** Check if objective queue is empty via `_has_postflight_review_work()` (line 71):
   - Has open review/concerns/subjective_review issues? → workflow item gates are suppressed
   - Has stale or under-target dimensions (via `policy.stale_ids` or `policy.under_target_ids`)? → workflow item gates are suppressed
3. **If queue IS empty:**
   - `sync_communicate_score_needed()` checks `scores_just_imported=True` flag (line 408)
   - `sync_import_scores_needed()` checks import mode (line 415)
   - `sync_create_plan_needed()` checks if triage is needed (line 422)
4. **Trigger fires:** These workflow gates return `QueueSyncResult` with `.injected` flags
5. **What gets started:**
   - **`workflow::communicate-score`** — injected when scores just imported
   - **`workflow::import-scores`** — injected when import mode is non-trusted
   - **`workflow::create-plan`** — injected when triage stages are pending or review cycle complete

**Phase resolution:** `sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import()` (line 93) calls the central resolver:
```python
sync_lifecycle_phase(
    plan,
    has_initial_reviews=bool(policy.unscored_ids),
    has_objective_backlog=bool(policy.has_objective_backlog),
    has_postflight_review=_has_postflight_review_work(state, policy=policy),
    has_postflight_workflow=any(item_id in plan.get("queue_order", []) ...),
    has_triage=any(isinstance(item_id, str) and item_id.startswith("triage::") ...),
    has_deferred=_has_deferred_disposition_work(plan),
)
```

---

## **SHARED LOGIC & CENTRAL DISPATCHER**

Both paths funnel through the same **phase resolution** in `desloppify/engine/_plan/refresh_lifecycle.py`:

**`resolve_lifecycle_phase()` (line 96)** — the canonical "what phase are we in?" decider:
```python
if has_initial_reviews:
    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
if has_objective_backlog:
    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
if has_deferred or postflight_scan_pending(plan):
    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
if has_postflight_review:
    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
if has_postflight_workflow:
    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW
if has_triage:
    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE
```

**Queue snapshot dispatches to execution items** in `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py`:

**`_phase_for_snapshot()` (line 164)** and **`_execution_items_for_phase()` (line 189)** partition the queue:
- When objective items exist → `PHASE_EXECUTE`
- When scan items exist → `PHASE_SCAN` (with `build_run_scan_item()` as the executable)
- When postflight review items exist → `PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT`
- When postflight workflow items exist → `PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT` (executes `build_communicate_score_item()`, `build_create_plan_item()`, etc.)
- When triage items exist → `PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT`

---

## **KEY DIFFERENCES**

| Aspect | Pre-Flight | Post-Flight |
|--------|-----------|------------|
| **Trigger** | `_mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready()` after scan | `sync_plan_after_import()` after review import |
| **Guards** | No deferred items + no objective backlog | Implicit in gate functions (policy checks) |
| **Items injected** | Subjective dimensions, auto-clusters, then workflow items | Workflow items only (communicate, import-scores, create-plan) |
| **Phase transition** | Via `set_lifecycle_phase()` after item injection | Via `sync_lifecycle_phase()` with explicit boolean inputs |
| **Duplication** | None — both call the same workflow gate functions | Minimal — `_has_postflight_review_work()` duplicated in both files (lines 71 and 397) |

---

    50→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    51→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    52→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    53→    target = cleaned.lower()
    54→
    55→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    56→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    57→            return str(dim_key)
    58→    return slugify(cleaned)
    59→
    60→
    61→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    63→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    64→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    65→    return {
    66→        cleaned.lower(),
    67→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    68→        slugify(cleaned),
    69→        canonical.lower(),
    70→        slugify(canonical),
    71→    }

    90→        [REDACTED](name)

    93→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()

    95→                continue

    97→            aliases.add(slugify(key))

   100→        for alias in aliases:

   109→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   110→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   111→
   112→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   113→    """
   114→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   115→    order_set = set(order)
   116→    present_ids = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   117→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   118→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   119→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   120→    present_names = {
   121→        name
   122→        for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS
   123→        if sid in present_ids
   124→    }
   125→    present_names.update(recorded_unconfirmed)
   126→    if not present_names:
   127→        return []
   128→
   129→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   130→    open_review_count = sum(
   131→        1 for f in issues.values()
   132→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   133→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   134→    )
   135→
   136→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   137→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   138→    for name, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
   139→        if name not in present_names:
   140→            continue
   141→        if name in confirmed:
   142→            continue
   143→
   144→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   145→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   146→        blocked_by = sorted(
   147→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps if dep in present_names and dep not in confirmed
   148→        )
   149→
   150→        only_stages = None if name == "commit" else name
   151→        cmd = triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=only_stages)
   152→
   153→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   154→            "id": sid,
   155→            "tier": 1,
   156→            "confidence": "high",
   157→            "detector": "triage",
   158→            "file": ".",
   159→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   160→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   161→            "detail": {
   162→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   163→                "stage": name,
   164→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   165→                "runner_commands": [
   166→                    {"label": label, "command": command}
   167→                    for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
   168→                ],
   169→                "manual_fallback": triage_manual_stage_command(name),
   170→            },
   171→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   172→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   173→        }
   174→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   175→        items.append(item)
   176→    return items
   177→
   178→
   179→def build_subjective_items(
   180→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   181→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   182→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   183→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   184→    if not dim_scores:
   185→        return []
   186→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   187→
   188→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   189→    if not subjective_entries:
   190→        return []
   191→    unassessed_dims = {
   192→        str(name).strip()
   193→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   194→            dim_scores
   195→        )
   196→    }
   197→
   198→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   199→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   200→    for issue in issues.values():
   201→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   202→            continue
   203→        if issue.get("detector") == "review":
   204→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   205→            if dim_key:
   206→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   207→
   208→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   209→    def _prepare_command(
   210→        cli_keys: list[str],
   211→        *,
   212→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   213→    ) -> str:
   214→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   215→        if cli_keys:
   216→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   217→        if force_review_rerun:
   218→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   219→        return command
   220→
   221→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   222→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   223→        if not name:
   224→            continue
   225→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   226→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   227→            continue
   228→
   229→        [REDACTED](name)
   230→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   231→        cli_keys = [
   232→            str(key).strip().lower()
   233→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   234→            if str(key).strip()
   235→        ]
   236→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   237→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   238→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   239→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   240→            name in unassessed_dims
   241→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   242→        )
   243→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   244→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   245→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   246→        if open_review > 0:
   247→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   248→        else:
   249→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   250→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   251→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   252→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   253→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   254→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   255→            "file": ".",
   256→            "confidence": "medium",
   257→            "summary": summary,
   258→            "detail": {
   259→                "dimension_name": name,
   260→                "dimension": dim_key,
   261→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   262→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   263→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   264→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   265→            },
   266→            "status": "open",
   267→        }
   268→        item["kind"] = "subjective_dimension"
   269→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   270→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   271→        items.append(item)
   272→    return items
   273→
   274→

   278→    "build_deferred_disposition_item",

1→"""Shared constants and helpers for plan internals."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
     9→
    10→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    11→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    12→
    13→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    14→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    15→    "triage::observe",
    16→    "triage::reflect",
    17→    "triage::organize",
    18→    "triage::enrich",
    19→    "triage::sense-check",
    20→    "triage::commit",
    21→)
    22→TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS = tuple(
    23→    (stage_id.removeprefix("triage::"), stage_id) for stage_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    24→)
    25→TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER = {
    26→    stage_name: index for index, (stage_name, _stage_id) in enumerate(TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS)
    27→}
    28→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    29→_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES = {
    30→    stage_id.removeprefix("triage::") for stage_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    31→}
    32→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    33→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    34→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    35→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    36→WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID = "workflow::deferred-disposition"
    37→WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID = "workflow::run-scan"
    38→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    39→WORKFLOW_IDS = {
    40→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    41→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    42→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    43→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    44→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    45→    WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
    46→}
    47→WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER = (
    48→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    49→    WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
    50→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    51→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    52→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    53→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    54→)
    55→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    56→
    57→
    58→@dataclass
    59→class QueueSyncResult:
    60→    """Unified result for all queue sync operations."""
    61→
    62→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    63→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    64→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    65→    deferred: bool = False
    66→
    67→    @property
    68→    def changes(self) -> int:
    69→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned) + len(self.resurfaced)
    70→
    71→
    72→def _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    73→    """Extract the triage stages dict from meta or a raw stages dict."""
    74→    if not isinstance(meta_or_stages, dict):
    75→        return {}
    76→    if "triage_stages" in meta_or_stages:
    77→        raw = meta_or_stages.get("triage_stages")
    78→        return raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    79→    candidate_names = {str(name) for name in meta_or_stages.keys()}
    80→    if candidate_names and candidate_names.issubset(_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES):
    81→        return meta_or_stages
    82→    return {}
    83→
    84→
    85→def confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    86→    """Return triage stage names with an explicit ``confirmed_at`` marker."""
    87→    return {
    88→        str(name)
    89→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    90→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get("confirmed_at")
    91→    }
    92→
    93→
    94→def recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    95→    """Return recorded triage stage names that still need confirmation."""
    96→    return {
    97→        str(name)
    98→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    99→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload and not payload.get("confirmed_at")
   100→    }
   101→
   102→
   103→def normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(queue_order: list[str]) -> None:
   104→    """Keep workflow items ahead of triage, then preserve the rest as-is."""
   105→    seen: set[str] = set()
   106→    normalized: list[str] = []
   107→
   108→    for issue_id in WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER:
   109→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   110→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   111→            seen.add(issue_id)
   112→
   113→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   114→        if issue_id.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX) and issue_id not in seen:
   115→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   116→            seen.add(issue_id)
   117→
   118→    for issue_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   119→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   120→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   121→            seen.add(issue_id)
   122→
   123→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   124→        if issue_id in seen:
   125→            continue
   126→        normalized.append(issue_id)
   127→        seen.add(issue_id)
   128→
   129→    queue_order[:] = normalized
   130→
   131→
   132→__all__ = [
   133→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   134→    "QueueSyncResult",
   135→    "normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix",
   136→    "confirmed_triage_stage_names",
   137→    "recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names",
   138→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   139→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   140→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   141→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   142→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER",
   143→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   144→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS",
   145→    "WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID",
   146→    "WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID",
   147→    "WORKFLOW_IDS",
   148→    "WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID",
   149→    "WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER",

   151→    "WORKFLOW_PREFIX",

    74→    ]
    75→    if manual_fallback:
    76→        tools.append({"label": "Manual fallback", "command": manual_fallback})
    77→    tools.append({"label": "Dashboard", "command": "desloppify plan triage"})
    78→    return tools
    79→
    80→
    81→def _triage_progression_target(plan: dict) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    82→    """Return current triage stage target and whether it requires confirmation."""
    83→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    84→    triage_stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {}) or {}
    85→    for stage, _sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    86→        stage_payload = triage_stages.get(stage)
    87→        if isinstance(stage_payload, dict) and stage_payload and not stage_payload.get("confirmed_at"):
    88→            return stage, True
    89→
    90→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    91→    for stage, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    92→        if sid not in order:
    93→            continue
    94→        return stage, False
    95→
    96→    if "triage::commit" in order:
    97→        return "commit", False
    98→
    99→    return None, False
   100→
   101→
   102→def _create_plan_primary_command(plan: dict) -> str:
   103→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
   104→    if stage is None:
   105→        return triage_run_stages_command()
   106→    if needs_confirm:
   107→        attestation = _confirm_attestation_hint(stage)
   108→        return (
   109→            f'desloppify plan triage --confirm {stage} '
   110→            f'--attestation "{attestation}"'
   111→        )
   112→    if stage == "commit":
   113→        return triage_run_stages_command()
   114→    return triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=stage)
   115→
   116→
   117→def _create_plan_planning_tools(plan: dict) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
   118→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
   119→    if needs_confirm:
   120→        return []
   121→    if stage == "commit":
   122→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   123→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("commit"),
   124→        )
   125→    if stage:
   126→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   127→            only_stages=stage,
   128→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command(stage),
   129→        )
   130→    return _runner_planning_tools(
   131→        only_stages="observe",
   132→        manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   133→    )
   134→
   135→
   136→def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   137→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` if queued."""
   138→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   142→
   143→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   144→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   145→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   146→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
   147→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   148→
   149→    return _workflow_action_item(
   150→        item_id=WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
   151→        summary=f"Score checkpoint: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   152→        detail={
   153→            "strict": strict,
   154→            "plan_start_strict": plan_start,
   155→            "delta": delta,
   156→            "planning_tools": _runner_planning_tools(
   157→                only_stages="observe",
   158→                manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   159→            ),
   160→        },
   161→        primary_command=triage_run_stages_command(only_stages="observe"),
   162→    )
   163→
   164→
   165→def build_create_plan_item(plan: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   166→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::create-plan`` if queued."""
   167→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   168→        return None
   169→
   170→    return _workflow_action_item(
   171→        item_id=WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   172→        summary="Create prioritized plan from review results",
   173→        detail={
   174→            "planning_tools": _create_plan_planning_tools(plan),
   175→        },
   176→        primary_command=_create_plan_primary_command(plan),
   177→    )
   178→
   179→
   180→def build_import_scores_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   181→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::import-scores`` if queued."""
   182→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   183→        return None
   184→    meta = pending_import_scores_meta(plan, state)
   185→    import_file = (meta.import_file if meta is not None else "").strip() or "issues.json"
   186→    quoted_import_file = shlex.quote(import_file)
   187→    packet_sha = meta.packet_sha256 if meta is not None else ""
   188→    explanation = (
   189→        "Review issues were imported but assessment scores were skipped "
   190→        "(untrusted source). Re-import the same batch with attestation to update dimension scores."
   191→    )
   192→    if import_file != "issues.json":
   193→        explanation += f" Expected import file: `{import_file}`."
   194→    if packet_sha:
   195→        explanation += f" Expected packet sha256: `{packet_sha}`."
   196→
   197→    return _workflow_action_item(
   198→        item_id=WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
   199→        summary="Import assessment scores with attestation",
   200→        detail={
   201→            "explanation": explanation,
   202→            "expected_import_file": import_file,
   203→            "packet_sha256": packet_sha,
   204→        },
   205→        primary_command=(
   206→            f"desloppify review --import {quoted_import_file} --attested-external "
   207→            '--attest "I validated this review was completed without awareness '
   208→            'of overall score and is unbiased."'
   209→        ),
   210→    )
   211→
   212→
   213→def build_communicate_score_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   214→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::communicate-score`` if queued."""
   215→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   216→        return None
   217→
   218→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   219→
   220→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   221→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   222→
   223→    # Use previous_plan_start_scores (stashed at rebaseline) for the delta
   224→    # so the user sees old-baseline → current, not current → current.
   225→    prev_start = (plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   226→    if prev_start is None:
   227→        prev_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   228→    delta = round(strict - prev_start, 1) if prev_start is not None else None
   229→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   230→
   231→    return _workflow_action_item(
   232→        item_id=WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
   233→        summary=f"Communicate updated score to user: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   234→        detail={
   235→            "strict": strict,
   236→            "previous_plan_start_strict": prev_start,
   237→            "delta": delta,
   238→        },
   239→        primary_command=(
   240→            f'desloppify plan resolve "{WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID}" '
   241→            '--note "Score communicated" --confirm'
   242→        ),
   243→    )
   244→
   245→
   246→def build_run_scan_item(plan: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   247→    """Build a synthetic item for the first post-flight scan step."""
   248→    if not postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   249→        return None
   250→
   251→    item = _workflow_action_item(
   252→        item_id=WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
   253→        summary="Run post-flight scan to refresh queue and surface follow-up review work.",
   254→        detail={
   255→            "phase": "postflight_scan",
   256→        },
   257→        primary_command="desloppify scan",
   258→    )
   259→    item["execution_visibility"] = "always"
   260→    return item
   261→
   262→
   263→def _temporary_skipped_ids(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   264→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   265→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
   266→        return []
   267→    deferred: list[str] = []
   268→    for issue_id, entry in skipped.items():
   269→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   270→            continue
   271→        if str(entry.get("kind", "temporary")) == "temporary":
   272→            deferred.append(str(issue_id))
   273→    deferred.sort()
   274→    return deferred
   275→
   276→
   277→def _deferred_cluster_breakdown(
   278→    plan: dict,
   279→    deferred_ids: list[str],
   280→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   281→    deferred_set = set(deferred_ids)
   282→    if not deferred_set:
   283→        return 0, 0
   284→
   285→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   286→    if not isinstance(clusters, dict):
   287→        return 0, len(deferred_ids)
   288→
   289→    covered_by_cluster: set[str] = set()
   290→    cluster_count = 0
   291→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   292→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   293→            continue
   294→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   295→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   296→            continue
   297→        matched = {str(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids if str(issue_id) in deferred_set}
   298→        if not matched:
   299→            continue
   300→        cluster_count += 1
   301→        covered_by_cluster.update(matched)
   302→
   303→    individual_count = len(deferred_set - covered_by_cluster)
   304→    return cluster_count, individual_count
   305→
   306→
   307→def build_deferred_disposition_item(plan: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   308→    """Build a synthetic item prompting deferred backlog disposition."""
   309→    deferred_ids = _temporary_skipped_ids(plan)
   310→    if not deferred_ids:
   311→        return None
   312→
   313→    count = len(deferred_ids)
   314→    cluster_count, individual_count = _deferred_cluster_breakdown(plan, deferred_ids)
   315→    cluster_label = "cluster" if cluster_count == 1 else "clusters"
   316→    individual_label = "item" if individual_count == 1 else "items"
   317→    reactivate_cmd = 'desloppify plan unskip "*"'
   318→    subset_reactivate_cmd = "desloppify plan unskip <cluster-or-id>"
   319→    inspect_cmd = "desloppify plan queue --include-skipped"
   320→    inspect_item_cmd = "desloppify show <cluster-or-id>"
   321→    backlog_cmd = 'desloppify plan backlog "*"'
   322→    subset_backlog_cmd = "desloppify plan backlog <cluster-or-id>"
   323→    wontfix_cmd = (
   324→        'desloppify plan skip --permanent "*" '
   325→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   326→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently." '
   327→        "--confirm"
   328→    )
   329→    subset_wontfix_cmd = (
   330→        "desloppify plan skip --permanent <cluster-or-id> "
   331→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   332→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently."'
   333→    )
   334→
   335→    item = _workflow_action_item(
   336→        item_id=WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
   337→        summary=(
   338→            "Deferred backlog decision required: "
   339→            f"{cluster_count} {cluster_label} + {individual_count} individual {individual_label} "
   340→            "must be reactivated, moved to backlog, or marked wontfix."
   341→        ),
   342→        detail={
   343→            "temporary_skipped_count": count,
   344→            "deferred_cluster_count": cluster_count,
   345→            "deferred_individual_count": individual_count,
   346→            "reactivate_command": reactivate_cmd,
   347→            "backlog_command": backlog_cmd,
   348→            "wontfix_command": wontfix_cmd,
   349→            "planning_tools": [
   350→                {
   351→                    "label": "Review deferred backlog",
   352→                    "command": inspect_cmd,
   353→                },
   354→                {
   355→                    "label": "Inspect a specific cluster or item",
   356→                    "command": inspect_item_cmd,
   357→                },
   358→                {
   359→                    "label": "Reactivate a subset",
   360→                    "command": subset_reactivate_cmd,
   361→                },
   362→                {
   363→                    "label": "Move a subset to backlog",
   364→                    "command": subset_backlog_cmd,
   365→                },
   366→                {
   367→                    "label": "Mark a subset as permanent wontfix",
   368→                    "command": subset_wontfix_cmd,
   369→                },
   370→            ],
   371→            "decision_options": [
   372→                {
   373→                    "label": "Reactivate deferred work",
   374→                    "command": reactivate_cmd,
   375→                },
   376→                {
   377→                    "label": "Move deferred work to backlog",
   378→                    "command": backlog_cmd,
   379→                },
   380→                {
   381→                    "label": "Convert deferred work to wontfix",
   382→                    "command": wontfix_cmd,
   383→                },
   384→            ],
   385→        },
   386→        primary_command=reactivate_cmd,
   387→    )
   388→    item["execution_visibility"] = "always"
   389→    return item
   390→
   391→
   392→__all__ = [
   393→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   394→    "build_create_plan_item",
   395→    "build_deferred_disposition_item",
   396→    "build_import_scores_item",
   397→    "build_run_scan_item",
   398→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   399→]
   400→

1→"""Triage sync — inject/prune triage stage IDs based on review issue changes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
     7→    TRIAGE_IDS,
     8→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
     9→    QueueSyncResult,
    10→    confirmed_triage_stage_names,
    11→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix,
    12→    recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→
    18→from .defer_policy import (
    19→    DeferEscalationOptions,
    20→    DeferUpdateOptions,
    21→    should_escalate_defer_state,
    22→    update_defer_state,
    23→)
    24→from .triage_start_policy import decide_triage_start
    25→
    26→[REDACTED]
    27→_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD = "deferred_review_ids"
    28→[REDACTED]
