    67→
    68→def _option_value(options: object | None, name: str, default: Any) -> Any:
    69→    if options is None:
    70→        return default
    71→    return getattr(options, name, default)
    72→
    73→
    74→def _resolved_scan_path(options: object | None, state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    75→    scan_path = _option_value(options, "scan_path", state.get("scan_path"))
    76→    if hasattr(scan_path, "__class__") and scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState":
    77→        return state.get("scan_path")
    78→    return scan_path
    79→
    80→
    81→def _is_fresh_boundary(plan: dict | None) -> bool:
    82→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    83→        return True
    84→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    85→    if not scores:
    86→        return True
    87→    return isinstance(scores, dict) and bool(scores.get("reset"))
    88→
    89→
    90→def _is_objective_item(item: WorkQueueItem, *, skipped_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    91→    return (
    92→        item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
    93→        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    94→        and item.get("id", "") not in skipped_ids
    95→    )
    96→
    97→
    98→def _review_issue_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    99→    return [
   100→        item for item in items
   101→        if item.get("detector", "") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
   102→    ]
   103→
   104→
   105→def _executable_review_issue_items(
   106→    plan: dict | None,
   107→    state: StateModel,
   108→    review_issue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   109→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   110→    """Hide raw review findings until triage is current for the live issue set."""
   111→    if not review_issue_items or not isinstance(plan, dict):
   112→        return review_issue_items
   113→
   114→    triage_snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(plan, state)
   115→    if triage_snapshot.has_triage_in_queue:
   116→        return []
   117→    if triage_snapshot.is_triage_stale:
   118→        return []
   119→    if not triage_snapshot.triage_has_run:
   120→        return []
   121→    return review_issue_items
   122→
   123→
   124→def _subjective_partitions(
   125→    state: StateModel,
   126→    *,
   127→    scoped_issues: dict[str, dict],
   128→    threshold: float,
   129→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   130→    candidates = build_subjective_items(state, scoped_issues, threshold=threshold)
   131→    initial = [item for item in candidates if item.get("initial_review")]
   132→    postflight = [item for item in candidates if not item.get("initial_review")]
   133→    return initial, postflight
   134→
   135→
   136→def _workflow_partitions(
   137→    plan: dict | None,
   138→    state: StateModel,
   139→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   140→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   141→        return [], [], []
   142→    scan_items = [
   143→        item
   144→        for item in (
   145→            build_deferred_disposition_item(plan),
   146→            build_run_scan_item(plan),
   147→        )
   148→        if item is not None
   149→    ]
   150→    postflight_workflow = [
   151→        item
   152→        for item in (
   153→            build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state),
   154→            build_import_scores_item(plan, state),
   155→            build_communicate_score_item(plan, state),
   156→            build_create_plan_item(plan),
   157→        )
   158→        if item is not None
   159→    ]
   160→    triage_items = build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)
   161→    return scan_items, postflight_workflow, triage_items
   162→
   163→
   164→def _phase_for_snapshot(
   165→    *,
   166→    fresh_boundary: bool,
   167→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   168→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   169→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   170→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   171→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   172→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   173→) -> str:
   174→    if fresh_boundary and initial_review_items:
   175→        return PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   176→    if objective_items:
   177→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   178→    if scan_items:
   179→        return PHASE_SCAN
   180→    if postflight_review_items:
   181→        return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   182→    if postflight_workflow_items:
   183→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   184→    if triage_items:
   185→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   186→    return PHASE_SCAN
   187→
   188→
   189→def _execution_items_for_phase(
   190→    phase: str,
   191→    *,
   192→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   193→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   194→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   195→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   196→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   197→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   198→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   199→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL:
   200→        return initial_review_items
   201→    if phase == PHASE_EXECUTE:
   202→        return objective_items
   203→    if phase == PHASE_SCAN:
   204→        deferred_items = [
   205→            item for item in scan_items
   206→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID
   207→        ]
   208→        if deferred_items:
   209→            return deferred_items
   210→        return [
   211→            item for item in scan_items
   212→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
   213→        ]
   214→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   215→        return postflight_review_items
   216→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   217→        return postflight_workflow_items
   218→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   219→        return triage_items
   220→    return []
   221→
   222→
   223→def build_queue_snapshot(
   224→    state: StateModel,
   225→    *,
   226→    options: object | None = None,
   227→    plan: dict | None = None,
   228→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   229→) -> QueueSnapshot:
   230→    """Build the canonical queue snapshot for the current state."""
   231→    context = _option_value(options, "context", None)
   232→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else (
   233→        plan if plan is not None else _option_value(options, "plan", None)
   234→    )
   235→    scan_path = _resolved_scan_path(options, state)
   236→    skipped_ids = set((effective_plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   237→    scoped_issues = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   238→    scope = _option_value(options, "scope", None)
   239→    chronic = bool(_option_value(options, "chronic", False))
   240→
   241→    all_issue_items = build_issue_items(
   242→        state,
   243→        scan_path=scan_path,
   244→        status_filter="open",
   245→        scope=scope,
   246→        chronic=chronic,
   247→    )
   248→    objective_items = [
   249→        item for item in all_issue_items
   250→        if _is_objective_item(item, skipped_ids=skipped_ids)
   251→    ]
   252→    planned_ids = _planned_objective_ids(
   253→        {item.get("id", "") for item in objective_items},
   254→        effective_plan,
   255→    )
   256→    planned_objective_items = [
   257→        item for item in objective_items
   258→        if item.get("id", "") in planned_ids
   259→    ]
   260→    review_issue_items = _review_issue_items(all_issue_items)
   261→    executable_review_items = _executable_review_issue_items(
   262→        effective_plan,
   263→        state,
   264→        review_issue_items,
   265→    )
   266→    initial_review_items, subjective_postflight_items = _subjective_partitions(
   267→        state,
   268→        scoped_issues=scoped_issues,
   269→        threshold=target_strict,
   270→    )
   271→    postflight_review_items = [*subjective_postflight_items, *executable_review_items]
   272→    scan_items, postflight_workflow_items, triage_items = _workflow_partitions(
   273→        effective_plan,
   274→        state,
   275→    )
   276→
   277→    fresh_boundary = _is_fresh_boundary(effective_plan)
   278→    phase = _phase_for_snapshot(
   279→        fresh_boundary=fresh_boundary,
   280→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   281→        objective_items=planned_objective_items,
   282→        scan_items=scan_items,
   283→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   284→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   285→        triage_items=triage_items,
   286→    )
   287→    execution_items = _execution_items_for_phase(
   288→        phase,
   289→        objective_items=planned_objective_items,
   290→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   291→        scan_items=scan_items,
   292→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   293→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   294→        triage_items=triage_items,
   295→    )
   296→
   297→    execution_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in execution_items}
   298→    backlog_items = [
   299→        item for item in (
   300→            [
   301→                *planned_objective_items,
   302→                *initial_review_items,
   303→                *subjective_postflight_items,
   304→                *review_issue_items,
   305→                *scan_items,
   306→                *postflight_workflow_items,
   307→                *triage_items,
   308→            ]
   309→        )
   310→        if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   311→    ]
   312→    objective_backlog_count = sum(
   313→        1 for item in planned_objective_items if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   314→    )
   315→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers = len(planned_objective_items) < len(objective_items)
   316→
   317→    return QueueSnapshot(
   318→        phase=phase,
   319→        all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items),
   320→        all_initial_review_items=tuple(initial_review_items),
   321→        all_postflight_review_items=tuple(postflight_review_items),
   322→        all_scan_items=tuple(scan_items),
   323→        all_postflight_workflow_items=tuple(postflight_workflow_items),
   324→        all_postflight_triage_items=tuple(triage_items),
   325→        execution_items=tuple(execution_items),
   326→        backlog_items=tuple(backlog_items),
   327→        objective_in_scope_count=len(objective_items),
   328→        planned_objective_count=len(planned_objective_items),
   329→        objective_execution_count=sum(
   330→            1
   331→            for item in execution_items
   332→            if item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   333→            and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   334→        ),
   335→        objective_backlog_count=objective_backlog_count,
   336→        subjective_initial_count=len(initial_review_items),
   337→        subjective_postflight_count=len(postflight_review_items),
   338→        workflow_postflight_count=len(postflight_workflow_items),
   339→        triage_pending_count=len(triage_items),
   340→        has_unplanned_objective_blockers=has_unplanned_objective_blockers,
   341→    )
   342→
   343→
   344→def coarse_phase_name(phase: str) -> str:
   345→    """Map internal queue phases to the persisted coarse lifecycle value."""
   346→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL or phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   347→        return "review"
   348→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   349→        return "workflow"
   350→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   351→        return "triage"
   352→    return phase
   353→
   354→
   355→__all__ = [
   356→    "PHASE_EXECUTE",
   357→    "PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL",
   358→    "PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT",
   359→    "PHASE_SCAN",
   360→    "PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT",
   361→    "PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT",
   362→    "QueueSnapshot",
   363→    "build_queue_snapshot",
   364→    "coarse_phase_name",
   365→]

1→"""Shared helpers for queue-sync decisions.
     2→
     3→Every sync module (``sync_triage``, ``sync_dimensions``, ``sync_workflow``,
     4→``auto_cluster_sync``) needs to answer two questions:
     5→
     6→1. **Is there an objective backlog?**  (Should we defer optional items?)
     7→2. **Are we mid-cycle?**  (Has a queue cycle started and not yet completed?)
     8→
     9→This module provides a single definition of each so the logic stays
    10→consistent and changes propagate everywhere at once.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    17→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→def has_objective_backlog(
    26→    state_or_issues: StateModel | dict,
    27→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    28→) -> bool:
    29→    """Return whether a planned objective backlog exists.
    30→
    31→    Prefers the pre-computed *policy* snapshot when available (plan-aware:
    32→    only counts planned objectives post-triage).  Falls back to scanning
    33→    *state_or_issues* directly (counts all open non-subjective issues).
    34→    """
    35→    if policy is not None:
    36→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
    37→
    38→    # Accept either full state payload (`{"issues": ...}`) or raw issues dict.
    39→    issues = state_or_issues
    40→    if isinstance(state_or_issues, dict):
    41→        maybe_issues = state_or_issues.get("issues")
    42→        if isinstance(maybe_issues, dict):
    43→            issues = maybe_issues
    44→    return any(
    45→        f.get("status") == "open"
    46→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    47→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    48→        for f in issues.values()
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def is_mid_cycle(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    53→    """True when a queue cycle is in progress.
    54→
    55→    A cycle is active when ``plan_start_scores`` is set to real score
    56→    values.  The ``{"reset": True}`` sentinel (set by lifecycle reset)
    57→    does NOT count as mid-cycle — it means "seed real scores on next scan".
    58→    """
    59→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    60→    return bool(scores) and not (isinstance(scores, dict) and scores.get("reset"))
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = ["has_objective_backlog", "is_mid_cycle"]
    64→

1→"""Shared defer-tracking helpers for triage and subjective rerun fairness."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
     9→
    10→DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS = 3
    11→DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS = 7
    12→
    13→
    14→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    15→class DeferUpdateOptions:
    16→    """Options for durable defer-state updates."""
    17→
    18→    deferred_ids_field: str = "deferred_ids"
    19→    now: str | None = None
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    23→class DeferEscalationOptions:
    24→    """Options for defer-state escalation checks."""
    25→
    26→    deferred_ids_field: str = "deferred_ids"
    27→    now: str | None = None
    28→    min_scans: int = DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS
    29→    min_days: int = DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS
    30→
    31→
    32→def _coerce_non_negative_int(value: object, *, default: int = 0) -> int:
    33→    try:
    34→        parsed = int(value)
    35→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    36→        return default
    37→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else default
    38→
    39→
    40→def _scan_count(state: StateModel) -> int:
    41→    return _coerce_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count"), default=0)
    42→
    43→
    44→def _normalize_timestamp(raw: object) -> str | None:
    45→    if not isinstance(raw, str):
    46→        return None
    47→    value = raw.strip()
    48→    return value or None
    49→
    50→
    51→def _parse_timestamp(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    52→    normalized = _normalize_timestamp(raw)
    53→    if not normalized:
    54→        return None
    55→    try:
    56→        return datetime.fromisoformat(normalized.replace("Z", "+00:00")).astimezone(UTC)
    57→    except ValueError:
    58→        return None
    59→
    60→
    61→def _current_timestamp(
    62→    state: StateModel,
    63→    *,
    64→    now: str | None = None,
    65→) -> str:
    66→    for raw in (state.get("last_scan"), now):
    67→        normalized = _normalize_timestamp(raw)
    68→        if normalized:
    69→            return normalized
    70→    return utc_now()
    71→
    72→
    73→def _normalized_ids(ids: set[str]) -> list[str]:
    74→    return sorted({str(issue_id).strip() for issue_id in ids if str(issue_id).strip()})
    75→
    76→
    77→def _deferred_ids(
    78→    defer_state: dict[str, object],
    79→    *,
    80→    deferred_ids_field: str,
    81→) -> list[str]:
    82→    raw = defer_state.get(deferred_ids_field)
    83→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
    84→        return []
    85→    return sorted(
    86→        {
    87→            str(issue_id).strip()
    88→            for issue_id in raw
    89→            if str(issue_id).strip()
    90→        }
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→def update_defer_state(
    95→    defer_state: dict[str, object] | None,
    96→    *,
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    deferred_ids: set[str],
    99→    options: DeferUpdateOptions | None = None,
   100→) -> dict[str, object]:
   101→    """Return updated durable defer metadata for the current deferred ID set."""
   102→    resolved_options = options or DeferUpdateOptions()
   103→    previous = defer_state if isinstance(defer_state, dict) else {}
   104→    current_ids = _normalized_ids(deferred_ids)
   105→    previous_ids = _deferred_ids(
   106→        previous,
   107→        deferred_ids_field=resolved_options.deferred_ids_field,
   108→    )
   109→    scan_count = _scan_count(state)
   110→    timestamp = _current_timestamp(state, now=resolved_options.now)
   111→
   112→    updated = dict(previous)
   113→    updated[resolved_options.deferred_ids_field] = current_ids
   114→    updated["last_deferred_scan"] = scan_count
   115→    updated["last_deferred_at"] = timestamp
   116→
   117→    same_ids = bool(current_ids) and current_ids == previous_ids
   118→    if same_ids:
   119→        defer_count = _coerce_non_negative_int(previous.get("defer_count"), default=1)
   120→        prior_scan = _coerce_non_negative_int(previous.get("last_deferred_scan"), default=-1)
   121→        if prior_scan != scan_count:
   122→            defer_count += 1
   123→        branch_updates = {
   124→            "defer_count": max(1, defer_count),
   125→            "first_deferred_scan": _coerce_non_negative_int(
   126→            previous.get("first_deferred_scan"),
   127→            default=scan_count,
   128→            ),
   129→            "first_deferred_at": str(previous.get("first_deferred_at") or timestamp),
   130→        }
   131→    else:
   132→        branch_updates = {
   133→            "defer_count": 1 if current_ids else 0,
   134→            "first_deferred_scan": scan_count,
   135→            "first_deferred_at": timestamp,
   136→        }
   137→    updated.update(branch_updates)
   138→    return updated
   139→
   140→
   141→def should_escalate_defer_state(
   142→    defer_state: dict[str, object] | None,
   143→    *,
   144→    state: StateModel,
   145→    options: DeferEscalationOptions | None = None,
   146→) -> bool:
   147→    """Return True when defer metadata crosses configured escalation bounds."""
   148→    if not isinstance(defer_state, dict):
   149→        return False
   150→    resolved_options = options or DeferEscalationOptions()
   151→
   152→    deferred_ids = _deferred_ids(
   153→        defer_state,
   154→        deferred_ids_field=resolved_options.deferred_ids_field,
   155→    )
   156→    if not deferred_ids:
   157→        return False
   158→
   159→    min_scans = max(1, int(resolved_options.min_scans))
   160→    min_days = max(1, int(resolved_options.min_days))
   161→
   162→    defer_count = _coerce_non_negative_int(defer_state.get("defer_count"), default=0)
   163→    if defer_count >= min_scans:
   164→        return True
   165→
   166→    first_scan = _coerce_non_negative_int(
   167→        defer_state.get("first_deferred_scan"),
   168→        default=-1,
   169→    )
   170→    current_scan = _scan_count(state)
   171→    if first_scan >= 0 and current_scan - first_scan + 1 >= min_scans:
   172→        return True
   173→
   174→    first_deferred_at = _parse_timestamp(defer_state.get("first_deferred_at"))
   175→    current_at = _parse_timestamp(state.get("last_scan")) or _parse_timestamp(
   176→        resolved_options.now
   177→    )
   178→    if first_deferred_at is None or current_at is None:
   179→        return False
   180→    return (current_at - first_deferred_at).total_seconds() >= min_days * 86400
   181→
   182→
   183→__all__ = [
   184→    "DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS",
   185→    "DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS",
   186→    "DeferEscalationOptions",
   187→    "DeferUpdateOptions",
   188→    "should_escalate_defer_state",
   189→    "update_defer_state",
   190→]
   191→

1→"""Shared helpers for queue-sync decisions.
     2→
     3→Every sync module (``sync_triage``, ``sync_dimensions``, ``sync_workflow``,
     4→``auto_cluster_sync``) needs to answer two questions:
     5→
     6→1. **Is there an objective backlog?**  (Should we defer optional items?)
     7→2. **Are we mid-cycle?**  (Has a queue cycle started and not yet completed?)
     8→
     9→This module provides a single definition of each so the logic stays
    10→consistent and changes propagate everywhere at once.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    17→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→def has_objective_backlog(
    26→    state_or_issues: StateModel | dict,
    27→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    28→) -> bool:
    29→    """Return whether a planned objective backlog exists.
    30→
    31→    Prefers the pre-computed *policy* snapshot when available (plan-aware:
    32→    only counts planned objectives post-triage).  Falls back to scanning
    33→    *state_or_issues* directly (counts all open non-subjective issues).
    34→    """
    35→    if policy is not None:
    36→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
    37→
    38→    # Accept either full state payload (`{"issues": ...}`) or raw issues dict.
    39→    issues = state_or_issues
    40→    if isinstance(state_or_issues, dict):
    41→        maybe_issues = state_or_issues.get("issues")
    42→        if isinstance(maybe_issues, dict):
    43→            issues = maybe_issues
    44→    return any(
    45→        f.get("status") == "open"
    46→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    47→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    48→        for f in issues.values()
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def is_mid_cycle(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    53→    """True when a queue cycle is in progress.
    54→
    55→    A cycle is active when ``plan_start_scores`` is set to real score
    56→    values.  The ``{"reset": True}`` sentinel (set by lifecycle reset)
    57→    does NOT count as mid-cycle — it means "seed real scores on next scan".
    58→    """
    59→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    60→    return bool(scores) and not (isinstance(scores, dict) and scores.get("reset"))
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = ["has_objective_backlog", "is_mid_cycle"]
    64→

1→"""Canonical stale/unscored subjective policy helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import all_subjective_entries
    11→
    12→_REVIEW_DETECTORS = ("review", "concerns")
    13→
    14→
    15→def open_review_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    16→    """Return IDs of open review/concerns issues from state."""
    17→    return {
    18→        fid
    19→        for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    20→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
    21→    }
    22→
    23→
    24→def _subjective_entry_id(
    25→    dimension_key: object,
    26→    *,
    27→    subjective_prefix: str,
    28→) -> str:
    29→    return f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(str(dimension_key))}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def _subjective_entries(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
    33→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    34→    if not dim_scores:
    35→        return []
    36→    return list(all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores))
    37→
    38→
    39→def _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
    40→    entries: list[dict],
    41→    *,
    42→    subjective_prefix: str,
    43→    predicate,
    44→) -> set[str]:
    45→    collected: set[str] = set()
    46→    for entry in entries:
    47→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    48→        if not dim_key or not predicate(entry):
    49→            continue
    50→        collected.add(_subjective_entry_id(dim_key, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix))
    51→    return collected
    52→
    53→
    54→def current_stale_ids(
    55→    state: StateModel,
    56→    *,
    57→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    58→) -> set[str]:
    59→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    60→    return _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
    61→        _subjective_entries(state),
    62→        subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
    63→        predicate=lambda entry: bool(entry.get("stale")),
    64→    )
    65→
    66→
    67→def _unscored_ids_from_assessments(
    68→    assessments: dict,
    69→    *,
    70→    subjective_prefix: str,
    71→) -> set[str]:
    72→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    73→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    74→        if not isinstance(payload, dict) or not payload.get("placeholder") or not dim_key:
    75→            continue
    76→        unscored.add(_subjective_entry_id(dim_key, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix))
    77→    return unscored
    78→
    79→
    80→def _unscored_ids_from_dimension_scores(
    81→    entries: list[dict],
    82→    *,
    83→    subjective_prefix: str,
    84→) -> set[str]:
    85→    return _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
    86→        entries,
    87→        subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
    88→        predicate=lambda entry: bool(entry.get("placeholder")),
    89→    )
    90→
    91→
    92→def current_unscored_ids(
    93→    state: StateModel,
    94→    *,
    95→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    96→) -> set[str]:
    97→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored."""
    98→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    99→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
   100→        return _unscored_ids_from_assessments(
   101→            assessments,
   102→            subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
   103→        )
   104→    return _unscored_ids_from_dimension_scores(
   105→        _subjective_entries(state),
   106→        subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
   107→    )
   108→
   109→
   110→def current_under_target_ids(
   111→    state: StateModel,
   112→    *,
   113→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   114→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
   115→) -> set[str]:
   116→    """Return under-target subjective IDs that are neither stale nor unscored."""
   117→    entries = _subjective_entries(state)
   118→    if not entries:
   119→        return set()
   120→
   121→    stale_ids = current_stale_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
   122→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
   123→
   124→    return {
   125→        item_id
   126→        for item_id in _collect_subjective_entry_ids(
   127→            entries,
   128→            subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix,
   129→            predicate=lambda entry: (
   130→                not entry.get("placeholder")
   131→                and not entry.get("stale")
   132→                and float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))) < target_strict
   133→            ),
   134→        )
   135→        if item_id not in stale_ids and item_id not in unscored_ids
   136→    }
   137→
   138→
   139→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   140→    """Hash open review/concerns issue IDs to detect triage-relevant changes."""
   141→    review_ids = sorted(open_review_ids(state))
   142→    if not review_ids:
   143→        return ""
   144→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   145→
   146→
   147→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   148→    """Return open review/concerns IDs that appeared since the last triage."""
   149→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   150→    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   151→    active = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
   152→    known = triaged | active
   153→    return open_review_ids(state) - known if known else set()
   154→
   155→
   156→def is_triage_stale(
   157→    plan: dict,
   158→    state: StateModel,
   159→) -> bool:
   160→    """Return True when genuinely new review issues appeared since last triage.
   161→
   162→    In-progress triage (confirmed stages + stage IDs in queue) is NOT
   163→    considered stale — the lifecycle filter in the work queue already
   164→    forces triage stages to the front.
   165→    """
   166→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   167→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   168→    active_ids = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
   169→    known = triaged_ids | active_ids
   170→    return bool(open_review_ids(state) - known)
   171→
   172→
   173→__all__ = [
   174→    "compute_new_issue_ids",

    77→def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    78→    """Best-effort clear for config.needs_rescan after a successful scan merge."""
    79→    if not config.get("needs_rescan"):

    81→    try:
    82→        config["needs_rescan"] = False
    83→        _save_config(config)

   106→def _ensure_state_lang_capabilities(
   107→    state: StateModel,
   108→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:

   110→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
   111→    if capabilities is None:
   112→        normalized: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   113→        state["lang_capabilities"] = normalized
   114→        return normalized
   115→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict):

   151→    reset_subjective_count: int = 0

   179→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   180→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   181→    config: dict[str, object],
   182→    state: StateModel,
   183→    lang: LangRun | None,
   184→) -> LangRun | None:
   185→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   186→    if not lang:
   187→        return None
   188→
   189→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   190→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   191→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   192→        lang,
   193→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   194→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   195→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   196→            subjective_assessments=_state_subjective_assessments(state),
   197→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   198→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   199→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   200→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   201→        ),
   202→    )
   203→
   204→    lang_capabilities = _ensure_state_lang_capabilities(state)
   205→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   206→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   207→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   208→    }
   209→    return runtime_lang
   210→
   211→
   212→def _apply_assessment_reset(payload: dict, *, source: str, now: str) -> None:
   213→    """Apply a standard reset mutation to a single subjective assessment payload.
   214→
   215→    Sets score to 0.0, stamps assessed_at/reset_by/placeholder, and strips
   216→    any cached scoring artifacts (integrity_penalty, components,
   217→    component_scores) plus provisional-override markers.
   218→    """
   219→    payload["score"] = 0.0
   220→    payload["source"] = source
   221→    payload["assessed_at"] = now
   222→    payload["reset_by"] = source
   223→    payload["placeholder"] = True
   224→    payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   225→    payload.pop("components", None)
   226→    payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   227→    payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   228→    payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   229→
   230→
   231→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   232→    state: StateModel,
   233→    *,
   234→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   235→) -> int:
   236→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   237→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   238→
   239→    reset_keys = {
   240→        key.strip()
   241→        for key in assessments
   242→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   243→    }
   244→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   245→
   246→    now = utc_now()
   247→    source = "scan_reset_subjective"
   248→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   249→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   250→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   251→            _apply_assessment_reset(payload, source=source, now=now)
   252→            continue
   253→        assessments[key] = {
   254→            "score": 0.0,
   255→            "source": source,
   256→            "assessed_at": now,
   257→            "reset_by": source,
   258→            "placeholder": True,
   259→        }
   260→    return len(reset_keys)
   261→
   262→
   263→
   264→
   265→def prepare_scan_runtime(args: argparse.Namespace) -> ScanRuntime:
   266→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   267→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   268→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   269→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   270→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   271→    path = Path(args.path)
   272→    reset_script_import_caches(str(path))
   273→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   274→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   275→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   276→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   277→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   278→            state,
   279→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   280→        )
   281→
   282→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   283→    profile = resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   284→    include_slow_effective = effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   285→
   286→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   287→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   288→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   289→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   290→
   291→    return ScanRuntime(
   292→        args=args,
   293→        state_path=state_file,
   294→        state=state,
   295→        path=path,
   296→        config=config,
   297→        lang=lang,
   298→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   299→        profile=profile,
   300→        effective_include_slow=include_slow_effective,
   301→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   302→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   303→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   304→        force_rescan=bool(getattr(args, "force_rescan", False)),
   305→    )
   306→
   307→
   308→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_issues(
   309→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   310→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   311→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   312→    """Append stale exclude issues when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   313→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   314→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   315→        return issues
   316→
   317→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   318→    stale = audit_excluded_dirs(
   319→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, get_project_root()
   320→    )
   321→    if not stale:
   322→        return issues
   323→
   324→    augmented = list(issues)
   325→    augmented.extend(stale)
   326→    for stale_issue in stale:
   327→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_issue['summary']}", "dim"))
   328→    return augmented
   329→
   330→
   331→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_issues(
   332→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   333→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   334→    *,
   335→    decay_scans: int,
   336→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   337→    """Append re-triage issues for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   338→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   339→        issues,
   340→        state=runtime.state,
   341→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   342→        project_root=get_project_root(),
   343→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   344→    )
   345→
   346→
   347→def run_scan_generation(
   348→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   349→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   350→    """Run detector pipeline and return issues, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   351→    enable_file_cache()
   352→    enable_parse_cache()
   353→    try:
   354→        issues, potentials = generate_plan_issues(
   355→            runtime.path,
   356→            lang=runtime.lang,
   357→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   358→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   359→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   360→                profile=runtime.profile,
   361→            ),
   362→        )
   363→    finally:
   364→        disable_parse_cache()
   365→        disable_file_cache()
   366→
   367→    codebase_metrics = collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   368→    warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   369→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   370→    )
   371→    issues = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_issues(issues, runtime)
   372→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   373→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   374→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   375→    )
   376→    issues, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_issues(
   377→        issues,
   378→        runtime,
   379→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   380→    )
   381→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   382→    return issues, potentials, codebase_metrics
   383→
   384→
   385→def merge_scan_results(
   386→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   387→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   388→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   389→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   390→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   391→    """Merge issues into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   392→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   393→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   394→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   395→
   396→    if not path_changed:
   397→        prev = score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   398→    else:
   399→        prev = ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   400→    prev_dim_scores = (
   401→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   402→    )
   403→
   404→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   405→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   406→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   407→
   408→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   409→
   410→    diff = merge_scan(
   411→        runtime.state,
   412→        issues,
   413→        options=MergeScanOptions(
   414→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   415→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   416→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   417→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   418→            potentials=potentials,
   419→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   420→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   421→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   422→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   423→        ),
   424→    )
   425→
   426→    mark_stale_holistic(
   427→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   428→    )
   429→    save_state(
   430→        runtime.state,
   431→        runtime.state_path,
   432→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   433→    )
   434→
   435→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   436→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   437→
   438→    return ScanMergeResult(
   439→        diff=diff,
   440→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   441→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   442→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   443→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   444→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   445→    )
   446→
   447→
   448→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   449→    state: StateModel,
   450→    config: dict[str, object],
   451→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   452→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden issue counters."""
   453→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   454→        resolve_issue_noise_settings(config)
   455→    )
   456→    issues_by_id = _state_issues(state)
   457→    open_issues = [
   458→        issue
   459→        for issue in path_scoped_issues(
   460→            issues_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   461→        ).values()
   462→        if issue.get("status") == "open"
   463→    ]
   464→    _, hidden_by_detector = apply_issue_noise_budget(
   465→        open_issues,
   466→        budget=noise_budget,
   467→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   468→    )
   469→
   470→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   471→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   472→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   473→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   474→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   475→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   476→    )
   477→
   478→
   479→def persist_reminder_history(
   480→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   481→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   482→) -> None:
   483→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   484→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   485→        return
   486→
   487→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   488→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   489→    save_state(
   490→        runtime.state,
   491→        runtime.state_path,
   492→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   493→    )
   494→
   495→
   496→__all__ = [
   497→    "ScanStateContractError",
   498→    "ScanMergeResult",
   499→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   500→    "ScanRuntime",
   501→    "merge_scan_results",
   502→    "persist_reminder_history",
   503→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   504→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   505→    "run_scan_generation",
   506→]
   507→

    51→    ImportPayloadLoadError,
    52→    resolve_override_context,
    53→)
    54→from .plan_sync import PlanImportSyncRequest, sync_plan_after_import
    55→from .results import report_review_import_outcome
    56→
    57→_SCORECARD_SUBJECTIVE_AT_TARGET = bind_scorecard_subjective_at_target(
    58→    reporting_dimensions_mod=reporting_dimensions_mod,
    59→    subjective_integrity_mod=subjective_integrity_mod,

    61→
    62→
    63→def _resolve_import_payload(
    64→    import_file,
    65→    *,
    66→    lang_name: str,
    67→    import_config: ReviewImportConfig,
    68→) -> tuple[dict, bool, str | None]:
    69→    """Validate import flags and load payload with policy checks."""
    70→    override_enabled, override_attest = resolve_override_context(
    71→        manual_override=import_config.manual_override,
    72→        manual_attest=import_config.manual_attest,
    73→    )
    74→    try:
    75→        issues_data = load_import_issues_data(
    76→            import_file,
    77→            options=build_import_load_config(
    78→                lang_name=lang_name,
    79→                import_config=import_config,
    80→                override_enabled=override_enabled,
    81→                override_attest=override_attest,
    82→            ),
    83→        )
    84→    except ImportPayloadLoadError as exc:
    85→        print_import_load_errors(
    86→            exc.errors,
    87→            import_file=str(import_file),
    88→            colorize_fn=colorize,
    89→        )
    90→        raise PacketValidationError("import payload validation failed", exit_code=1) from exc
    91→
    92→    return issues_data, override_enabled, override_attest
    93→
    94→
    95→def _build_working_state(state: dict, state_file) -> dict:
    96→    """Return state snapshot used for import mutation/dry-run rendering."""
    97→    state_path = Path(state_file) if state_file is not None else None
    98→    if state_path is not None and state_path.exists():
    99→        return copy.deepcopy(state_mod.load_state(state_path))
   100→    return copy.deepcopy(state)
   101→
   102→
   103→def _apply_assessment_policy(
   104→    *,
   105→    working_state: dict,
   106→    issues_data: dict,
   107→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   108→) -> int:
   109→    """Apply provisional/clear flags based on assessment policy mode."""
   110→    assessment_keys = imported_assessment_keys(issues_data)
   111→    if assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override":
   112→        return mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
   113→            working_state,
   114→            assessment_keys=assessment_keys,
   115→        )
   116→    if assessment_policy.mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
   117→        clear_provisional_override_flags(
   118→            working_state,
   119→            assessment_keys=assessment_keys,
   120→        )
   121→    return 0
   122→
   123→
   124→def _raise_on_partial_skip(diff: dict, *, allow_partial: bool) -> None:
   125→    """Refuse import when payload skips issues and partial imports are disabled."""
   126→    if diff.get("skipped", 0) <= 0 or allow_partial:
   127→        return
   128→    details_lines: list[str] = []
   129→    for detail in diff.get("skipped_details", []):
   130→        reasons = "; ".join(detail.get("missing", []))
   131→        details_lines.append(
   132→            f"  #{detail.get('index', '?')} ({detail.get('identifier', '<none>')}): {reasons}"
   133→        )
   134→    msg = "import produced skipped issue(s); refusing partial import."
   135→    if details_lines:
   136→        msg += "\n" + "\n".join(details_lines)
   137→    msg += "\nFix the payload and retry, or pass --allow-partial to override."
   138→    raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1)
   139→
   140→
   141→def _append_assessment_import_audit(
   142→    *,
   143→    working_state: dict,
   144→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   145→    provisional_count: int,
   146→    override_attest: str | None,
   147→    import_file,
   148→) -> None:
   149→    """Record audit metadata for assessment-bearing import payloads."""
   150→    if not assessment_policy.assessments_present:
   151→        return
   152→    audit = working_state.setdefault("assessment_import_audit", [])
   153→    audit.append(
   154→        {
   155→            "timestamp": state_mod.utc_now(),
   156→            "mode": assessment_policy.mode,
   157→            "trusted": bool(assessment_policy.trusted),
   158→            "reason": assessment_policy.reason,
   159→            "override_used": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   160→            "attested_external": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "attested_external"),
   161→            "provisional": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   162→            "provisional_count": int(provisional_count),
   163→            "attest": (override_attest or "").strip(),
   164→            "import_file": str(import_file),
   165→        }
   166→    )
   167→
   168→
   169→def _persist_import_state(
   170→    *,
   171→    state: dict,
   172→    working_state: dict,
   173→    state_file,
   174→    diff: dict,
   175→    assessment_mode: str,
   176→    config: dict | None,
   177→    import_file: str,
   178→    import_payload: dict,
   179→) -> None:
   180→    """Persist imported state and synchronize the work plan."""
   181→    state.clear()
   182→    state.update(working_state)
   183→    state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
   184→    sync_plan_after_import(
   185→        state,
   186→        diff,
   187→        assessment_mode,
   188→        request=PlanImportSyncRequest(
   189→            state_file=state_file,
   190→            config=config,
   191→            import_file=import_file,
   192→            import_payload=import_payload,
   193→        ),
   194→    )
   195→
   196→
   197→def _guard_pending_import_scores_match(
   198→    *,
   199→    state: dict,
   200→    state_file,
   201→    import_file: str,
   202→    issues_data: dict,
   203→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   204→) -> None:
   205→    """Refuse durable imports that do not match the queued score-import batch."""
   206→    if assessment_policy.mode not in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
   207→        return
   208→    plan_path = plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
   209→    if not has_living_plan(plan_path):
   210→        return
   211→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   212→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   213→        return
   214→    pending_meta = pending_import_scores_meta(plan, state)
   215→    matches, reason = import_scores_meta_matches(
   216→        pending_meta,
   217→        import_file=import_file,
   218→        import_payload=issues_data,
   219→    )
   220→    if matches:
   221→        return
   222→    expected_file = ""
   223→    if pending_meta is not None:
   224→        expected_file = pending_meta.import_file.strip()
   225→    raise CommandError(
   226→        "Refusing durable score import: the pending "
   227→        "`workflow::import-scores` task is bound to a different review batch.\n"
   228→        f"  - {reason}\n"
   229→        + (f"Expected queued import file: {expected_file}\n" if expected_file else "")
   230→        + "Use the exact file shown by `desloppify next`, or clear the stale workflow item first.",
   231→        exit_code=1,
   232→    )
   233→
   234→
   235→def _has_refreshable_scorecard_context(state: dict) -> bool:
   236→    """Return True when state has scan-backed scorecard context.
   237→
   238→    Review imports can run against minimal/synthetic states (for example test
   239→    fixtures or pre-scan workflows). Refreshing the badge from those states can
   240→    overwrite scorecard.png with a misleading partial card.
   241→    """
   242→    if not state.get("last_scan"):
   243→        return False
   244→
   245→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores")
   246→    if not isinstance(dim_scores, dict) or not dim_scores:
   247→        return False
   248→
   249→    for data in dim_scores.values():
   250→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   251→            continue
   252→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   253→        if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
   254→            continue
   255→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
   256→            continue
   257→        if int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0) <= 0:
   258→            continue
   259→        return True
   260→    return False
   261→
   262→
   263→def _refresh_scorecard_after_import(
   264→    *,
   265→    state: dict,
   266→    config: dict | None,
   267→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   268→) -> bool:
   269→    """Refresh the scorecard badge when a trusted import updates live scores."""
   270→    if not assessment_policy.assessments_present or not assessment_policy.trusted:
   271→        return False
   272→    if not _has_refreshable_scorecard_context(state):
   273→        return False
   274→    emit_scorecard_badge(
   275→        SimpleNamespace(no_badge=False, badge_path=None),
   276→        config or {},
   277→        state,
   278→    )
   279→    return True
   280→
   281→
   282→def do_import(
   283→    import_file,
   284→    state,
   285→    lang,
   286→    state_file,
   287→    *,
   288→    import_config: ReviewImportConfig | None = None,
   289→    dry_run: bool = False,
   290→) -> None:
   291→    """Import mode: ingest agent-produced issues."""
   292→    resolved_import_config = import_config or ReviewImportConfig()
   293→    issues_data, _override_enabled, override_attest = _resolve_import_payload(
   294→        import_file,
   295→        lang_name=lang.name,
   296→        import_config=resolved_import_config,
   297→    )
   298→
   299→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel = (
   300→        assessment_policy_model_from_payload(issues_data)
   301→    )
   302→    print_assessment_mode_banner(
   303→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   304→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   305→    )
   306→    print_assessment_policy_notice(
   307→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   308→        import_file=str(import_file),
   309→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   310→    )
   311→    _guard_pending_import_scores_match(
   312→        state=state,
   313→        state_file=state_file,
   314→        import_file=str(import_file),
   315→        issues_data=issues_data,
   316→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   317→    )
   318→
   319→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   320→    working_state = _build_working_state(state, state_file)
   321→
   322→    diff = import_holistic_issues(issues_data, working_state, lang.name)
   323→    label = "Holistic review"
   324→    provisional_count = _apply_assessment_policy(
   325→        working_state=working_state,
   326→        issues_data=issues_data,
   327→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   328→    )
   329→    _raise_on_partial_skip(diff, allow_partial=resolved_import_config.allow_partial)
   330→    _append_assessment_import_audit(
   331→        working_state=working_state,
   332→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   333→        provisional_count=provisional_count,
   334→        override_attest=override_attest,
   335→        import_file=import_file,
   336→    )
   337→
   338→    if not dry_run:
   339→        _persist_import_state(
   340→            state=state,
   341→            working_state=working_state,
   342→            state_file=state_file,
   343→            diff=diff,
   344→            assessment_mode=assessment_policy.mode,
   345→            config=resolved_import_config.config,
   346→            import_file=str(import_file),
   347→            import_payload=issues_data,
   348→        )
   349→
   350→    display_state = state if not dry_run else working_state
   351→    report_review_import_outcome(
   352→        state=display_state,
   353→        lang_name=lang.name,
   354→        config=resolved_import_config.config,
   355→        diff=diff,
   356→        prev=prev,
   357→        label=label,
   358→        provisional_count=provisional_count,
   359→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   360→        scorecard_subjective_at_target_fn=_SCORECARD_SUBJECTIVE_AT_TARGET,
   361→    )
   362→    if not dry_run:
   363→        _refresh_scorecard_after_import(
   364→            state=state,
   365→            config=resolved_import_config.config,
   366→            assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   367→        )
   368→
   369→
   370→def do_validate_import(
   371→    import_file,
   372→    lang,
   373→    *,
   374→    import_config: ReviewImportConfig | None = None,
   375→) -> None:
   376→    """Validate import payload/policy and print mode without mutating state."""
   377→    resolved_import_config = import_config or ReviewImportConfig()
   378→    override_enabled, override_attest = resolve_override_context(
   379→        manual_override=resolved_import_config.manual_override,
   380→        manual_attest=resolved_import_config.manual_attest,
   381→    )
   382→    try:
   383→        issues_data = load_import_issues_data(
   384→            import_file,
   385→            config=build_import_load_config(
   386→                lang_name=lang.name,
   387→                import_config=resolved_import_config,
   388→                override_enabled=override_enabled,
   389→                override_attest=override_attest,
   390→            ),
   391→        )
   392→    except ImportPayloadLoadError as exc:
   393→        print_import_load_errors(
   394→            exc.errors,
   395→            import_file=str(import_file),
   396→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   397→        )
   398→        raise PacketValidationError("import payload validation failed", exit_code=1) from exc
   399→
   400→    assessment_policy = assessment_policy_model_from_payload(
   401→        issues_data
   402→    )
   403→    print_assessment_mode_banner(
   404→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   405→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   406→    )
   407→    print_assessment_policy_notice(
   408→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   409→        import_file=str(import_file),
   410→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   411→    )
   412→
   413→    issues_count = len(issues_data["issues"])
   414→    print(colorize("\n  Import payload validation passed.", "bold"))
   415→    print(colorize(f"  Issues parsed: {issues_count}", "dim"))
   416→    if assessment_policy.assessments_present:
   417→        count = int(assessment_policy.assessment_count)
   418→        print(colorize(f"  Assessment entries in payload: {count}", "dim"))
   419→    print(colorize("  No state changes were made (--validate-import).", "dim"))
   420→
   421→
   422→__all__ = [
   423→    "ReviewImportConfig",
   424→    "do_import",
   425→    "do_validate_import",
   426→    "subjective_at_target_dimensions",
   427→]

1→"""Living-plan update helpers used by resolve command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import logging
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→from typing import NamedTuple
     9→
    10→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    11→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.plan_load import warn_plan_load_degraded_once
    13→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import (
    14→    append_log_entry,
    15→    auto_complete_steps,
    16→    purge_ids,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.refresh_lifecycle import clear_postflight_scan_completion
    19→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import (
    20→    add_uncommitted_issues,
    21→    has_living_plan,
    22→    load_plan,
    23→    plan_path_for_state,
    24→    purge_uncommitted_ids,
    25→    save_plan,
    26→)
    27→
    28→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    29→
    30→
    31→class ClusterContext(NamedTuple):
    32→    cluster_name: str | None
    33→    cluster_completed: bool
    34→    cluster_remaining: int
    35→
    36→
    37→def capture_cluster_context(plan: dict, resolved_ids: list[str]) -> ClusterContext:
    38→    """Determine cluster membership for resolved issues before purge."""
    39→    clusters = plan.get("clusters") or {}
    40→    overrides = plan.get("overrides") or {}
    41→    cluster_name: str | None = None
    42→    for resolved_id in resolved_ids:
    43→        override = overrides.get(resolved_id)
    44→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
    45→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    46→            break
    47→    if not cluster_name or cluster_name not in clusters:
    48→        return ClusterContext(cluster_name=None, cluster_completed=False, cluster_remaining=0)
    49→    current_ids = set(clusters[cluster_name].get("issue_ids") or [])
    50→    remaining = current_ids - set(resolved_ids)
    51→    return ClusterContext(
    52→        cluster_name=cluster_name,
    53→        cluster_completed=len(remaining) == 0,
    54→        cluster_remaining=len(remaining),
    55→    )
    56→
    57→
    58→def update_living_plan_after_resolve(
    59→    *,
    60→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    61→    all_resolved: list[str],
    62→    attestation: str | None,
    63→    state_file: Path | str | None = None,
    64→) -> tuple[dict | None, ClusterContext]:
    65→    """Apply resolve side effects to the living plan when it exists."""
    66→    plan_path = plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file)) if state_file else None
    67→    plan = None
    68→    ctx = ClusterContext(cluster_name=None, cluster_completed=False, cluster_remaining=0)
    69→    try:
    70→        if not has_living_plan(plan_path):
    71→            return None, ctx
    72→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
    73→        ctx = capture_cluster_context(plan, all_resolved)
    74→        purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    75→        step_messages = auto_complete_steps(plan)
    76→        for msg in step_messages:
    77→            print(colorize(msg, "green"))
    78→        append_log_entry(
    79→            plan,
    80→            "resolve",
    81→            issue_ids=all_resolved,
    82→            actor="user",
    83→            note=getattr(args, "note", None),
    84→            detail={"status": args.status, "attestation": attestation},
    85→        )
    86→        if ctx.cluster_completed and ctx.cluster_name:
    87→            append_log_entry(
    88→                plan,
    89→                "cluster_done",
    90→                issue_ids=all_resolved,
    91→                cluster_name=ctx.cluster_name,
    92→                actor="user",
    93→            )
    94→        if args.status == "fixed":
    95→            add_uncommitted_issues(plan, all_resolved)
    96→        elif args.status == "open":
    97→            purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    98→        clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=all_resolved)
    99→        save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   100→        if purged:
   101→            print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
   102→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   103→        _logger.debug("plan update failed after resolve", exc_info=True)
   104→        warn_plan_load_degraded_once(
   105→            command_label="resolve",
   106→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
   107→            behavior="Living-plan queue metadata could not be updated after resolve.",
   108→        )
   109→    return plan, ctx
   110→
   111→
   112→__all__ = ["ClusterContext", "capture_cluster_context", "update_living_plan_after_resolve"]
   113→

1→"""Unified subjective-visibility policy.
     2→
     3→A single frozen dataclass computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``has_objective_items`` / ``objective_count`` computations in
     5→``stale_dimensions`` and ``auto_cluster``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    13→from desloppify.base.enums import Status
    14→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import planned_objective_ids as _planned_objective_ids
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import issue_in_scan_scope
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    19→
    20→# Detectors whose issues are NOT objective mechanical work.
    21→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    22→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    23→})
    24→
    25→
    26→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    27→class SubjectiveVisibility:
    28→    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    29→
    30→    has_objective_backlog: bool  # any planned open non-subjective issues?
    31→    objective_count: int  # how many (planned only, post-triage)
    32→    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing initial review
    33→    stale_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing re-review
    34→    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]  # below target, not stale/unscored
    35→
    36→    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    37→        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
    38→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    39→            return True  # unconditional
    40→        if fid in self.stale_ids:
    41→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    42→        if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    43→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    44→        return False
    45→
    46→    def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    47→        """Should this subjective ID be removed from plan queue_order?"""
    48→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    49→            return False  # never evict unscored
    50→        if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    51→            return self.has_objective_backlog
    52→        return False
    53→
    54→    @property
    55→    def backlog_blocks_rerun(self) -> bool:
    56→        """Preflight: should reruns be blocked?"""
    57→        return self.has_objective_backlog
    58→
    59→
    60→def _is_evidence_only(issue: dict) -> bool:
    61→    """Return True if the issue is below its detector's standalone threshold."""
    62→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    63→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    64→    if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
    65→        threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
    66→        issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
    67→        if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
    68→            return True
    69→    return False
    70→
    71→
    72→class _ScanPathFromStatePolicy:
    73→    """Sentinel type: resolve scan_path from state."""
    74→
    75→
    76→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY = _ScanPathFromStatePolicy()
    77→ScanPathPolicyOption = str | None | _ScanPathFromStatePolicy
    78→
    79→
    80→def compute_subjective_visibility(
    81→    state: StateModel,
    82→    *,
    83→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    84→    scan_path: ScanPathPolicyOption = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY,
    85→    plan: dict | None = None,
    86→) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    87→    """Build the policy snapshot from current state.
    88→
    89→    *scan_path* defaults to ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers don't need to
    90→    thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` to override, or ``None``
    91→    to disable scope filtering.  When *plan* is set, issues whose IDs
    92→    appear in ``plan["skipped"]`` are excluded.
    93→
    94→    Imports policy helpers from ``stale_policy`` so this module remains
    95→    side-effect free and cycle-safe.
    96→    """
    97→    from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import (
    98→        current_stale_ids,
    99→        current_under_target_ids,
   100→        current_unscored_ids,
   101→    )
   102→
   103→    resolved_scan_path: str | None = (
   104→        state.get("scan_path")
   105→        if isinstance(scan_path, _ScanPathFromStatePolicy)
   106→        else scan_path
   107→    )
   108→
   109→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   110→    skipped_ids = set((plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   111→
   112→    # Count open, non-suppressed, objective issues.
   113→    # Evidence-only issues (below standalone confidence threshold) are
   114→    # excluded — they still affect scores but are not actionable queue items.
   115→    # Issues outside scan_path and plan-skipped issues are also excluded
   116→    # so the policy matches what the user actually sees in the queue.
   117→    objective_issue_ids = [
   118→        issue_id
   119→        for issue_id, issue in issues.items()
   120→        if issue.get("status") == Status.OPEN
   121→        and issue.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   122→        and not issue.get("suppressed")
   123→        and not _is_evidence_only(issue)
   124→        and issue_in_scan_scope(str(issue.get("file", "")), resolved_scan_path)
   125→        and issue_id not in skipped_ids
   126→    ]
   127→
   128→    # When the plan has tracked objectives (post-triage), only count
   129→    # planned items — unplanned items are backlog, not blocking work.
   130→    objective_count = len(_planned_objective_ids(set(objective_issue_ids), plan))
   131→
   132→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   133→    stale = current_stale_ids(state)
   134→    under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   135→
   136→    return SubjectiveVisibility(
   137→        has_objective_backlog=objective_count > 0,
   138→        objective_count=objective_count,
   139→        unscored_ids=frozenset(unscored),
   140→        stale_ids=frozenset(stale),
   141→        under_target_ids=frozenset(under_target),
   142→    )
   143→
   144→
   145→__all__ = [
   146→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   147→    "SubjectiveVisibility",
   148→    "compute_subjective_visibility",
   149→]
   150→

   199→        return
