    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.snapshot import build_triage_snapshot
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_issues
    20→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    21→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import build_issue_items
    22→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import (
    23→    build_subjective_items,
    24→    build_triage_stage_items,
    25→)
    26→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic_workflow import (
    27→    build_communicate_score_item,
    28→    build_create_plan_item,
    29→    build_deferred_disposition_item,
    30→    build_import_scores_item,
    31→    build_run_scan_item,
    32→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    33→)
    34→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    35→
    36→PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL = "review_initial"
    37→PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    38→PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    39→PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT = "review_postflight"
    40→PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT = "workflow_postflight"
    41→PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT = "triage_postflight"
    42→
    43→
    44→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    45→class QueueSnapshot:
    46→    """Canonical queue facts and partitions for one invocation."""
    47→
    48→    phase: str
    49→    all_objective_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    50→    all_initial_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    51→    all_postflight_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    52→    all_scan_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    53→    all_postflight_workflow_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    54→    all_postflight_triage_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    55→    execution_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    56→    backlog_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    57→    objective_in_scope_count: int
    58→    planned_objective_count: int
    59→    objective_execution_count: int
    60→    objective_backlog_count: int
    61→    subjective_initial_count: int
    62→    subjective_postflight_count: int
    63→    workflow_postflight_count: int
    64→    triage_pending_count: int
    65→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers: bool
    66→
    67→
    68→def _option_value(options: object | None, name: str, default: Any) -> Any:
    69→    if options is None:
    70→        return default
    71→    return getattr(options, name, default)
    72→
    73→
    74→def _resolved_scan_path(options: object | None, state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    75→    scan_path = _option_value(options, "scan_path", state.get("scan_path"))
    76→    if hasattr(scan_path, "__class__") and scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState":
    77→        return state.get("scan_path")
    78→    return scan_path
    79→
    80→
    81→def _is_fresh_boundary(plan: dict | None) -> bool:
    82→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    83→        return True
    84→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    85→    if not scores:
    86→        return True
    87→    return isinstance(scores, dict) and bool(scores.get("reset"))
    88→
    89→
    90→def _is_objective_item(item: WorkQueueItem, *, skipped_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    91→    return (
    92→        item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
    93→        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    94→        and item.get("id", "") not in skipped_ids
    95→    )
    96→
    97→
    98→def _review_issue_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    99→    return [
   100→        item for item in items
   101→        if item.get("detector", "") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
   102→    ]
   103→
   104→
   105→def _executable_review_issue_items(
   106→    plan: dict | None,
   107→    state: StateModel,
   108→    review_issue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   109→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   110→    """Hide raw review findings until triage is current for the live issue set."""
   111→    if not review_issue_items or not isinstance(plan, dict):
   112→        return review_issue_items
   113→
   114→    triage_snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(plan, state)
   115→    if triage_snapshot.has_triage_in_queue:
   116→        return []
   117→    if triage_snapshot.is_triage_stale:
   118→        return []
   119→    if not triage_snapshot.triage_has_run:
   120→        return []
   121→    return review_issue_items
   122→
   123→
   124→def _subjective_partitions(
   125→    state: StateModel,
   126→    *,
   127→    scoped_issues: dict[str, dict],
   128→    threshold: float,
   129→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   130→    candidates = build_subjective_items(state, scoped_issues, threshold=threshold)
   131→    initial = [item for item in candidates if item.get("initial_review")]
   132→    postflight = [item for item in candidates if not item.get("initial_review")]
   133→    return initial, postflight
   134→
   135→
   136→def _workflow_partitions(
   137→    plan: dict | None,
   138→    state: StateModel,
   139→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   140→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   141→        return [], [], []
   142→    scan_items = [
   143→        item
   144→        for item in (
   145→            build_deferred_disposition_item(plan),
   146→            build_run_scan_item(plan),
   147→        )
   148→        if item is not None
   149→    ]
   150→    postflight_workflow = [
   151→        item
   152→        for item in (
   153→            build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state),
   154→            build_import_scores_item(plan, state),
   155→            build_communicate_score_item(plan, state),
   156→            build_create_plan_item(plan),
   157→        )
   158→        if item is not None
   159→    ]
   160→    triage_items = build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)
   161→    return scan_items, postflight_workflow, triage_items
   162→
   163→
   164→def _phase_for_snapshot(
   165→    *,
   166→    fresh_boundary: bool,
   167→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   168→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   169→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   170→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   171→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   172→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   173→) -> str:
   174→    if fresh_boundary and initial_review_items:
   175→        return PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   176→    if objective_items:
   177→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   178→    if scan_items:
   179→        return PHASE_SCAN
   180→    if postflight_review_items:
   181→        return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   182→    if postflight_workflow_items:
   183→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   184→    if triage_items:
   185→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   186→    return PHASE_SCAN
   187→
   188→
   189→def _execution_items_for_phase(
   190→    phase: str,
   191→    *,
   192→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   193→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   194→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   195→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   196→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   197→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   198→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   199→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL:
   200→        return initial_review_items
   201→    if phase == PHASE_EXECUTE:
   202→        return objective_items
   203→    if phase == PHASE_SCAN:
   204→        deferred_items = [
   205→            item for item in scan_items
   206→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID
   207→        ]
   208→        if deferred_items:
   209→            return deferred_items
   210→        return [
   211→            item for item in scan_items
   212→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
   213→        ]
   214→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   215→        return postflight_review_items
   216→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   217→        return postflight_workflow_items
   218→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   219→        return triage_items
   220→    return []
   221→
   222→
   223→def build_queue_snapshot(
   224→    state: StateModel,
   225→    *,
   226→    options: object | None = None,
   227→    plan: dict | None = None,
   228→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   229→) -> QueueSnapshot:
   230→    """Build the canonical queue snapshot for the current state."""
   231→    context = _option_value(options, "context", None)
   232→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else (
   233→        plan if plan is not None else _option_value(options, "plan", None)
   234→    )
   235→    scan_path = _resolved_scan_path(options, state)
   236→    skipped_ids = set((effective_plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   237→    scoped_issues = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   238→    scope = _option_value(options, "scope", None)
   239→    chronic = bool(_option_value(options, "chronic", False))
   240→
   241→    all_issue_items = build_issue_items(
   242→        state,
   243→        scan_path=scan_path,
   244→        status_filter="open",
   245→        scope=scope,
   246→        chronic=chronic,
   247→    )
   248→    objective_items = [
   249→        item for item in all_issue_items
   250→        if _is_objective_item(item, skipped_ids=skipped_ids)
   251→    ]
   252→    planned_ids = _planned_objective_ids(
   253→        {item.get("id", "") for item in objective_items},
   254→        effective_plan,
   255→    )
   256→    planned_objective_items = [
   257→        item for item in objective_items
   258→        if item.get("id", "") in planned_ids
   259→    ]
   260→    review_issue_items = _review_issue_items(all_issue_items)
   261→    executable_review_items = _executable_review_issue_items(
   262→        effective_plan,
   263→        state,
   264→        review_issue_items,
   265→    )
   266→    initial_review_items, subjective_postflight_items = _subjective_partitions(
   267→        state,
   268→        scoped_issues=scoped_issues,
   269→        threshold=target_strict,
   270→    )
   271→    postflight_review_items = [*subjective_postflight_items, *executable_review_items]
   272→    scan_items, postflight_workflow_items, triage_items = _workflow_partitions(
   273→        effective_plan,
   274→        state,
   275→    )
   276→
   277→    fresh_boundary = _is_fresh_boundary(effective_plan)
   278→    phase = _phase_for_snapshot(
   279→        fresh_boundary=fresh_boundary,
   280→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   281→        objective_items=planned_objective_items,
   282→        scan_items=scan_items,
   283→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   284→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   285→        triage_items=triage_items,
   286→    )
   287→    execution_items = _execution_items_for_phase(
   288→        phase,
   289→        objective_items=planned_objective_items,
   290→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   291→        scan_items=scan_items,
   292→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   293→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   294→        triage_items=triage_items,
   295→    )
   296→
   297→    execution_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in execution_items}
   298→    backlog_items = [
   299→        item for item in (
   300→            [
   301→                *planned_objective_items,
   302→                *initial_review_items,
   303→                *subjective_postflight_items,
   304→                *review_issue_items,
   305→                *scan_items,
   306→                *postflight_workflow_items,
   307→                *triage_items,
   308→            ]
   309→        )
   310→        if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   311→    ]
   312→    objective_backlog_count = sum(
   313→        1 for item in planned_objective_items if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   314→    )
   315→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers = len(planned_objective_items) < len(objective_items)
   316→
   317→    return QueueSnapshot(
   318→        phase=phase,
   319→        all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items),
   320→        all_initial_review_items=tuple(initial_review_items),
   321→        all_postflight_review_items=tuple(postflight_review_items),
   322→        all_scan_items=tuple(scan_items),
   323→        all_postflight_workflow_items=tuple(postflight_workflow_items),
   324→        all_postflight_triage_items=tuple(triage_items),
   325→        execution_items=tuple(execution_items),
   326→        backlog_items=tuple(backlog_items),
   327→        objective_in_scope_count=len(objective_items),
   328→        planned_objective_count=len(planned_objective_items),
   329→        objective_execution_count=sum(
   330→            1
   331→            for item in execution_items
   332→            if item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   333→            and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   334→        ),
   335→        objective_backlog_count=objective_backlog_count,
   336→        subjective_initial_count=len(initial_review_items),
   337→        subjective_postflight_count=len(postflight_review_items),
   338→        workflow_postflight_count=len(postflight_workflow_items),
   339→        triage_pending_count=len(triage_items),
   340→        has_unplanned_objective_blockers=has_unplanned_objective_blockers,
   341→    )
   342→
   343→
   344→def coarse_phase_name(phase: str) -> str:
   345→    """Map internal queue phases to the persisted coarse lifecycle value."""
   346→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL or phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   347→        return "review"
   348→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   349→        return "workflow"
   350→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   351→        return "triage"
   352→    return phase
   353→
   354→
   355→__all__ = [
   356→    "PHASE_EXECUTE",
   357→    "PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL",
   358→    "PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT",
   359→    "PHASE_SCAN",
   360→    "PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT",
   361→    "PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT",
   362→    "QueueSnapshot",
   363→    "build_queue_snapshot",
   364→    "coarse_phase_name",
   365→]
   366→

   119→    Observe writes verdict fields; reflect writes decision fields.
   120→    NOT derived state — ``plan["clusters"]`` and ``plan["skipped"]`` remain
   121→    authoritative for "what is actually true now."

   124→    # Observe writes (what did we find?):

   130→    # Reflect writes (what should we do about it?):

1→"""Stable app-facing workflow gate exports.
     2→
     3→This keeps app command code off the broader `workflow.py` module path while
     4→the engine sync package continues to own the implementation there.
     5→"""
     6→

1→"""Triage sync — inject/prune triage stage IDs based on review issue changes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
     7→    TRIAGE_IDS,
     8→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
     9→    QueueSyncResult,
    10→    confirmed_triage_stage_names,
    11→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix,
    12→    recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→
    18→from .defer_policy import (
    19→    DeferEscalationOptions,
    20→    DeferUpdateOptions,
    21→    should_escalate_defer_state,
    22→    update_defer_state,
    23→)
    24→from .triage_start_policy import decide_triage_start
    25→
    26→[REDACTED]
    27→_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD = "deferred_review_ids"
    28→[REDACTED]
    29→
    30→
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→# Helpers
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→
    35→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
    36→    state: StateModel,
    37→    meta: dict,
    38→) -> set[str]:
    39→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
    40→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    41→    active_ids = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
    42→    known_ids = triaged_ids | active_ids
    43→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state) - known_ids if known_ids else set()
    44→
    45→
    46→def _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta: dict) -> set[str]:
    47→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    48→    active_ids = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
    49→    return triaged_ids | active_ids
    50→
    51→
    52→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
    53→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
    54→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    55→        while sid in order:
    56→            order.remove(sid)
    57→
    58→
    59→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
    60→    order: list[str],
    61→    confirmed: set[str],
    62→    *,
    63→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    64→) -> list[str]:
    65→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
    66→
    67→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
    68→    Returns list of injected stage IDs.
    69→    """
    70→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
    71→    existing = set(order)
    72→    injected: list[str] = []
    73→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
    74→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
    75→            if skipped is not None:
    76→                skipped.pop(sid, None)
    77→            order.append(sid)
    78→            injected.append(sid)
    79→            existing.add(sid)
    80→    return injected
    81→
    82→
    83→def _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta: dict) -> None:
    84→    """Clear defer metadata once triage is no longer deferred."""
    85→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
    86→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
    87→
    88→
    89→def _store_triage_meta(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
    90→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
    91→
    92→
    93→def _inject_triage_result(
    94→    order: list[str],
    95→    confirmed: set[str],
    96→    *,
    97→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    98→    normalize: bool = False,
    99→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   100→    injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, confirmed, skipped=skipped)
   101→    if not injected:
   102→        return QueueSyncResult()
   103→    if normalize:
   104→        normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
   105→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=injected)
   106→
   107→
   108→def _mark_triage_deferred(
   109→    plan: PlanModel,
   110→    meta: dict,
   111→    *,
   112→    defer_state: dict,
   113→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   114→    meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   115→    meta["triage_recommended"] = True
   116→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
   117→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   118→    return QueueSyncResult(deferred=True)
   119→
   120→
   121→def _mark_triage_escalated(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   122→    meta[_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY] = True
   123→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   124→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   125→
   126→
   127→def _mark_triage_ready(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   128→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   129→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   130→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   131→
   132→
   133→def _prune_stale_present_stages(
   134→    *,
   135→    plan: PlanModel,
   136→    state: StateModel,
   137→    order: list[str],
   138→    meta: dict,
   139→    last_hash: str,
   140→    confirmed: set[str],
   141→    recorded_unconfirmed: set[str],
   142→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   143→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   144→    if not last_hash or confirmed or recorded_unconfirmed:
   145→        return result
   146→    if not _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta) and stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state):
   147→        return result
   148→    new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   149→    if new_since_triage:
   150→        return result
   151→    _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   152→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   153→    current_hash = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   154→    if current_hash:
   155→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   156→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   157→    result.pruned = list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
   158→    return result
   159→
   160→
   161→def _backfill_partial_triage_snapshot(
   162→    *,
   163→    plan: PlanModel,
   164→    state: StateModel,
   165→    meta: dict,
   166→    last_hash: str,
   167→) -> None:
   168→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   169→    has_completed_stage = any(
   170→        isinstance(v, dict) and v.get("confirmed_at")
   171→        for v in stages.values()
   172→    )
   173→    if not has_completed_stage or meta.get("triaged_ids") or last_hash:
   174→        return
   175→    current_review = sorted(stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state))
   176→    if current_review:
   177→        meta["triaged_ids"] = current_review
   178→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   179→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   180→
   181→
   182→def _defer_or_inject_triage(
   183→    *,
   184→    plan: PlanModel,
   185→    state: StateModel,
   186→    order: list[str],
   187→    meta: dict,
   188→    confirmed: set[str],
   189→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   190→    new_since_triage: set[str],
   191→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   192→    decision = decide_triage_start(
   193→        plan,
   194→        state,
   195→        policy=policy,
   196→        explicit_start=False,
   197→        attested_override=False,
   198→    )
   199→    if decision.action == "defer":
   200→        defer_state = update_defer_state(
   201→            meta.get(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY),
   202→            state=state,
   203→            deferred_ids=new_since_triage,
   204→            options=DeferUpdateOptions(
   205→                deferred_ids_field=_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   206→            ),
   207→        )
   208→        meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   209→        escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   210→            defer_state,
   211→            state=state,
   212→            options=DeferEscalationOptions(
   213→                deferred_ids_field=_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   214→            ),
   215→        )
   216→        if not escalated:
   217→            return _mark_triage_deferred(plan, meta, defer_state=defer_state)
   218→        _mark_triage_escalated(plan, meta)
   219→        return _inject_triage_result(
   220→            order,
   221→            confirmed,
   222→            skipped=plan.get("skipped", {}),
   223→            normalize=True,
   224→        )
   225→
   226→    _mark_triage_ready(plan, meta)
   227→    return _inject_triage_result(
   228→        order,
   229→        confirmed,
   230→        skipped=plan.get("skipped", {}),
   231→    )
   232→
   233→
   234→def _sync_hash_change(
   235→    *,
   236→    plan: PlanModel,
   237→    state: StateModel,
   238→    order: list[str],
   239→    meta: dict,
   240→    confirmed: set[str],
   241→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   242→    current_hash: str,
   243→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   244→    new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   245→    if not new_since_triage and not _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta):
   246→        new_since_triage = stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state)
   247→    if new_since_triage:
   248→        return _defer_or_inject_triage(
   249→            plan=plan,
   250→            state=state,
   251→            order=order,
   252→            meta=meta,
   253→            confirmed=confirmed,
   254→            policy=policy,
   255→            new_since_triage=new_since_triage,
   256→        )
   257→
   258→    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   259→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   260→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   261→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   262→    return QueueSyncResult()
   263→
   264→
   265→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   266→# Public API
   267→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   268→
   269→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   270→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   271→
   272→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   273→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   274→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   275→    resolved since then.
   276→
   277→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   278→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   279→    through the plan.
   280→    """
   281→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   282→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state)
   283→
   284→
   285→def compute_open_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   286→    """Return the set of currently-open review/concerns issue IDs."""
   287→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state)
   288→
   289→
   290→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   291→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   292→
   293→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   294→    """
   295→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   296→
   297→
   298→def sync_triage_needed(
   299→    plan: PlanModel,
   300→    state: StateModel,
   301→    *,
   302→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   303→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   304→    """Append triage stage IDs to back of queue when review issues change.
   305→
   306→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   307→
   308→    **Mid-cycle guard**: when the objective backlog still has work, triage
   309→    stages are NOT injected.  Instead, ``epic_triage_meta["triage_recommended"]``
   310→    is set so the UI can show a non-blocking banner.  Stages are injected
   311→    once the objective backlog drains (or on manual ``plan triage``). If
   312→    deferral repeats for multiple scans/days, triage escalates and stages are
   313→    injected in workflow/triage priority order with a forced-visibility marker.
   314→
   315→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   316→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   317→
   318→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   319→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   320→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   321→    """
   322→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   323→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   324→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   325→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   326→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   327→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   328→
   329→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   330→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   331→
   332→    current_hash = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   333→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   334→
   335→    if already_present:
   336→        return _prune_stale_present_stages(
   337→            plan=plan,
   338→            state=state,
   339→            order=order,
   340→            meta=meta,
   341→            last_hash=last_hash,
   342→            confirmed=confirmed,
   343→            recorded_unconfirmed=recorded_unconfirmed,
   344→        )
   345→
   346→    _backfill_partial_triage_snapshot(
   347→        plan=plan,
   348→        state=state,
   349→        meta=meta,
   350→        last_hash=last_hash,
   351→    )
   352→
   353→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   354→        return _sync_hash_change(
   355→            plan=plan,
   356→            state=state,
   357→            order=order,
   358→            meta=meta,
   359→            confirmed=confirmed,
   360→            policy=policy,
   361→            current_hash=current_hash,
   362→        )
   363→
   364→    return result
   365→
   366→
   367→__all__ = [
   368→    "compute_open_issue_ids",
   369→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   370→    "is_triage_stale",
   371→    "sync_triage_needed",
   372→]
   373→

1→"""Plan sync helpers for review-import flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.triage import (
    10→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    11→    compute_open_issue_ids,
    12→    sync_triage_needed,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass
    18→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
    19→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
    20→
    21→    new_ids: set[str]
    22→    added_to_queue: list[str]
    23→    triage_injected: bool
    24→    stale_pruned_from_queue: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    25→    triage_injected_ids: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    26→    triage_deferred: bool = False
    27→
    28→
    29→def _has_triage_baseline(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    30→    """Return True when triage has recorded at least one baseline issue ID."""
    31→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    32→    triaged_ids = meta.get("triaged_ids", [])
    33→    return bool(triaged_ids)
    34→
    35→
    36→def _review_issue_ids_for_import_sync(
    37→    plan: PlanModel,
    38→    state: StateModel,
    39→    *,
    40→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    41→) -> set[str]:
    42→    """Return review IDs that should be synced into queue_order after import.
    43→
    44→    No triage baseline yet: include all currently-open review IDs so the
    45→    first import cannot drop follow-up work.
    46→    Existing triage baseline: include only IDs that are new since triage.
    47→    """
    48→    if _has_triage_baseline(plan):
    49→        return compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
    50→    return set(open_review_ids) if open_review_ids is not None else compute_open_issue_ids(state)
    51→
    52→
    53→def _is_review_queue_id(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    54→    """Return True for queue IDs representing review/concerns issues."""
    55→    return issue_id.startswith("review::") or issue_id.startswith("concerns::")
    56→
    57→
    58→def _prune_stale_triage_meta(
    59→    plan: PlanModel,
    60→    *,
    61→    stale_ids: set[str],
    62→) -> None:
    63→    """Drop stale review IDs from triage recovery metadata."""
    64→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta")
    65→    if not isinstance(meta, dict) or not stale_ids:
    66→        return
    67→
    68→    active_ids = meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids")
    69→    if isinstance(active_ids, list):
    70→        filtered_active = [
    71→            issue_id for issue_id in active_ids
    72→            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id not in stale_ids
    73→        ]
    74→        if filtered_active:
    75→            meta["active_triage_issue_ids"] = filtered_active
    76→        else:
    77→            meta.pop("active_triage_issue_ids", None)
    78→
    79→    undispositioned_ids = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_ids")
    80→    if isinstance(undispositioned_ids, list):
    81→        filtered_undispositioned = [
    82→            issue_id for issue_id in undispositioned_ids
    83→            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id not in stale_ids
    84→        ]
    85→        if filtered_undispositioned:
    86→            meta["undispositioned_issue_ids"] = filtered_undispositioned
    87→            meta["undispositioned_issue_count"] = len(filtered_undispositioned)
    88→        else:
    89→            meta.pop("undispositioned_issue_ids", None)
    90→            meta.pop("undispositioned_issue_count", None)
    91→
    92→
    93→def _prune_stale_review_ids_from_plan(
    94→    plan: PlanModel,
    95→    *,
    96→    live_open_review_ids: set[str],
    97→) -> list[str]:
    98→    """Remove stale review IDs from queue-related plan containers.
    99→
   100→    Review imports can auto-resolve prior review IDs. These IDs must be removed
   101→    from queue_order (and linked queue metadata) or the queue can drift from
   102→    state reality.
   103→    """
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→    stale_ids = sorted(
   106→        {
   107→            issue_id
   108→            for issue_id in order
   109→            if _is_review_queue_id(issue_id) and issue_id not in live_open_review_ids
   110→        }
   111→    )
   112→    if not stale_ids:
   113→        return []
   114→
   115→    stale_set = set(stale_ids)
   116→    order[:] = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in stale_set]
   117→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, stale_set)
   118→    _prune_stale_triage_meta(plan, stale_ids=stale_set)
   119→
   120→    deferred = plan.get("deferred")
   121→    if isinstance(deferred, list):
   122→        plan["deferred"] = [issue_id for issue_id in deferred if issue_id not in stale_set]
   123→
   124→    skipped = plan.get("skipped")
   125→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
   126→        for issue_id in stale_set:
   127→            skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   128→
   129→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   130→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids")
   131→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   132→            continue
   133→        cluster["issue_ids"] = [
   134→            issue_id for issue_id in issue_ids if issue_id not in stale_set
   135→        ]
   136→
   137→    return stale_ids
   138→
   139→
   140→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   141→    plan: PlanModel,
   142→    state: StateModel,
   143→    *,
   144→    policy=None,
   145→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   146→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   147→
   148→    Appends new issue IDs to queue_order, prunes stale review IDs from queue
   149→    containers, and injects triage stages if needed (respects mid-cycle guard
   150→    — defers when objective work remains). Returns ``None`` when no queue
   151→    changes are required.
   152→    """
   153→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   154→    open_review_ids = compute_open_issue_ids(state)
   155→    stale_pruned_from_queue = _prune_stale_review_ids_from_plan(
   156→        plan,
   157→        live_open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   158→    )
   159→    new_ids = _review_issue_ids_for_import_sync(
   160→        plan,
   161→        state,
   162→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   163→    )
   164→    if not new_ids and not stale_pruned_from_queue:
   165→        return None
   166→
   167→    # Add new issue IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   168→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   169→    existing = set(order)
   170→    added: list[str] = []
   171→    for issue_id in sorted(new_ids):
   172→        if issue_id not in existing:
   173→            order.append(issue_id)
   174→            added.append(issue_id)
   175→
   176→    # Inject triage stages if needed (policy enables mid-cycle guard)
   177→    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   178→    triage_injected_ids = list(getattr(triage_result, "injected", []) or [])
   179→    triage_injected = bool(triage_injected_ids)
   180→    triage_deferred = bool(triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "deferred", False))
   181→
   182→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   183→        new_ids=new_ids,
   184→        added_to_queue=added,

    81→def _triage_progression_target(plan: dict) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:

    83→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})

    85→    for stage, _sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:

    87→        if isinstance(stage_payload, dict) and stage_payload and not stage_payload.get("confirmed_at"):
    88→            return stage, True

    94→        return stage, False
    95→

   223→    # Use previous_plan_start_scores (stashed at rebaseline) for the delta

   250→
   251→    item = _workflow_action_item(

   254→        detail={

   263→def _temporary_skipped_ids(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   264→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   265→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
   266→        return []
   267→    deferred: list[str] = []
   268→    for issue_id, entry in skipped.items():
   269→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   270→            continue
   271→        if str(entry.get("kind", "temporary")) == "temporary":
   272→            deferred.append(str(issue_id))
   273→    deferred.sort()
   274→    return deferred
   275→
   276→
   277→def _deferred_cluster_breakdown(
   278→    plan: dict,
   279→    deferred_ids: list[str],
   280→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   281→    deferred_set = set(deferred_ids)
   282→    if not deferred_set:
   283→        return 0, 0
   284→
   285→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   286→    if not isinstance(clusters, dict):
   287→        return 0, len(deferred_ids)
   288→
   289→    covered_by_cluster: set[str] = set()
   290→    cluster_count = 0
   291→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   292→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   293→            continue
   294→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   295→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   296→            continue
   297→        matched = {str(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids if str(issue_id) in deferred_set}
   298→        if not matched:
   299→            continue
   300→        cluster_count += 1
   301→        covered_by_cluster.update(matched)
   302→
   303→    individual_count = len(deferred_set - covered_by_cluster)
   304→    return cluster_count, individual_count
   305→
   306→
   307→def build_deferred_disposition_item(plan: dict) -> WorkflowActionItem | None:
   308→    """Build a synthetic item prompting deferred backlog disposition."""
   309→    deferred_ids = _temporary_skipped_ids(plan)
   310→    if not deferred_ids:
   311→        return None
   312→
   313→    count = len(deferred_ids)
   314→    cluster_count, individual_count = _deferred_cluster_breakdown(plan, deferred_ids)
   315→    cluster_label = "cluster" if cluster_count == 1 else "clusters"
   316→    individual_label = "item" if individual_count == 1 else "items"
   317→    reactivate_cmd = 'desloppify plan unskip "*"'
   318→    subset_reactivate_cmd = "desloppify plan unskip <cluster-or-id>"
   319→    inspect_cmd = "desloppify plan queue --include-skipped"
   320→    inspect_item_cmd = "desloppify show <cluster-or-id>"
   321→    backlog_cmd = 'desloppify plan backlog "*"'
   322→    subset_backlog_cmd = "desloppify plan backlog <cluster-or-id>"
   323→    wontfix_cmd = (
   324→        'desloppify plan skip --permanent "*" '
   325→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   326→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently." '
   327→        "--confirm"
   328→    )
   329→    subset_wontfix_cmd = (
   330→        "desloppify plan skip --permanent <cluster-or-id> "
   331→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   332→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently."'
   333→    )
   334→
   335→    item = _workflow_action_item(
   336→        item_id=WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
   337→        summary=(
   338→            "Deferred backlog decision required: "
   339→            f"{cluster_count} {cluster_label} + {individual_count} individual {individual_label} "
   340→            "must be reactivated, moved to backlog, or marked wontfix."
   341→        ),
   342→        detail={
   343→            "temporary_skipped_count": count,
   344→            "deferred_cluster_count": cluster_count,
   345→            "deferred_individual_count": individual_count,
   346→            "reactivate_command": reactivate_cmd,
   347→            "backlog_command": backlog_cmd,
   348→            "wontfix_command": wontfix_cmd,
   349→            "planning_tools": [
   350→                {
   351→                    "label": "Review deferred backlog",
   352→                    "command": inspect_cmd,
   353→                },
   354→                {
   355→                    "label": "Inspect a specific cluster or item",
   356→                    "command": inspect_item_cmd,
   357→                },
   358→                {
   359→                    "label": "Reactivate a subset",
   360→                    "command": subset_reactivate_cmd,
   361→                },
   362→                {
   363→                    "label": "Move a subset to backlog",
   364→                    "command": subset_backlog_cmd,
   365→                },
   366→                {
   367→                    "label": "Mark a subset as permanent wontfix",
   368→                    "command": subset_wontfix_cmd,
   369→                },
   370→            ],
   371→            "decision_options": [
   372→                {
   373→                    "label": "Reactivate deferred work",
   374→                    "command": reactivate_cmd,
   375→                },
   376→                {
   377→                    "label": "Move deferred work to backlog",
   378→                    "command": backlog_cmd,
   379→                },
   380→                {
   381→                    "label": "Convert deferred work to wontfix",
   382→                    "command": wontfix_cmd,
   383→                },
   384→            ],
   385→        },
   386→        primary_command=reactivate_cmd,
   387→    )
   388→    item["execution_visibility"] = "always"

1→"""Helpers for the persisted queue lifecycle phase.
     2→
     3→The persisted phase is the normal source of truth for CLI/debugging, but it is
     4→not trusted blindly. Queue assembly still re-resolves the active phase from the
     5→currently visible items as a safety net so stale saved state cannot strand the
     6→user in the wrong phase after out-of-band changes.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from typing import Iterable
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→
    16→[REDACTED]
    17→_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY = "lifecycle_phase"
    18→
    19→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    20→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW = "review"
    21→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW = "workflow"
    22→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE = "triage"
    23→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    24→VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES = frozenset(
    25→    {
    26→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    27→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    28→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    29→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    30→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    31→    }
    32→)
    33→
    34→
    35→def _refresh_state(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, object]:
    36→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    37→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    38→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    39→        refresh_state = {}
    40→        plan["refresh_state"] = refresh_state
    41→    return refresh_state
    42→
    43→
    44→def _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    45→    return not any(str(issue_id).startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
    46→
    47→
    48→def current_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel) -> str | None:
    49→    """Return the persisted lifecycle phase, falling back for legacy plans."""
    50→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    51→    if isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    52→        phase = refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY)
    53→        if isinstance(phase, str) and phase in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    54→            return phase
    55→    if postflight_scan_pending(plan):
    56→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
    57→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    58→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
    59→    return None
    60→
    61→
    62→def set_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel, phase: str) -> bool:
    63→    """Persist the current queue lifecycle phase."""
    64→    if phase not in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    65→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported lifecycle phase: {phase}")
    66→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
    67→    if refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY) == phase:
    68→        return False
    69→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = phase
    70→    return True
    71→
    72→
    73→def sync_lifecycle_phase(
    74→    plan: PlanModel,
    75→    *,
    76→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
    77→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
    78→    has_postflight_review: bool,
    79→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
    80→    has_triage: bool,
    81→    has_deferred: bool,
    82→) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    83→    """Resolve and persist the current lifecycle phase from queue-state facts."""
    84→    phase = resolve_lifecycle_phase(
    85→        plan,
    86→        has_initial_reviews=has_initial_reviews,
    87→        has_objective_backlog=has_objective_backlog,
    88→        has_postflight_review=has_postflight_review,
    89→        has_postflight_workflow=has_postflight_workflow,
    90→        has_triage=has_triage,
    91→        has_deferred=has_deferred,
    92→    )
    93→    return phase, set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
    94→
    95→
    96→def resolve_lifecycle_phase(
    97→    plan: PlanModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
   100→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
   101→    has_postflight_review: bool,
   102→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
   103→    has_triage: bool,
   104→    has_deferred: bool,
   105→) -> str:
   106→    """Resolve the lifecycle phase from explicit queue-state facts."""
   107→    if has_initial_reviews:
   108→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   109→    if has_objective_backlog:
   110→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   111→    if has_deferred or postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   112→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   113→    if has_postflight_review:
   114→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   115→    if has_postflight_workflow:
   116→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW
   117→    if has_triage:
   118→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE
   119→    persisted = current_lifecycle_phase(plan)
   120→    if persisted is not None:
   121→        return persisted
   122→    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   123→
   124→
   125→def postflight_scan_pending(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
   126→    """Return True when the current empty-queue boundary still needs a scan."""
   127→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
   128→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
   129→        return True
   130→    return not isinstance(refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY), int)
   131→
   132→
   133→def mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   134→    plan: PlanModel,
   135→    *,
   136→    scan_count: int | None,
   137→) -> bool:
   138→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current refresh cycle."""
   139→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   140→    try:
   141→        normalized_scan_count = int(scan_count or 0)
   142→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   143→        normalized_scan_count = 0
   144→    if refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY) == normalized_scan_count:
   145→        return False
   146→    refresh_state[_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY] = normalized_scan_count
   147→    return True
   148→
   149→
   150→def clear_postflight_scan_completion(
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    *,
   153→    issue_ids: Iterable[str] | None = None,
   154→) -> bool:
   155→    """Require a fresh scan after queue-changing work on real issues."""
   156→    if issue_ids is not None and not any(
   157→        _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids
   158→    ):
   159→        return False
   160→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   161→    if _POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY not in refresh_state:
   162→        return False
   163→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   164→    refresh_state.pop(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY, None)
   165→    return True
   166→
   167→
   168→__all__ = [
   169→    "clear_postflight_scan_completion",
   170→    "current_lifecycle_phase",
   171→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE",

    54→        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
    55→            return None
    56→        meta = cls(
    57→            timestamp=str(raw.get("timestamp", "")).strip(),
    58→            import_file=str(raw.get("import_file", "")).strip(),
    59→            normalized_import_file=str(raw.get("normalized_import_file", "")).strip(),

    79→def _latest_assessment_audit(

    81→    *,
    82→    modes: set[str],

    84→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    85→    if not isinstance(audit, list):
    86→        return None

   117→    return PendingImportScoresMeta(
   118→        timestamp=timestamp,
   119→        import_file=recorded_file,
   120→        normalized_import_file=_normalize_match_path(recorded_file) or "",
   121→        packet_sha256=str(provenance.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip(),
   122→    )

   125→def pending_import_scores_meta(
   126→    plan: PlanModel,
   127→    state: StateModel,
   128→) -> PendingImportScoresMeta | None:
   129→    """Return queued score-import metadata without mutating the plan."""
   130→    refresh_state = _get_refresh_state(plan)
   131→    if refresh_state is not None:
   132→        meta = PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(
   133→            refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   134→        )
   135→        if meta is not None:
   136→            return meta
   137→    issues_only_audit = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   138→    if issues_only_audit is None:
   139→        return None
   140→    return _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
   141→        import_file=str(issues_only_audit.get("import_file", "")),
   142→        import_payload=None,
   143→        issues_only_audit=issues_only_audit,
   144→    )

   146→
   147→def import_scores_meta_matches(
   148→    meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | dict[str, Any] | None,
   149→    *,
   150→    import_file: str,
   151→    import_payload: dict[str, Any],
   152→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   153→    """Return (matches, reason) for whether the import matches the pending batch.
   154→
   155→    Checks packet_sha256 first (strongest signal), falls back to normalized
   156→    file path.  Returns a single human-readable reason on mismatch.
   157→    """
   158→    normalized_meta = (
   159→        meta
   160→        if isinstance(meta, PendingImportScoresMeta)
   161→        else PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(meta)
   162→    )
   163→    if normalized_meta is None:
   164→        return True, ""
   165→
   166→    provenance = import_payload.get("provenance")
   167→    provenance_dict = provenance if isinstance(provenance, dict) else {}
   168→
   169→    expected_hash = normalized_meta.packet_sha256
   170→    current_hash = str(provenance_dict.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip()
   171→    if expected_hash and current_hash:
   172→        if current_hash == expected_hash:
   173→            return True, ""
   174→        return False, f"expected packet_sha256 {expected_hash}, got {current_hash}"
   175→
   176→    expected_file = normalized_meta.normalized_import_file
   177→    current_file = _normalize_match_path(import_file) or ""
   178→    if expected_file and current_file and current_file != expected_file:
   179→        return False, f"expected import file {normalized_meta.import_file}, got {import_file}"
   180→
   181→    return True, ""
   182→

   184→def _clear_pending_import_scores(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   185→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   186→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   187→        order[:] = [item for item in order if item != WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID]
   188→    refresh_state = _get_refresh_state(plan)
   189→    if refresh_state is not None:
   190→        refresh_state.pop(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY, None)
   191→
   192→
   193→def _pending_compare_timestamp(
   194→    pending_meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | None,
   195→    latest_issues_only: dict[str, Any],
   196→) -> str:
   197→    if pending_meta is not None and pending_meta.timestamp:
   198→        return pending_meta.timestamp
   199→    return str(latest_issues_only.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   200→

   202→def _pending_import_scores_stale(
   203→    *,
   204→    order: list[str],
   205→    pending_meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | None,
   206→    latest_issues_only: dict[str, Any] | None,
   207→    latest_trusted: dict[str, Any] | None,
   208→) -> bool:
   209→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in order:
   210→        return False
   211→    if latest_issues_only is None:
   212→        return True
   213→    if latest_trusted is None:
   214→        return False
   215→
   216→    latest_trusted_ts = str(latest_trusted.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   217→    compare_ts = _pending_compare_timestamp(pending_meta, latest_issues_only)
   218→    return bool(compare_ts and latest_trusted_ts and latest_trusted_ts >= compare_ts)

   229→        import_file=import_file,

   243→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,

   247→def _inject(plan: PlanModel, item_id: str) -> QueueSyncResult:
   248→    """Inject *item_id* into the workflow prefix and clear stale skip entries."""
   249→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   250→    if item_id not in order:
   251→        order.append(item_id)
   252→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
   253→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   254→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
   255→        skipped.pop(item_id, None)
   256→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=[item_id])
   257→
   258→
   259→_EMPTY = QueueSyncResult
   260→
   261→
   262→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   263→    plan: PlanModel,
   264→    state: StateModel,
   265→    *,
   266→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   267→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   268→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   269→
   270→    Injects when:
   271→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   272→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   273→
   274→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   275→    """
   276→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   277→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   278→
   279→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   280→        return _EMPTY()
   281→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   282→        return _EMPTY()
   283→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   284→
   285→
   286→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   287→    plan: PlanModel,
   288→    state: StateModel,
   289→    *,
   290→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   291→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   292→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   293→
   294→    Only injects when:
   295→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   296→    - At least one objective issue exists
   297→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   298→    - No triage stages are pending
   299→
   300→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   301→    """
   302→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   303→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   304→
   305→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   306→        return _EMPTY()
   307→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   308→        return _EMPTY()
   309→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   310→        return _EMPTY()
   311→
   312→    if not has_objective_backlog(state, policy):
   313→        return _EMPTY()
   314→
   315→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   316→
   317→
   318→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   319→    plan: PlanModel,
   320→    state: StateModel,
   321→    *,
   322→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   323→    import_file: str | None = None,
   324→    import_payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   325→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   326→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   327→
   328→    Only injects when:
   329→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   330→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   331→
   332→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   333→    """
   334→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   335→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   336→    refresh_state = _ensure_refresh_state(plan)
   337→    pending_meta = PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(
   338→        refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   339→    )
   340→    latest_issues_only = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   341→    latest_trusted = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes=_TRUSTED_ASSESSMENT_MODES)
   342→
   343→    if _pending_import_scores_stale(
   344→        order=order,
   345→        pending_meta=pending_meta,
   346→        latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   347→        latest_trusted=latest_trusted,
   348→    ):
   349→        _clear_pending_import_scores(plan)
   350→        return QueueSyncResult(pruned=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   351→
   352→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   353→        if assessment_mode == "issues_only":
   354→            _record_pending_import_scores(
   355→                refresh_state,
   356→                import_file=import_file,
   357→                import_payload=import_payload,
   358→                latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   359→            )
   360→            return QueueSyncResult(resurfaced=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   361→        return _EMPTY()
   362→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   363→        return _EMPTY()
   364→    result = _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   365→    _record_pending_import_scores(
   366→        refresh_state,
   367→        import_file=import_file,
   368→        import_payload=import_payload,
   369→        latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   370→    )
   371→    return result
   372→
   373→
   374→class ScoreSnapshot:
   375→    """Minimal score snapshot for rebaseline — avoids importing state.py."""
   376→
   377→    __slots__ = ("strict", "overall", "objective", "verified")
   378→
   379→    def __init__(
   380→        self,
   381→        *,
   382→        strict: float | None,
   383→        overall: float | None,
   384→        objective: float | None,
   385→        verified: float | None,
   386→    ) -> None:
   387→        self.strict = strict
   388→        self.overall = overall
   389→        self.objective = objective
   390→        self.verified = verified
   391→
   392→
   393→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   394→    plan: PlanModel,
   395→    state: StateModel,
   396→    *,
   397→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   398→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   399→    current_scores: ScoreSnapshot | None = None,
   400→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   401→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` and rebaseline scores.
   402→
   403→    Injects when:
   404→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dims), OR
   405→      scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   406→    - ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue
   407→    - Score has not already been communicated this cycle
   408→      (``previous_plan_start_scores`` absent), unless a trusted score import
   409→      explicitly refreshed the live score mid-cycle
   410→
   411→    When injected and *current_scores* is provided, ``plan_start_scores``
   412→    is rebaselined to the current score so the score display unfreezes at
   413→    the new value.  The previous baseline is preserved in
   414→    ``previous_plan_start_scores`` so the communicate-score queue item can
   415→    show the old → new delta — and so mid-cycle scans know not to
   416→    re-inject.
   417→    """
   418→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   419→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   420→
   421→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order:
   422→        return _EMPTY()
   423→    # Already communicated this cycle — previous_plan_start_scores is set
   424→    # at injection time and cleared at cycle boundaries.
   425→    if "previous_plan_start_scores" in plan and not scores_just_imported:
   426→        return _EMPTY()
   427→    if not scores_just_imported and not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   428→        return _EMPTY()
   429→
   430→    if current_scores is not None:
   431→        _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(plan, current_scores)
   432→    # Set sentinel even when rebaseline was a no-op (no plan_start_scores
   433→    # to rebaseline) so mid-cycle scans don't re-inject.
   434→    if not plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores"):
   435→        plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = {}
   436→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   437→
   438→
   439→def _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(
   440→    plan: PlanModel,
   441→    scores: ScoreSnapshot,
   442→) -> None:
   443→    """Snapshot the current score as the new baseline, preserving the old one."""
   444→    old_start = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   445→    if not isinstance(old_start, dict) or not old_start:
   446→        return
   447→    if scores.strict is None:
   448→        return
   449→
   450→    plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = dict(old_start)
   451→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   452→        "strict": scores.strict,
   453→        "overall": scores.overall,
   454→        "objective": scores.objective,
   455→        "verified": scores.verified,
   456→    }
   457→
   458→
   459→__all__ = [
   460→    "PendingImportScoresMeta",
   461→    "ScoreSnapshot",
   462→    "import_scores_meta_matches",
   463→    "pending_import_scores_meta",
   464→    "sync_communicate_score_needed",
   465→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   466→    "sync_import_scores_needed",
   467→    "sync_score_checkpoint_needed",
   468→]
   469→

    45→    """Normalized contract for the queued score-import workflow."""
    46→
    47→    timestamp: str = ""
    48→    import_file: str = ""
    49→    normalized_import_file: str = ""
    50→    packet_sha256: str = ""
    51→
    52→    @classmethod
    53→    def from_mapping(cls, raw: object) -> PendingImportScoresMeta | None:
    54→        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
    55→            return None
    56→        meta = cls(
    57→            timestamp=str(raw.get("timestamp", "")).strip(),
    58→            import_file=str(raw.get("import_file", "")).strip(),
    59→            normalized_import_file=str(raw.get("normalized_import_file", "")).strip(),
    60→            packet_sha256=str(raw.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip(),
    61→        )
    62→        return meta if any((meta.timestamp, meta.import_file, meta.packet_sha256)) else None
    63→
    64→    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, str]:
    65→        return {
    66→            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
    67→            "import_file": self.import_file,
    68→            "normalized_import_file": self.normalized_import_file,
    69→            "packet_sha256": self.packet_sha256,
    70→        }
    71→
    72→
    73→def _normalize_match_path(raw_path: object) -> str | None:
    74→    if not isinstance(raw_path, str) or not raw_path.strip():
    75→        return None
    76→    return str(Path(raw_path).expanduser().resolve(strict=False))
    77→
    78→
    79→def _latest_assessment_audit(
    80→    state: StateModel,
    81→    *,
    82→    modes: set[str],
    83→) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    84→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    85→    if not isinstance(audit, list):
    86→        return None
    87→    for entry in reversed(audit):
    88→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    89→            continue
    90→        mode = entry.get("mode")
    91→        if isinstance(mode, str) and mode in modes:
    92→            return entry
    93→    return None
    94→
    95→
    96→def _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
    97→    *,
    98→    import_file: str | None,
    99→    import_payload: dict[str, Any] | None,
   100→    issues_only_audit: dict[str, Any] | None,
   101→) -> PendingImportScoresMeta:
   102→    provenance = {}
   103→    if isinstance(import_payload, dict):
   104→        raw_provenance = import_payload.get("provenance")
   105→        if isinstance(raw_provenance, dict):
   106→            provenance = raw_provenance
   107→    recorded_file = (
   108→        str(import_file).strip()
   109→        if isinstance(import_file, str) and import_file.strip()
   110→        else str(issues_only_audit.get("import_file", "")).strip()
   111→        if isinstance(issues_only_audit, dict)
   112→        else ""
   113→    )
   114→    timestamp = ""
   115→    if isinstance(issues_only_audit, dict):
   116→        timestamp = str(issues_only_audit.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   117→    return PendingImportScoresMeta(
   118→        timestamp=timestamp,
   119→        import_file=recorded_file,
   120→        normalized_import_file=_normalize_match_path(recorded_file) or "",
   121→        packet_sha256=str(provenance.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip(),
   122→    )
   123→
   124→
   125→def pending_import_scores_meta(
   126→    plan: PlanModel,
   127→    state: StateModel,
   128→) -> PendingImportScoresMeta | None:
   129→    """Return queued score-import metadata without mutating the plan."""
   130→    refresh_state = _get_refresh_state(plan)
   131→    if refresh_state is not None:
   132→        meta = PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(
   133→            refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   134→        )
   135→        if meta is not None:
   136→            return meta
   137→    issues_only_audit = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   138→    if issues_only_audit is None:
   139→        return None
   140→    return _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
   141→        import_file=str(issues_only_audit.get("import_file", "")),
   142→        import_payload=None,
   143→        issues_only_audit=issues_only_audit,
   144→    )
   145→
   146→
   147→def import_scores_meta_matches(
   148→    meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | dict[str, Any] | None,
   149→    *,
   150→    import_file: str,
   151→    import_payload: dict[str, Any],
   152→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   153→    """Return (matches, reason) for whether the import matches the pending batch.
   154→
   155→    Checks packet_sha256 first (strongest signal), falls back to normalized
   156→    file path.  Returns a single human-readable reason on mismatch.
   157→    """
   158→    normalized_meta = (
   159→        meta
   160→        if isinstance(meta, PendingImportScoresMeta)
   161→        else PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(meta)
   162→    )
   163→    if normalized_meta is None:
   164→        return True, ""
   165→
   166→    provenance = import_payload.get("provenance")
   167→    provenance_dict = provenance if isinstance(provenance, dict) else {}
   168→
   169→    expected_hash = normalized_meta.packet_sha256
   170→    current_hash = str(provenance_dict.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip()
   171→    if expected_hash and current_hash:
   172→        if current_hash == expected_hash:
   173→            return True, ""
   174→        return False, f"expected packet_sha256 {expected_hash}, got {current_hash}"
   175→
   176→    expected_file = normalized_meta.normalized_import_file
   177→    current_file = _normalize_match_path(import_file) or ""
   178→    if expected_file and current_file and current_file != expected_file:
   179→        return False, f"expected import file {normalized_meta.import_file}, got {import_file}"
   180→
   181→    return True, ""
   182→
   183→
   184→def _clear_pending_import_scores(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   185→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   186→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   187→        order[:] = [item for item in order if item != WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID]
   188→    refresh_state = _get_refresh_state(plan)
   189→    if refresh_state is not None:
   190→        refresh_state.pop(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY, None)
   191→
   192→
   193→def _pending_compare_timestamp(
   194→    pending_meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | None,
   195→    latest_issues_only: dict[str, Any],
   196→) -> str:
   197→    if pending_meta is not None and pending_meta.timestamp:
   198→        return pending_meta.timestamp
   199→    return str(latest_issues_only.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   200→
   201→
   202→def _pending_import_scores_stale(
   203→    *,
   204→    order: list[str],
   205→    pending_meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | None,
   206→    latest_issues_only: dict[str, Any] | None,
   207→    latest_trusted: dict[str, Any] | None,
   208→) -> bool:
   209→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in order:
   210→        return False
   211→    if latest_issues_only is None:
   212→        return True
   213→    if latest_trusted is None:
   214→        return False
   215→
   216→    latest_trusted_ts = str(latest_trusted.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   217→    compare_ts = _pending_compare_timestamp(pending_meta, latest_issues_only)
   218→    return bool(compare_ts and latest_trusted_ts and latest_trusted_ts >= compare_ts)
   219→
   220→
   221→def _record_pending_import_scores(
   222→    refresh_state: dict[str, Any],
   223→    *,
   224→    import_file: str | None,
   225→    import_payload: dict[str, Any] | None,
   226→    latest_issues_only: dict[str, Any] | None,
   227→) -> None:
   228→    refresh_state[_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY] = _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
   229→        import_file=import_file,
   230→        import_payload=import_payload,
   231→        issues_only_audit=latest_issues_only,
   232→    ).to_dict()
   233→
   234→
   235→def _no_unscored(
   236→    state: StateModel,
   237→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   238→) -> bool:
   239→    """Return True when no unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain."""
   240→    if policy is not None:
   241→        return not policy.unscored_ids
   242→    return not stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
   243→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   244→    )
   245→
   246→
   247→def _inject(plan: PlanModel, item_id: str) -> QueueSyncResult:
   248→    """Inject *item_id* into the workflow prefix and clear stale skip entries."""
   249→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   250→    if item_id not in order:
   251→        order.append(item_id)
   252→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
   253→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   254→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
   255→        skipped.pop(item_id, None)
   256→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=[item_id])
   257→
   258→
   259→_EMPTY = QueueSyncResult
   260→
   261→
   262→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   263→    plan: PlanModel,
   264→    state: StateModel,
   265→    *,
   266→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   267→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   268→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   269→
   270→    Injects when:
   271→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   272→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   273→
   274→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   275→    """
   276→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   277→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   278→
   279→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   280→        return _EMPTY()
   281→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   282→        return _EMPTY()
   283→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   284→
   285→
   286→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   287→    plan: PlanModel,
   288→    state: StateModel,
   289→    *,
   290→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   291→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   292→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   293→
   294→    Only injects when:
   295→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   296→    - At least one objective issue exists
   297→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   298→    - No triage stages are pending
   299→
   300→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.

1→"""Lifecycle and reset mutations for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    empty_plan,
    10→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    13→
    14→
    15→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
    16→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
    17→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    18→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
    19→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
    20→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
    21→
    22→
    23→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    24→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
    25→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    26→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
    27→
    28→
    29→def clear_focus_if_cluster_empty(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    30→    """Clear focus when the active cluster no longer has actionable queue members."""
    31→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    32→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    33→    if not active:
    34→        return
    35→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active)
    36→    if cluster is None:
    37→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
    38→        return
    39→    issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids") or []
    40→    if not issue_ids:
    41→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
    42→        return
    43→    queue_order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    44→    if not any(issue_id in queue_order for issue_id in issue_ids):
    45→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
    46→
    47→
    48→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    49→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp.
    50→
    51→    Sets ``plan_start_scores`` to a sentinel so the next scan seeds real
    52→    scores instead of incorrectly treating the reset as a completed cycle.
    53→    """
    54→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
    55→    plan.clear()
    56→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
    57→        plan[k] = v
    58→    plan["created"] = created
    59→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {"reset": True}
    60→
    61→
    62→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]) -> int:
    63→    """Remove issue IDs from the plan entirely.
    64→
    65→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
    66→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
    67→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
    68→    """
    69→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    70→    found = 0
    71→
    72→    purge_set = set(issue_ids)
    73→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, purge_set)
    74→
    75→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    76→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    77→    for fid in issue_ids:
    78→        was_present = False
    79→        if fid in order:
    80→            order.remove(fid)
    81→            was_present = True
    82→        if fid in skipped:
    83→            skipped.pop(fid)
    84→            was_present = True
    85→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    86→            ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
    87→            if fid in ids:
    88→                ids.remove(fid)
    89→                was_present = True
    90→        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
    91→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
    92→            override["cluster"] = None
    93→            override["updated_at"] = utc_now()
    94→        if was_present:
    95→            found += 1
    96→
    97→    clear_focus_if_cluster_empty(plan)
    98→    return found
    99→
   100→
   101→__all__ = [
   102→    "clear_focus",
   103→    "clear_focus_if_cluster_empty",
   104→    "purge_ids",
   105→    "reset_plan",
   106→    "set_focus",
   107→]
   108→

    55→    if stage == "reflect":

    71→    tools = [

    73→        for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
    74→    ]
    75→    if manual_fallback:

    77→    tools.append({"label": "Dashboard", "command": "desloppify plan triage"})

    81→def _triage_progression_target(plan: dict) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:

    83→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})

    85→    for stage, _sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:

    87→        if isinstance(stage_payload, dict) and stage_payload and not stage_payload.get("confirmed_at"):
    88→            return stage, True

    91→    for stage, sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:

    94→        return stage, False

1→"""Integration tests for lifecycle transitions through reconcile → work queue.
     2→
     3→Exercises the full lifecycle by walking through each stage:
     4→  scan → initial reviews → communicate-score → create-plan → triage → objectives
     5→
     6→Between scans, items are completed via ``purge_ids`` (what ``plan resolve``
     7→does) and the queue is re-checked without reconciling.  ``reconcile`` only
     8→runs at scan boundaries — matching the real CLI flow.
