    44→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    45→# State / plan helpers
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→
    48→def _placeholder_dim_entries(key: str) -> tuple[str, dict, dict]:
    49→    """(display_name, dim_scores_entry, subjective_assessment) for a placeholder."""
    50→    return (
    51→        DIM_DISPLAY[key],
    52→        {
    53→            "score": 0, "strict": 0, "failing": 0, "checks": 0,
    54→            "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"placeholder": True, "dimension_key": key}},
    55→        },
    56→        {"score": 0, "placeholder": True},
    57→    )
    58→
    59→
    60→def _scored_dim_entries(key: str, score: float) -> tuple[str, dict, dict]:
    61→    """(display_name, dim_scores_entry, subjective_assessment) with a real score."""
    62→    return (
    63→        DIM_DISPLAY[key],
    64→        {
    65→            "score": score, "strict": score, "failing": 0, "checks": 1,
    66→            "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"placeholder": False, "dimension_key": key}},
    67→        },
    68→        {"score": score, "placeholder": False},
    69→    )
    70→
    71→
    72→def _build_state(
    73→    issues: list[dict],
    74→    dims: list[tuple[str, dict, dict]],
    75→    *,
    76→    strict_score: float | None = None,
    77→    overall_score: float | None = None,
    78→    objective_score: float | None = None,
    79→) -> dict:
    80→    state: dict = {
    81→        "issues": {i["id"]: dict(i) for i in issues},
    82→        "scan_count": 1,
    83→        "dimension_scores": {},
    84→        "subjective_assessments": {},
    85→    }
    86→    for display, dim_entry, assessment in dims:
    87→        state["dimension_scores"][display] = dim_entry
    88→        dim_key = dim_entry["detectors"]["subjective_assessment"]["dimension_key"]
    89→        state["subjective_assessments"][dim_key] = assessment
    90→    if strict_score is not None:
    91→        state["strict_score"] = strict_score
    92→    if overall_score is not None:
    93→        state["overall_score"] = overall_score
    94→    if objective_score is not None:
    95→        state["objective_score"] = objective_score
    96→    return state
    97→
    98→
    99→def _reconcile(state: dict, plan: dict, monkeypatch) -> dict:
   100→    """Simulate a scan boundary: run reconcile_plan_post_scan with mocked I/O."""
   101→    saved: list[dict] = []
   102→    monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "load_plan", lambda _path=None: plan)
   103→    monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "save_plan", lambda p, _path=None: saved.append(p))
   104→    reconcile_mod.reconcile_plan_post_scan(SimpleNamespace(
   105→        state=state, state_path=Path("/tmp/fake-state.json"), config={},
   106→    ))
   107→    return saved[-1] if saved else plan
   108→
   109→
   110→def _queue_ids(state: dict, plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   111→    result = build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(plan=plan, count=None))
   112→    return [item["id"] for item in result["items"]]
   113→
   114→
   115→def _spoof_reviews_complete(state: dict) -> None:
   116→    """Mutate state in place: replace all placeholder dims with scored ones."""
   117→    for key in DIM_KEYS:
   118→        display, dim_entry, assessment = _scored_dim_entries(key, 75.0)
   119→        state["dimension_scores"][display] = dim_entry
   120→        state["subjective_assessments"][key] = assessment
   121→    state["strict_score"] = 75.0
   122→    state["overall_score"] = 75.0
   123→    state["objective_score"] = 80.0
   124→
   125→
   126→def _complete_endgame_subjective_reruns(state: dict) -> None:
   127→    """Mark scored subjective dimensions as fully current/high to clear rerun queue."""
   128→    for key in DIM_KEYS:
   129→        assessment = state["subjective_assessments"].setdefault(key, {})
   130→        assessment["placeholder"] = False
   131→        assessment["needs_review_refresh"] = False
   132→        assessment["score"] = 100.0
   133→        dim = state["dimension_scores"][DIM_DISPLAY[key]]
   134→        dim["score"] = 100.0
   135→        dim["strict"] = 100.0
   136→
   137→
   138→def _add_review_issues(state: dict) -> None:
   139→    """Mutate state in place: add review detector issues that trigger triage."""
   140→    for key in ("naming_quality", "logic_clarity"):
   141→        fid = f"review-{key}"
   142→        state["issues"][fid] = {
   143→            "id": fid, "detector": "review", "file": "src/app.py",
   144→            "status": "open", "detail": {"dimension": key},
   145→        }
   146→

    44→def _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    45→    """Clear all cycle state when --force-rescan is used.
    46→
    47→    Force-rescan means "start over" — the old cycle's triage stages,
    48→    workflow items, subjective dimensions, plan-start scores, and triage
    49→    metadata are all stale and must be removed.
    50→    """
    51→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    52→    synthetic = [item for item in order if any(item.startswith(p) for p in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)]
    53→    if not synthetic and not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    54→        return False
    55→    for item in synthetic:
    56→        order.remove(item)
    57→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    58→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
    59→    plan.pop("scan_count_at_plan_start", None)
    60→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    61→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    62→        meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
    63→    count = len(synthetic)
    64→    if count:
    65→        print(
    66→            colorize(
    67→                f"  Plan: force-rescan — removed {count} synthetic item(s) "
    68→                f"and reset cycle state.",
    69→                "yellow",
    70→            )
    71→        )
    72→    return True
    73→
    74→
    75→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    76→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    77→    return bool(
    78→        plan.get("queue_order")
    79→        or plan.get("overrides")
    80→        or plan.get("clusters")
    81→        or plan.get("skipped")
    82→    )
    83→
    84→
    85→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    86→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    87→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    88→        return False
    89→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    90→    if recon.resurfaced:
    91→        print(
    92→            colorize(
    93→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    94→                "cyan",
    95→            )
    96→        )
    97→    return bool(recon.changes)
    98→
    99→
   100→def _sync_subjective_dimensions_display(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
   101→    """Sync all subjective dimensions (unscored + stale + under-target) in plan queue."""
   102→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
   103→    if sync.resurfaced:
   104→        print(
   105→            colorize(
   106→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.resurfaced)} skipped subjective dimension(s) resurfaced — never reviewed.",
   107→                "yellow",
   108→            )
   109→        )
   110→    if sync.pruned:
   111→        print(
   112→            colorize(
   113→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
   114→                "cyan",
   115→            )
   116→        )
   117→    if sync.injected:
   118→        print(
   119→            colorize(
   120→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} subjective dimension(s) queued for review.",
   121→                "cyan",
   122→            )
   123→        )
   124→    return bool(sync.changes)
   125→
   126→
   127→def _sync_auto_clusters(
   128→    plan: dict[str, object],
   129→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   130→    *,
   131→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   132→    policy=None,
   133→) -> bool:
   134→    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""
   135→    return bool(auto_cluster_issues(
   136→        plan, state,
   137→        target_strict=target_strict,
   138→        policy=policy,
   139→    ))
   140→
   141→
   142→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   143→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   144→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   145→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   146→        return False
   147→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   148→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   149→        return False
   150→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   151→    if scores.strict is None:
   152→        return False
   153→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   154→        "strict": scores.strict,
   155→        "overall": scores.overall,
   156→        "objective": scores.objective,
   157→        "verified": scores.verified,
   158→    }
   159→    # New cycle — clear the communicate-score sentinel so it can fire again.
   160→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   161→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   162→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   163→    return True
   164→
   165→
   166→def _has_objective_cycle(
   167→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   168→    plan: dict[str, object],
   169→) -> bool | None:
   170→    """Return True when objective queue work exists and a cycle baseline should freeze."""
   171→    try:
   172→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   173→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   174→        )
   175→
   176→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   177→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   178→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "compute queue breakdown for plan-start seeding", exc)
   179→        return None
   180→    return breakdown.objective_actionable > 0
   181→
   182→
   183→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   184→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   185→) -> bool:
   186→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   187→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   188→        return False
   189→    # Don't clear while communicate-score is pending — the rebaseline just
   190→    # set plan_start_scores and the user hasn't seen the update yet.
   191→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   192→        return False
   193→
   194→    try:
   195→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   196→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   197→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   198→            score_display_mode,
   199→        )
   200→
   201→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   202→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   203→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   204→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   205→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   206→        return False
   207→    if not queue_empty:
   208→        return False
   209→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   210→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   211→    # Clear the cycle sentinel so communicate-score can be injected
   212→    # in the next cycle.
   213→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   214→    return True
   215→

   217→def _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(

   459→def _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   460→    """Return True when a plan cycle is active and queue items remain.
   461→
   462→    Extends ``is_mid_cycle`` (which checks ``plan_start_scores``) with an
   463→    additional queue-items guard — even if a cycle is nominally active, we
   464→    only skip destructive operations when work actually remains.
   465→
   466→    Mid-cycle scans (via --force-rescan or PHASE_TRANSITION gate) must NOT
   467→    regenerate clusters or inject triage stages — doing so wipes triage
   468→    state and reorders the queue, undoing prioritisation work.
   469→    """
   470→    if not is_mid_cycle(plan):
   471→        return False
   472→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   473→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   474→    return any(item not in skipped for item in order)

   476→
   477→def _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   478→    *,
   479→    plan: dict[str, object],
   480→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   481→    target_strict: float,
   482→    policy,
   483→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   484→    force_rescan: bool = False,
   485→) -> bool:
   486→    """Run post-scan sync steps that require policy/cycle context.
   487→
   488→    When running mid-cycle (plan_start_scores set, queue non-empty) or
   489→    via --force-rescan, skip auto-clustering and triage injection.  These
   490→    steps regenerate queue structure and issue IDs, which wipes triage
   491→    state and reorders the queue.  They only run at cycle boundaries
   492→    (pre-flight / post-flight).
   493→    """
   494→    dirty = False
   495→    mid_cycle = _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state) or force_rescan
   496→
   497→    if _sync_subjective_and_log(
   498→        plan,
   499→        state,
   500→        policy=policy,
   501→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   502→    ):
   503→        dirty = True
   504→    if not mid_cycle:
   505→        if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   506→            plan,
   507→            state,
   508→            target_strict=target_strict,
   509→            policy=policy,
   510→        ):
   511→            dirty = True
   512→    else:
   513→        print(
   514→            colorize(
   515→                "  Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration to "
   516→                "preserve queue state.",
   517→                "dim",
   518→            )
   519→        )
   520→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   521→        dirty = True
   522→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   523→        dirty = True
   524→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   525→        dirty = True
   526→    if not force_rescan:
   527→        if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, state):
   528→            dirty = True
   529→        if _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, state):
   530→            dirty = True
   531→    if _sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log(plan, state):
   532→        dirty = True
   533→    return dirty

   536→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   537→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   538→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   539→    try:
   540→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   541→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   542→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   543→        return
   544→
   545→    force_rescan = getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False)
   546→
   547→    # Force-rescan: clear all cycle state first.  The user explicitly chose
   548→    # to start over, so triage stages, workflow items, subjective dimensions,
   549→    # and plan-start scores from the old cycle are all stale.
   550→    dirty = _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan) if force_rescan else False
   551→
   552→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan=plan, state=runtime.state) or dirty
   553→
   554→    # Policy must be computed after reconciliation, which mutates plan
   555→    # (supersede/prune resolved issues) before policy reads it.
   556→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   557→        runtime,
   558→        plan,
   559→    )
   560→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   561→        plan=plan,
   562→        state=runtime.state,
   563→        target_strict=target_strict,
   564→        policy=policy,
   565→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   566→        force_rescan=getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False),
   567→    ) or dirty
   568→
   569→    if dirty:
   570→        try:
   571→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   572→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   573→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   574→

   318→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   319→    plan: PlanModel,
   320→    state: StateModel,
   321→    *,
   322→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   323→    import_file: str | None = None,
   324→    import_payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   325→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   326→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   327→
   328→    Only injects when:
   329→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   330→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   331→
   332→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   333→    """
   334→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   335→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   336→    refresh_state = _ensure_refresh_state(plan)
   337→    pending_meta = PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(
   338→        refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   339→    )
   340→    latest_issues_only = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   341→    latest_trusted = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes=_TRUSTED_ASSESSMENT_MODES)
   342→
   343→    if _pending_import_scores_stale(
   344→        order=order,
   345→        pending_meta=pending_meta,
   346→        latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   347→        latest_trusted=latest_trusted,
   348→    ):
   349→        _clear_pending_import_scores(plan)
   350→        return QueueSyncResult(pruned=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   351→
   352→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   353→        if assessment_mode == "issues_only":
   354→            _record_pending_import_scores(
   355→                refresh_state,
   356→                import_file=import_file,
   357→                import_payload=import_payload,
   358→                latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   359→            )
   360→            return QueueSyncResult(resurfaced=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   361→        return _EMPTY()
   362→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   363→        return _EMPTY()
   364→    result = _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   365→    _record_pending_import_scores(
   366→        refresh_state,
   367→        import_file=import_file,
   368→        import_payload=import_payload,
   369→        latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   370→    )
   371→    return result
   372→
   373→
   374→class ScoreSnapshot:
   375→    """Minimal score snapshot for rebaseline — avoids importing state.py."""
   376→
   377→    __slots__ = ("strict", "overall", "objective", "verified")
   378→
   379→    def __init__(
   380→        self,
   381→        *,
   382→        strict: float | None,
   383→        overall: float | None,
   384→        objective: float | None,
   385→        verified: float | None,
   386→    ) -> None:
   387→        self.strict = strict
   388→        self.overall = overall
   389→        self.objective = objective
   390→        self.verified = verified
   391→
   392→
   393→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   394→    plan: PlanModel,
   395→    state: StateModel,
   396→    *,
   397→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   398→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   399→    current_scores: ScoreSnapshot | None = None,
   400→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   401→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` and rebaseline scores.
   402→
   403→    Injects when:
   404→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dims), OR
   405→      scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   406→    - ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue
   407→    - Score has not already been communicated this cycle
   408→      (``previous_plan_start_scores`` absent), unless a trusted score import
   409→      explicitly refreshed the live score mid-cycle
   410→
   411→    When injected and *current_scores* is provided, ``plan_start_scores``
   412→    is rebaselined to the current score so the score display unfreezes at
   413→    the new value.  The previous baseline is preserved in
   414→    ``previous_plan_start_scores`` so the communicate-score queue item can
   415→    show the old → new delta — and so mid-cycle scans know not to
   416→    re-inject.
   417→    """
   418→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   419→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   420→
   421→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order:
   422→        return _EMPTY()
   423→    # Already communicated this cycle — previous_plan_start_scores is set
   424→    # at injection time and cleared at cycle boundaries.
   425→    if "previous_plan_start_scores" in plan and not scores_just_imported:
   426→        return _EMPTY()
   427→    if not scores_just_imported and not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   428→        return _EMPTY()
   429→
   430→    if current_scores is not None:
   431→        _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(plan, current_scores)
   432→    # Set sentinel even when rebaseline was a no-op (no plan_start_scores
   433→    # to rebaseline) so mid-cycle scans don't re-inject.
   434→    if not plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores"):
   435→        plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = {}
   436→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   437→
   438→
   439→def _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(
   440→    plan: PlanModel,
   441→    scores: ScoreSnapshot,
   442→) -> None:
   443→    """Snapshot the current score as the new baseline, preserving the old one."""
   444→    old_start = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   445→    if not isinstance(old_start, dict) or not old_start:
   446→        return
   447→    if scores.strict is None:
   448→        return
   449→
   450→    plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = dict(old_start)
   451→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   452→        "strict": scores.strict,
   453→        "overall": scores.overall,
   454→        "objective": scores.objective,
   455→        "verified": scores.verified,
   456→    }
   457→
   458→
   459→__all__ = [
   460→    "PendingImportScoresMeta",
   461→    "ScoreSnapshot",
   462→    "import_scores_meta_matches",
   463→    "pending_import_scores_meta",
   464→    "sync_communicate_score_needed",
   465→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   466→    "sync_import_scores_needed",
   467→    "sync_score_checkpoint_needed",
   468→]
   469→

## Comprehensive Analysis: Synthetic Workflow Item Builders and Gate Conditions

I've thoroughly reviewed all the relevant files. Here's the detailed breakdown:

---

## **File Locations & Constants**

- **builders**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic_workflow.py` (6 functions)
- **builders**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic.py` (2 functions)
- **gates**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/workflow.py` (4 gate functions)
- **gates**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/triage.py` (1 gate function)
- **gates**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/dimensions.py` (1 gate function)
- **IDs**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/constants.py`
- **Context helpers**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/context.py`
- **Deferral tracking**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/defer_policy.py`
- **Lifecycle**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/refresh_lifecycle.py`

---

## **WORKFLOW ITEMS (Workflow Sync Triggers)**

These items have gate functions that **decide when to inject/prune** them. The builders consume the gate decisions.

### **1. `workflow::run-scan` (WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID)**

**Gate**: `postflight_scan_pending(plan)` in `refresh_lifecycle.py`

**Builder**: `build_run_scan_item(plan)` → `WorkflowActionItem | None`

**Condition — Returns item when:**
- `plan["refresh_state"]["postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count"]` is **NOT set** (i.e., missing or not an int)
- This marks a queue boundary where the persisted scan completion marker is absent

**Read fields:**
- `plan["refresh_state"][postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count]` (sentinel key)

**Mutates?** No direct mutation in builder; gate sync clears this key via `clear_postflight_scan_completion()` when real queue work completes.

**Execution visibility:** `execution_visibility: "always"` (forces display even in constrained contexts)

---

### **2. `workflow::import-scores` (WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)**

**Gate**: `sync_import_scores_needed(plan, state, assessment_mode, import_file, import_payload)` in `workflow.py`

**Builder**: `build_import_scores_item(plan, state)` → `WorkflowActionItem | None`

**Condition — Returns item when:**
- Assessment mode is **exactly** `"issues_only"` (scores were imported but withheld for untrusted external review)
- **AND** item is not already in `plan["queue_order"]`
- **AND** the pending import hasn't been superseded by a trusted import (see `_pending_import_scores_stale()`)

**Read fields:**
- `state["assessment_import_audit"]` — list of import audit entries; gate scans reversed (latest first)
- `plan["refresh_state"]["pending_import_scores"]` — cached pending-import metadata
- `plan["queue_order"]` — check if item already present
- Audit entry: `mode` ("issues_only", "trusted_internal", "attested_external")
- Audit entry: `timestamp` — compared with latest trusted import to detect staleness
- `import_payload["provenance"]["packet_sha256"]` — packet integrity check

**Mutates?**
- `plan["refresh_state"]["pending_import_scores"]` = `{timestamp, import_file, normalized_import_file, packet_sha256}` — stores expected batch metadata
- `plan["queue_order"]` — appends item, then normalizes (workflow prefix ahead)
- `plan["skipped"][WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID]` — removed if present

**Staleness detection** (`_pending_import_scores_stale`):
- If **no** `issues_only` audit exists → **stale** (prune)
- If **no** trusted audit exists → **not stale** (keep queued)
- If trusted audit timestamp ≥ pending audit timestamp → **stale** (user already imported with trust; prune)

---

### **3. `workflow::score-checkpoint` (WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)**

**Gate**: `sync_score_checkpoint_needed(plan, state, policy)` in `workflow.py`

**Builder**: `build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state)` → `WorkflowActionItem | None`

**Condition — Returns item when:**
- **All initial subjective reviews are complete** (no placeholder/unscored dimensions)
- **AND** item is not already in `plan["queue_order"]`

**Read fields:**
- `state["dimension_scores"]` — extracted via `policy.unscored_ids` or `stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids()`
- `plan["plan_start_scores"]["strict"]` — baseline strict score for delta calculation
- `state["findings"]`/dimension data — via `state_mod.score_snapshot(state)` to compute current strict

**Mutates?**
- `plan["queue_order"]` — appends item, normalizes workflow prefix ahead
- `plan["skipped"][WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID]` — removed if present

**Detail fields** in WorkQueueItem:
- `strict` — current strict score
- `plan_start_strict` — baseline from plan
- `delta` — change from baseline to current

---

### **4. `workflow::communicate-score` (WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)**

**Gate**: `sync_communicate_score_needed(plan, state, policy, scores_just_imported, current_scores)` in `workflow.py`

**Builder**: `build_communicate_score_item(plan, state)` → `WorkflowActionItem | None`

**Condition — Returns item when:**
- **All initial subjective reviews complete** (no unscored dims) **OR** scores were just imported with trust
- **AND** item is not already in `plan["queue_order"]`
- **AND** the score has NOT already been communicated this cycle
  - Checked: `"previous_plan_start_scores" not in plan or scores_just_imported is True`
  - `previous_plan_start_scores` is set at injection time and cleared at cycle boundaries

**Read fields:**
- `state["dimension_scores"]` — same unscored check as checkpoint
- `plan["plan_start_scores"]["strict"]` — current baseline
- `plan["previous_plan_start_scores"]["strict"]` — the "previous" baseline set at last injection
- `state` snapshot — current score via `state_mod.score_snapshot()`

**Mutates?**
- **Rebaseline occurs** if `current_scores` is provided:
  - `plan["previous_plan_start_scores"]` = old value of `plan["plan_start_scores"]`
  - `plan["plan_start_scores"]` = new current scores
  - Sets sentinel even if no rebaseline: `plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = {}`
- `plan["queue_order"]` — appends item, normalizes
- `plan["skipped"]` — item removed if present

**Delta calculation:**
- Uses `previous_plan_start_scores` (set at rebaseline) for old → new delta display
- Falls back to `plan_start_scores` if previous not set (first time in cycle)

---

### **5. `workflow::create-plan` (WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)**

**Gate**: `sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy)` in `workflow.py`

**Builder**: `build_create_plan_item(plan)` → `WorkflowActionItem | None`

**Condition — Returns item when:**
- **All initial subjective reviews complete** (no unscored dims)
- **AND** at least one objective issue exists (`has_objective_backlog(state, policy)` = true)
- **AND** item is not already in `plan["queue_order"]`
- **AND** **no triage stages are currently pending** (none of `TRIAGE_IDS` in queue_order)

**Read fields:**
- `state["issues"]` — unscored check + objective backlog check
- `plan["queue_order"]` — check for item presence, check for triage stage presence
- `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_stages"]` — to determine next triage target stage
- `plan["lifecycle_phase"]` — for context (though gate doesn't check this)

**Mutates?** No mutation in builder, but gate normalizes:
- `plan["queue_order"]` — appends, normalizes workflow ahead
- `plan["skipped"]` — removed if present

**Dependencies:**
- This is **blocked if any triage stage is in the queue** — triage takes priority

**Primary command logic** (`_create_plan_primary_command`):
- If stage needs confirmation → suggest `desloppify plan triage --confirm {stage}`
- If commit stage → run all stages
- Otherwise → run only the current stage
- Falls back to observe if no stages recorded

---

### **6. `workflow::deferred-disposition` (WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID)**

**Gate**: None (builder directly checks `plan["skipped"]`)

**Builder**: `build_deferred_disposition_item(plan)` → `WorkflowActionItem | None`

**Condition — Returns item when:**
- At least one entry in `plan["skipped"]` has `kind: "temporary"` (deferred, not wontfix)

**Read fields:**
- `plan["skipped"]` — dict of skipped issue IDs
- `plan["clusters"]` — to compute breakdown of deferred items vs. clusters

**Mutates?** No mutation in builder

**Breakdown logic** (`_deferred_cluster_breakdown`):
- Counts how many deferred items are covered by cluster associations
- Returns `(cluster_count, individual_count)` for summary display

**Detail fields:**
- `temporary_skipped_count` — total deferred items
- `deferred_cluster_count`, `deferred_individual_count` — breakdown
- `planning_tools` — unskip, backlog, wontfix options

**Execution visibility:** `execution_visibility: "always"`

---

## **TRIAGE STAGE ITEMS**

### **7. `build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)` → list[WorkQueueItem]**

**Gate**: Implicit — builder checks `plan["queue_order"]` for presence of triage IDs

**Condition — Returns items when:**
- Any of the 6 triage stage IDs are in `plan["queue_order"]`:
  - `triage::observe`, `triage::reflect`, `triage::organize`, `triage::enrich`, `triage::sense-check`, `triage::commit`
- **AND** the stage is **not** in `confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)` (confirmed stages don't get items)

**Triage sync gate** (`sync_triage_needed` in `triage.py`):
- Decides when to **inject** stage IDs into queue order
- **Three scenarios:**

  1. **Hash change detected** (new review issues since last triage):
     - Calls `decide_triage_start()` (mid-cycle guard check):
       - If objective backlog exists → defer triage, set `meta["triage_recommended"]`
       - If deferred > escalation threshold → escalate, inject stages
       - Otherwise → inject stages and mark ready
  2. **Stages already in queue** — check for staleness:
     - If no baseline triage IDs recorded and fresh review issues exist → don't prune
     - If all new issues since triage have been resolved → **auto-prune** and update hash
     - Otherwise → keep stages queued
  3. **No hash change** — update hash silently, no action

**Read fields** (sync gate):
- `state["issues"]` — open review/concerns issues
- `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triaged_ids"]` — baseline set of issues triage saw
- `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["active_triage_issue_ids"]` — issues currently being triaged
- `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["issue_snapshot_hash"]` — content hash of open review issues
- `plan["queue_order"]` — check stage presence
- `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_stages"]` — confirmed/unconfirmed markers

**Mutates** (sync gate):
- `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["issue_snapshot_hash"]` — updated to match current state
- `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_defer_state"]` — tracks deferral count/escalation
- `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_recommended"]` — set when deferring (UI banner)
- `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_force_visible"]` — set when escalating
- `plan["queue_order"]` — injects/prunes triage stage IDs
- `plan["skipped"]` — removes stage IDs when injecting (unflag temporarily skipped)

**Dependency blocking** (builder):
- Each stage may have dependencies on earlier stages (e.g., organize depends on reflect, reflect on observe)
- If dependency stage is still in queue and not confirmed → add to item's `blocked_by`
- Stages are never removed mid-queue except by explicit pruning

**Builder fields** in WorkQueueItem:
- `id` — stage ID (e.g., "triage::observe")
- `kind` — "workflow_stage"
- `summary` — "Triage: {stage_label}"
- `detail`:
  - `stage` — stage name
  - `total_review_issues` — count of open review/concerns issues
  - `runner_commands` — suggested triage automation commands
  - `manual_fallback` — fallback command if automation fails
- `blocked_by` — list of unsatisfied dependency stages
- `is_blocked` — bool(blocked_by)

---

## **SUBJECTIVE DIMENSION ITEMS**

### **8. `build_subjective_items(state, issues, threshold=100.0)` → list[WorkQueueItem]**

**Gate**: `sync_subjective_dimensions(plan, state, policy, cycle_just_completed)` in `dimensions.py`

**Condition — Builder returns items when:**
- Dimension strict score < threshold (default 100.0 = must be perfect)
- Dimension is not in `skipped` (unless cycle just completed and unscored)

**Sync gate logic** (unified for all subjective IDs):

**Three categories:**
1. **Unscored** (placeholder, never reviewed):
   - **Injected at cycle boundaries only** (not mid-cycle)
   - **Always injected** regardless of objective backlog
   - **Resurfaced** from skip at cycle boundaries

2. **Stale** (assessment is old, needs refresh):
   - **If no objective backlog** → inject to back of queue
   - **If objective backlog exists** → defer (evict from queue, update deferral counter)
   - **If escalated** (deferred 3+ scans or 7+ days) → force-promote ahead of objectives

3. **Under-target** (scored but below target, not stale):
   - Same injection/deferral/escalation rules as stale

**Read fields** (sync gate):
- `state["dimension_scores"]` — placeholder markers, stale flags, scores
- `state["subjective_assessments"]` — fallback for placeholder detection
- `plan["queue_order"]` — check presence of subjective IDs
- `plan["skipped"]` — check which subjective IDs are skipped
- `plan["refresh_state"]["subjective_defer_meta"]` — deferral state with escalation tracking
- `policy.has_objective_backlog` or `has_objective_backlog(state)` — backlog presence

**Mutates** (sync gate):
- `plan["queue_order"]` — injects/prunes/promotes subjective IDs
- `plan["skipped"]` — removes skipped unscored IDs at cycle boundaries
- `plan["refresh_state"]["subjective_defer_meta"]`:
  - `deferred_ids`, `deferred_stale_ids`, `deferred_under_target_ids` — current deferred set
  - `defer_count` — how many scans this set has been deferred
  - `first_deferred_scan`, `first_deferred_at` — escalation timestamps
  - `force_visible_ids` — set when escalated, cleared when escalation ends
- `plan["refresh_state"]` — popped entirely if no deferral

**Escalation logic** (via `defer_policy.py`):
- Escalates when `defer_count >= 3` scans OR `7+ days` since first deferral
- When escalated: subjective IDs are promoted to front (after workflow/triage items)
- Next pass without deferral: escalation state is cleared

**Builder fields** in WorkQueueItem:
- `id` — `subjective::{slugified_dimension_key}`
- `kind` — "subjective_dimension"
- `detector` — "subjective_assessment"
- `summary` — "Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({score:.1f}%)" + optional "[stale — re-review]"
- `detail`:
  - `dimension_name` — human label
  - `dimension` — canonical key
  - `failing` — count of failing review issues
  - `strict_score` — current score
  - `open_review_issues` — count of open review work for this dimension
  - `cli_keys` — dimension aliases for CLI
- `status` — "open"
- `initial_review` — True if unscored/placeholder or failing with no reviews
- `primary_command`:
  - If open review issues exist → `desloppify show review --status open` (triage existing issues)
  - Otherwise → `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {cli_keys}` (start fresh review)

---

## **[REDACTED]**

### **Ordering & Blocking:**

1. **Workflow items** (6 types) → always front of queue via `normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix()`
2. **Triage stages** (6 stages) → after workflow, before objectives
   - Stages have **internal blocking**: later stages block until earlier ones are confirmed
3. **Subjective dimensions** → back of queue (or promoted ahead of objectives if escalated)
4. **Objective issues** → interspersed, subject to lifecycle phase

### **Gate Cascades:**

- **Initial phase**: no unscored → inject `workflow::score-checkpoint`
- **After checkpoint** + objective backlog → inject `workflow::create-plan`
- **Plan created**: triage stages injected into queue (if review issues changed)
- **Triage progresses**: objective issues processed
- **All objectives done + deferred items exist** → inject `workflow::deferred-disposition`
- **Deferred resolved** → inject `workflow::run-scan` (postflight)
- **Postflight scan completes** → back to initial phase

### **Lifecycle Phases:**

- `scan` — postflight boundary, no queue work pending
- `review` — initial reviews or postflight reviews pending
- `workflow` — workflow actions pending (score-checkpoint, create-plan, etc.)
- `triage` — triage stages in queue
- `execute` — objective work in progress

---

## **Critical Sentinel Values & Mutation Patterns:**

| Field | Purpose | Set by | Cleared by |
|-------|---------|--------|-----------|
| `plan["plan_start_scores"]` | Baseline scores at cycle start | Plan creation | Lifecycle reset |
| `plan["previous_plan_start_scores"]` | Old baseline for delta display | `sync_communicate_score_needed()` | Cycle boundaries |
| `plan["refresh_state"]["postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count"]` | Marks scan completion | `mark_postflight_scan_completed()` | Realwork on real issues |
| `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["issue_snapshot_hash"]` | Hash of open review issues | `sync_triage_needed()` | Triage pruning |
| `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_defer_state"]` | Deferral metadata | Triage gate | When triage escalates/ready |
| `plan["refresh_state"]["pending_import_scores"]` | Pending score-import batch | `sync_import_scores_needed()` | When import completes/stales |
| `plan["refresh_state"]["subjective_defer_meta"]` | Subjective dimension deferral | `sync_subjective_dimensions()` | When no longer deferring |

---

This analysis captures every gate condition, mutation, and dependency in the synthetic queue system. The key insight: **gate functions decide injection/pruning via return `QueueSyncResult`**, then **builders consume those queue IDs to generate display items**.

## Complete Picture: Queue Empty → Inject Next Phase Logic

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's the complete map of all entry points, mutation sites, and ordering dependencies for the "queue empty → inject next phase" logic:

### PRIMARY ENTRY POINTS

#### 1. **`reconcile_plan_post_scan()` — The Main Lifecycle Orchestrator**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py:536-574`

Called from: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/workflow.py:436` (line 436 in `merge_scan_results()`)

**Execution order (strictly sequential):**
1. `_reset_cycle_for_force_rescan()` — clears synthetic items and cycle state if `--force-rescan`
2. `_sync_post_scan_without_policy()` — calls `reconcile_plan_after_scan()` (reconciles skipped/resolved issues)
3. Compute `target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed` from the now-cleaned plan
4. `_sync_post_scan_with_policy()` — **THE CRITICAL SECTION** (see below)

**The critical section `_sync_post_scan_with_policy()` (lines 477-533) runs these in order:**
1. `_sync_subjective_and_log()` — resync subjective dimensions (in-queue review items)
2. If not mid-cycle: `_sync_auto_clusters_and_log()` — regenerate objective clusters
3. `_sync_communicate_score_and_log()` — injects `workflow::communicate-score` when reviews complete
4. `_sync_create_plan_and_log()` — injects `workflow::create-plan` when objectives exist post-review
5. `_sync_triage_and_log()` — injects `triage::*` stages when review issues change
6. If not force-rescan:
   - `_sync_plan_start_scores_and_log()` — seed OR clear frozen score when queue drains
   - `_sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log()` — marks postflight scan complete at queue boundary
7. `_sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log()` — persists coarse phase to `refresh_state.lifecycle_phase`

---

#### 2. **`sync_plan_after_import()` — Post-Review-Import Phase Resolution**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/plan_sync.py:373-493`

Called from: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/cmd.py:184` (in `_persist_import_state()`)

**Execution order:**
1. Check if living plan exists; exit silently if not
2. Load plan and compute policy from target_strict
3. Call `sync_communicate_score_needed()` — may inject `workflow::communicate-score`
4. Call `sync_import_scores_needed()` — may inject `workflow::import-scores` for issues-only imports
5. Call `sync_create_plan_needed()` — may inject `workflow::create-plan`
6. `_sync_review_delta()` — call `sync_plan_after_review_import()` to append new review IDs to queue
7. `_sync_subjective_queue_after_import()` — sync stale subjective dimensions + re-cluster
8. If mutations occurred:
   - `_sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import()` — calls `sync_lifecycle_phase()` with explicit phase-decision params
9. Save plan once

| `_inject()` in workflow.py | `workflow::communicate-score`, `workflow::import-scores`, `workflow::create-plan`, `workflow::score-checkpoint` | When preconditions met (see gates below) | `/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/workflow.py:247-256` |
| `sync_plan_after_review_import()` | New review issue IDs (e.g., `review::abc123`) | After review import merges new issues | `/desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile_review_import.py:159-174` |

### WORKFLOW GATE SYNC FUNCTIONS — The Conditional Injectors

All defined in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/workflow.py`:

#### **`sync_communicate_score_needed()` (lines 393-436)**
**Injects:** `workflow::communicate-score`
**When ALL of these are true:**
- NOT already in queue
- NOT already communicated this cycle (`previous_plan_start_scores` absent, OR scores just imported)
- Either: no unscored (placeholder) subjective dims remain, OR `scores_just_imported=True`

**Called from:** `reconcile_plan_post_scan()` + `sync_plan_after_import()`

**Side effect:** Rebaselines `plan_start_scores` to current score if provided (preserves old in `previous_plan_start_scores`)

#### **`sync_create_plan_needed()` (lines 286-315)**
**Injects:** `workflow::create-plan`
**When ALL of these are true:**
- NOT already in queue
- No triage stages pending (mid-cycle guard)
- No unscored subjective dims
- At least one objective issue exists

**Called from:** `reconcile_plan_post_scan()` + `sync_plan_after_import()`

#### **`sync_import_scores_needed()` (lines 318-371)**
**Injects:** `workflow::import-scores`
**When:**
- `assessment_mode == "issues_only"` (scores were skipped on first import)
- NOT already in queue OR stale
- Records pending metadata for later validation

**Called from:** `sync_plan_after_import()` only

#### **`sync_score_checkpoint_needed()` (lines 262-283)**
**Injects:** `workflow::score-checkpoint`
**When:**
- No unscored subjective dims
- NOT already in queue

**Called from:** (currently not called in post-scan flow, but available for future use)

#### **`sync_triage_needed()` (lines 298-364)**
**Injects/Prunes:** `triage::*` stage IDs
**File:** `/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/triage.py`

**Logic:**
- If triage stages already in queue: prune if all new issues resolved since injection
- Otherwise: compute review issue snapshot hash
- If hash changed (new review issues):
  - **Mid-cycle guard:** if objective backlog still has work AND defer hasn't escalated → defer, don't inject
  - Otherwise → inject pending stages
- **Escalation:** if defer repeats across multiple scans → escalate and force inject

**Called from:** `reconcile_plan_post_scan()` + `sync_plan_after_import()` + manual `plan triage` command

---

### LIFECYCLE PHASE PERSISTENCE & RESOLUTION

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/refresh_lifecycle.py`

#### **Phase Constants (lines 19-32):**
```
scan, review, workflow, triage, execute
```

#### **Key Functions:**

| Function | Purpose | Caller |
|---|---|---|
| `set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)` | Persist phase to `plan["refresh_state"]["lifecycle_phase"]` | `reconcile_plan_post_scan()` via `_sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log()` |
| `sync_lifecycle_phase(plan, has_initial_reviews, has_objective_backlog, has_postflight_review, has_postflight_workflow, has_triage, has_deferred)` | Resolve phase from queue facts + persist | `sync_plan_after_import()` via `_sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import()` |
| `current_lifecycle_phase(plan)` | Fetch persisted phase (with fallback to `postflight_scan_pending()`) | Read-side; used by CLI display |
| `postflight_scan_pending(plan)` | Check if empty-queue boundary still needs scan (sentinel check) | Read-side |
| `mark_postflight_scan_completed(plan, scan_count)` | Set sentinel once queue drains and scan completes | `_mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready()` in reconcile |
| `clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids)` | Reset sentinel when real issues are resolved/reopened (requires rescan) | `update_living_plan_after_resolve()` + `cmd_plan_reopen()` |

#### **Phase Resolution Algorithm** (`resolve_lifecycle_phase()` lines 96-122):
Priority order (first match wins):
1. If unscored initial reviews remain → **review**
2. If objective backlog exists → **execute**
3. If deferred work OR postflight scan pending → **scan**
4. If postflight review items exist → **review**
5. If postflight workflow items exist → **workflow**
6. If triage items pending → **triage**
7. Otherwise → use persisted phase or default to **scan**

---

### QUEUE SNAPSHOT & PHASE DETERMINATION

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py`

**`build_queue_snapshot()` (lines 223-341):**
- Assembles read-side partitions: objective, initial-review, scan, postflight-review, postflight-workflow, triage
- **Calls `_phase_for_snapshot()`** (lines 164-186) to determine current phase from visible items (NOT from persisted phase)
- Phase order: review_initial → execute → scan → review_postflight → workflow_postflight → triage_postflight
- **This is the AUTHORITATIVE phase** — used for CLI display

**`coarse_phase_name()` (lines 344-352):**
- Maps internal phases to coarse persisted values (review_initial/review_postflight → "review", etc.)

#### **Synthetic Item Builders** (all conditional; they build items ONLY if queued):
- `build_run_scan_item()` — if `postflight_scan_pending()` true
- `build_deferred_disposition_item()` — if deferred items exist
- `build_score_checkpoint_item()` — if queued
- `build_communicate_score_item()` — if queued
- `build_create_plan_item()` — if queued
- `build_import_scores_item()` — if queued
- `build_triage_stage_items()` — for each `triage::*` queued

---

### CRITICAL ORDERING DEPENDENCIES

1. **Plan reconciliation before policy computation:** `reconcile_plan_after_scan()` must run first (line 552) so issue supersede/resolution mutates plan before policy reads it.

2. **Subjective sync before auto-cluster:** Clustering works on the refined issue set (line 505-511).

3. **Workflow gates before triage inject:** `communicate-score` and `create-plan` must be checked (lines 520-525) before `sync_triage_needed()` injects stages; mid-cycle guard in triage respects whether objectives remain.

4. **Plan-start scores seeding/clearing last:** Only after all mutations (line 527-530) so score snapshot reflects final queue state.

5. **Postflight scan completion mark last:** Only when queue is empty AND no objective cycle (line 529-530).

6. **Phase sync runs after all injections:** So coarse phase is resolved from final queue state (line 531-532).

7. **Single save operation:** All mutations batch into one `save_plan()` call (line 571).

### TEST EXAMPLE

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/test_lifecycle_transitions.py`

Shows the full lifecycle:
- Fresh scan → initial reviews only
- Complete reviews (no reconcile) → objectives visible
- Next scan after reviews → communicate-score + create-plan injected
- Complete objectives → triage/postflight workflow becomes visible
- Etc.

---

### SUMMARY TABLE: What Triggers What

| Event | Mutation | Result Phase |
|---|---|---|
| Fresh scan, placeholders exist | Reconcile; no workflow injected | **review** (initial) |
| User completes initial reviews | No scan, purge subjective IDs | **execute** (objectives visible) |
| Scan after reviews complete | Communicate-score + create-plan injected; seed plan_start_scores | **workflow** (postflight) |
| User completes objectives | No scan, purge objective IDs | **triage** or **scan** (if review issues changed) |
| Review issues changed since triage | Triage stages injected (if not mid-cycle) | **triage** |
| User completes triage + objectives | Scan needed → postflight scan item | **scan** (postflight_scan_pending=true) |
| Scan runs with empty queue + no objective | Mark postflight_scan_completed; clear plan_start_scores | **scan** (idle/boundary) |

Each event chains to the next via the reconcile → phase-resolution → snapshot → display loop.

## Cycle Concept: Complete Architecture

The "cycle" is the central state machine governing the entire queue lifecycle. It's a phase-based flow triggered by `plan_start_scores` snapshots and controlled by mid-cycle guards. Here's the complete picture:

### 1. **What is a Cycle?**

A cycle is an execution period bounded by two moments:
- **Start**: User begins executing queue items (plan_start_scores is seeded with real score values)
- **End**: Queue becomes empty and a postflight scan completes (plan_start_scores is cleared)

The cycle gates the injection of synthetic workflow items (score checkpoint, create-plan, communicate-score, triage) and determines when subjective dimensions can be surfaced.

**Three states of `plan_start_scores`:**
1. **Empty dict `{}`** — cycle is NOT active (queue was drained or plan reset)
2. **Reset sentinel `{"reset": True}`** — plan was reset; next scan will seed real scores
3. **Real scores dict `{"strict": 70.0, "overall": ..., ...}`** — cycle IS active

### 2. **is_mid_cycle() — The Core Gate**

Located in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/context.py`:

```python
def is_mid_cycle(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    """True when a queue cycle is in progress.
    
    A cycle is active when ``plan_start_scores`` is set to real score
    values.  The ``{"reset": True}`` sentinel (set by lifecycle reset)
    does NOT count as mid-cycle — it means "seed real scores on next scan".
    """
    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    return bool(scores) and not (isinstance(scores, dict) and scores.get("reset"))
```

This single check controls:
- Whether to regenerate auto-clusters (skipped mid-cycle to preserve queue order)
- Whether to inject triage stages (skipped mid-cycle to avoid overwriting stage state)
- Whether to suppress certain subjective dimension injections

### 3. **_is_fresh_boundary() — Queue Phase Detection**

Located in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py`:

```python
def _is_fresh_boundary(plan: dict | None) -> bool:
    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
        return True
    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    if not scores:
        return True  # Empty, so fresh boundary
    return isinstance(scores, dict) and bool(scores.get("reset"))  # Sentinel also fresh
```

Returns True when the queue is entering a new phase (no cycle running). Used to determine if initial_review items should execute (only at phase boundaries).

### 4. **Cycle Lifecycle: Key Operations**

#### **Seeding the Cycle** (`_seed_plan_start_scores`)
Path: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py:142-163`

When:
- `plan_start_scores` is empty `{}` OR the reset sentinel `{"reset": True}`
- At least one objective issue exists (checked via `_has_objective_cycle()`)

What:
```python
plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
    "strict": scores.strict,
    "overall": scores.overall,
    "objective": scores.objective,
    "verified": scores.verified,
}
plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)  # Clear rebaseline sentinel
```

Effect: Freezes the current score baseline so the user sees "frozen at 70.0" during execution, and stale/under-target subjective dimensions are gated behind objective backlog.

#### **Clearing the Cycle** (`_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty`)
Path: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py:183-214`

When:
- `plan_start_scores` is set (cycle active)
- Queue is fully drained (checked via `score_display_mode()`)
- `workflow::communicate-score` is NOT pending (so the user has seen the score update)

What:
```python
state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])  # Stash for reveal
plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}  # Clear to end cycle
plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)  # Clear rebaseline sentinel
```

Effect: Transitions from frozen score display → live/updating display, enables postflight scan mechanics.

#### **Rebaseline in Mid-Cycle** (`_rebaseline_plan_start_scores`)
Path: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/workflow.py:439-456`

When: A trusted score import happens mid-cycle, or initial reviews complete

What:
```python
old_start = plan.get("plan_start_scores")  # e.g., {"strict": 70.0, ...}
plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = dict(old_start)  # Stash old baseline
plan["plan_start_scores"] = {  # Seed new baseline from current scores
    "strict": 74.5,  # e.g., improved
    "overall": 74.5,
    "objective": 97.5,
    "verified": 97.4,
}
```

Effect: Freezes a new baseline mid-cycle so the score display updates to the new frozen value. The old baseline is preserved so `workflow::communicate-score` can show the old → new delta.

#### **Resetting the Cycle** (`reset_plan`)
Path: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations/lifecycle.py:48-59`

When: User explicitly resets the plan

What:
```python
plan.clear()
for k, v in empty_plan().items():
    plan[k] = v
plan["plan_start_scores"] = {"reset": True}  # Sentinel, not a real cycle
```

Effect: Clears all queue metadata, triage stages, and clusters, but sets the reset sentinel so the next scan knows to seed real scores (not skip seeding).

### 5. **scan_count_at_plan_start — The Scan Counter Mechanism**

When a cycle is seeded:
```python
plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
```

This records which scan number marked the cycle start. Mid-cycle scans can check whether a fresh scan actually ran (by comparing `state.scan_count > scan_count_at_plan_start`).

### 6. **previous_plan_start_scores — The Rebaseline Sentinel**

A dict (possibly empty `{}`) that tracks whether scores have been communicated this cycle:

- Set by `sync_communicate_score_needed()` when injecting `workflow::communicate-score`
- Cleared by `_seed_plan_start_scores()` (new cycle) or `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty()` (cycle ends)
- Used to prevent double-injection of communicate-score in mid-cycle scans

Scenario from test:
```python
# Before rebaseline:
plan["plan_start_scores"] = {"strict": 70.0, ...}
plan["previous_plan_start_scores"]  # not set or empty

# After rebaseline from 74.5:
plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = {"strict": 70.0, ...}  # Old baseline stashed
plan["plan_start_scores"] = {"strict": 74.5, ...}  # New baseline
# communicate-score now shows: 70.0 → 74.5
```

### 7. **postflight_scan_pending() — Empty-Queue Boundary**

Path: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/refresh_lifecycle.py:125-130`

```python
[REDACTED]

def postflight_scan_pending(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    """Return True when the current empty-queue boundary still needs a scan."""
    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
        return True
    return not isinstance(refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY), int)
```

**When triggered:**
- Queue becomes empty (after draining all objective and subjective work)
- Deferred dispositions are resolved (reactivate/backlog/wontfix)
- No new issues appeared

**What marks it complete:**
```python
def mark_postflight_scan_completed(plan: PlanModel, *, scan_count: int | None) -> bool:
    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
    normalized_scan_count = int(scan_count or 0)
    refresh_state[_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY] = normalized_scan_count
    return True
```

**Effect:** Gates the transition from PHASE_SCAN → next phase. Once a postflight scan completes, the plan can clear `plan_start_scores` and start a fresh cycle.

### 8. **cycle_just_completed Flag — Post-Cycle Injection**

Path: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py:245`

```python
cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
```

When True (i.e., cycle was just cleared), the next scan overrides the objective backlog gate and injects stale/under-target subjective dimensions:

```python
sync_subjective_dimensions(
    plan, state,
    policy=policy,
    cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
)
```

From test `test_cycle_completed_injects_stale_despite_objective_backlog`:
- Normal: stale dims blocked by objective backlog → not injected
- Post-cycle: stale dims injected regardless → appear at back of queue

### 9. **Queue Phase Sequencing with Cycles**

From `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/refresh_lifecycle.py:96-122`:

```python
def resolve_lifecycle_phase(...) -> str:
    if has_initial_reviews:
        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW  # Subjective dims (unscored)
    if has_objective_backlog:
        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE  # Frozen cycle: execute objectives
    if has_deferred or postflight_scan_pending(plan):
        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN  # Empty queue: run postflight scan
    if has_postflight_review:
        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW  # After scan: re-review (stale/under-target)
    if has_postflight_workflow:
        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW  # score-checkpoint, import-scores, etc.
    if has_triage:
        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE  # Triage stages
    # Fallback...
    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
```

**Normal cycle flow:**
1. REVIEW_INITIAL (unscored subjective) → seed cycle when queue moves to execute
2. EXECUTE (frozen score, objective backlog) → mid-cycle guards active
3. SCAN (empty queue) → mark postflight scan complete
4. REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT (stale/under-target) → injected after cycle clears
5. WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT (score checkpoint, communicate-score, etc.)
6. TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT (if needed)
7. Back to step 1

### 10. **Mid-Cycle Scan Guards**

From `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py:459-474`:

```python
def _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> bool:
    """Return True when a plan cycle is active and queue items remain."""
    if not is_mid_cycle(plan):
        return False
    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    return any(item not in skipped for item in order)
```

When True, the post-scan reconciliation **skips**:
- Auto-cluster regeneration (would reorder and wipe triage state)
- Triage stage injection (would overwrite stage state and stage IDs)

Printed: `"Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration to preserve queue state."`

---

## Summary: How Cycle Interacts with Empty Queue → Next Phase

1. **Cycle starts** → `_seed_plan_start_scores()` sets real scores, freezes display
2. **During cycle** → `is_mid_cycle()` blocks cluster regen and triage injection
3. **Queue drains** → `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty()` clears scores, unfreezes display
4. **Postflight scan pending** → `postflight_scan_pending()` gates the SCAN phase
5. **Postflight scan completes** → `mark_postflight_scan_completed()` unblocks cycle end
6. **Next scan after cycle** → `cycle_just_completed=True` overrides objective gate, injects stale/under-target subjective dims
7. **Rebaseline mid-cycle** → `sync_communicate_score_needed()` + `_rebaseline_plan_start_scores()` freeze new baseline, inject communicate-score workflow item

The cycle is not just about freezing scores—it's a **state machine gating all major queue mutations** (clustering, triage, workflow items) to preserve user prioritization work during execution.

> DEVELOPER

## Background

We have two "when the queue is empty, start next process" orchestrators that duplicate the same pipeline logic:

1. **`plan_reconcile.py:_sync_post_scan_with_policy()`** — runs after scan completion
2. **`plan_sync.py:sync_plan_after_import()`** — runs after review import

Both call the same gate functions in sequence:
- `sync_subjective_dimensions()` — inject/prune subjective review items
- `sync_communicate_score_needed()` — inject communicate-score workflow item
- `sync_create_plan_needed()` — inject create-plan workflow item  
- `sync_triage_needed()` — inject/prune triage stage items

And both resolve the lifecycle phase afterward (but via different mechanisms).

Additionally, `plan_reconcile` also calls:
- `sync_auto_clusters_and_log()` (only when not mid-cycle)
- `_sync_plan_start_scores_and_log()` (seed/clear cycle baseline)
- `_sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log()` (mark scan complete at boundary)

And `plan_sync` also calls:
- `sync_import_scores_needed()` (only for issues_only imports)
- `sync_plan_after_review_import()` (inject new review IDs)

## The user's insight

The user's mental model: when the real work queue is empty, a fixed pipeline fires:
`scan → subjective review → (execute real work) → fetch score → triage`

This pipeline is the same whether you're at the START of a plan (no work yet) or at the END (cleared the backlog). The pre-flight/post-flight distinction should disappear.

Currently both orchestrators implement this pipeline independently. The refactor should make it ONE pipeline that both callers invoke.

## Key constraints

The gate functions have non-uniform signatures:
- `sync_subjective_dimensions(plan, state, *, policy, cycle_just_completed=False)`
- `sync_communicate_score_needed(plan, state, *, policy, scores_just_imported=False, current_scores=None)`
- `sync_import_scores_needed(plan, state, *, assessment_mode, import_file, import_payload)`
- `sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, *, policy)`
- `sync_triage_needed(plan, state, *, policy)`

The side effects in these functions (defer tracking, rebaseline, hash updates) need to survive — they maintain important state.

Lifecycle phase resolution is done two ways:
- plan_reconcile: builds full QueueSnapshot via `queue_context()`, then `coarse_phase_name()`
- plan_sync: manually computes boolean flags, calls `sync_lifecycle_phase()`

The shared helper `_has_postflight_review_work()` is literally copy-pasted in both files.

## Proposed approach: Centralized pipeline function

Create one function that runs all gates in sequence:

```python
def reconcile_postflight_gates(
    plan, state, *,
    policy,
    cycle_just_completed=False,
    scores_just_imported=False,
    current_scores=None,
    assessment_mode=None,
    import_file=None,
    import_payload=None,
) -> PostflightResult:
    # Run all gates in pipeline order
    # Resolve and persist lifecycle phase
    # Return structured result with all changes
```

Both callers shrink to: (1) domain-specific work, (2) call reconcile_postflight_gates, (3) save.

Phase resolution also moves into this function, eliminating the two different approaches.

Design a detailed implementation plan for this refactor. Consider:
1. Where should the centralized function live? (engine/_plan/sync/ seems right since it's alongside the gate functions)
2. What should the result type look like?
3. How should lifecycle phase resolution be unified inside the function?
4. What about the shared `_has_postflight_review_work()` — where should it live?
5. How do the logging/display calls work? Currently both callers have their own logging wrappers. Should the pipeline return results and let callers log, or should it log itself?
6. What about auto-clustering — it's called between subjective sync and workflow gates in plan_reconcile, and after review delta in plan_sync. Should it be inside the pipeline or stay in callers?
7. What about plan_start_scores seeding/clearing — plan_reconcile does this after the gates. Should it be in the pipeline?
