   202→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   203→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   204→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   205→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   206→        return False
   207→    if not queue_empty:
   208→        return False
   209→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   210→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   211→    # Clear the cycle sentinel so communicate-score can be injected
   212→    # in the next cycle.
   213→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   214→    return True
   215→
   216→
   217→def _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(
   218→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   219→    plan: dict[str, object],
   220→) -> bool:
   221→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current empty-queue boundary."""
   222→    if build_deferred_disposition_item(plan) is not None:
   223→        return False
   224→    objective_cycle = _has_objective_cycle(state, plan)
   225→    if objective_cycle is not False:
   226→        return False
   227→    return mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   228→        plan,
   229→        scan_count=int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0),
   230→    )
   231→
   232→
   233→def _subjective_policy_context(
   234→    runtime: Any,
   235→    plan: dict[str, object],
   236→) -> tuple[float, object, bool]:
   237→    from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
   238→
   239→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   240→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   241→        runtime.state,
   242→        target_strict=target_strict,
   243→        plan=plan,
   244→    )
   245→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   246→    return target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed
   247→
   248→
   249→def _sync_subjective_and_log(
   250→    plan: dict[str, object],
   251→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   252→    *,
   253→    policy,
   254→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   255→) -> bool:
   256→    changed = _sync_subjective_dimensions_display(
   257→        plan,
   258→        state,
   259→        lambda p, s: sync_subjective_dimensions(
   260→            p,
   261→            s,
   262→            policy=policy,
   263→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   264→        ),
   265→    )
   266→    if changed:
   267→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_subjective", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   268→    return changed
   269→
   270→
   271→def _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   272→    plan: dict[str, object],
   273→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   274→    *,
   275→    target_strict: float,
   276→    policy,
   277→) -> bool:
   278→    changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   279→        plan,
   280→        state,
   281→        target_strict=target_strict,
   282→        policy=policy,
   283→    )
   284→    if changed:
   285→        append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   286→    return changed
   287→
   288→
   289→def _sync_triage_and_log(
   290→    plan: dict[str, object],
   291→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   292→    *,
   293→    policy=None,
   294→) -> bool:
   295→    triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   296→    if triage_sync.deferred:
   297→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   298→        if meta.get("triage_recommended"):
   299→            print(
   300→                colorize(
   301→                    "  Plan: review issues changed — triage recommended after current work.",
   302→                    "dim",
   303→                )
   304→            )
   305→        return False
   306→    if not triage_sync.changes:
   307→        return False
   308→    if triage_sync.injected:
   309→        print(
   310→            colorize(
   311→                "  Plan: planning mode needed — review issues changed since last triage.",
   312→                "cyan",
   313→            )
   314→        )
   315→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   316→    return True
   317→
   318→
   319→def _sync_communicate_score_and_log(
   320→    plan: dict[str, object],
   321→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   322→    *,
   323→    policy,
   324→) -> bool:
   325→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   326→    current_scores = ScoreSnapshot(
   327→        strict=snapshot.strict,
   328→        overall=snapshot.overall,
   329→        objective=snapshot.objective,
   330→        verified=snapshot.verified,
   331→    )
   332→    communicate_sync = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   333→        plan, state, policy=policy, current_scores=current_scores,
   334→    )
   335→    if not communicate_sync.changes:
   336→        return False
   337→    append_log_entry(
   338→        plan,
   339→        "sync_communicate_score",
   340→        actor="system",
   341→        detail={"injected": True},
   342→    )
   343→    return True
   344→
   345→
   346→def _sync_create_plan_and_log(
   347→    plan: dict[str, object],
   348→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   349→    *,
   350→    policy,
   351→) -> bool:
   352→    create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   353→    if not create_plan_sync.changes:
   354→        return False
   355→    if create_plan_sync.injected:
   356→        print(
   357→            colorize(
   358→                "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",
   359→                "cyan",
   360→            )
   361→        )
   362→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   363→    return True
   364→
   365→
   366→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(
   367→    plan: dict[str, object],
   368→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   369→) -> bool:
   370→    seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state)
   371→    if seeded:
   372→        append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   373→        return True
   374→    # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   375→    # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   376→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   377→    if cleared:
   378→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   379→    return cleared
   380→
   381→
   382→def _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(
   383→    plan: dict[str, object],
   384→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   385→) -> bool:
   386→    changed = _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(state, plan)
   387→    if changed:
   388→        append_log_entry(
   389→            plan,
   390→            "complete_postflight_scan",
   391→            actor="system",
   392→            detail={"scan_count": int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)},
   393→        )
   394→    return changed
   395→
   396→
   397→def _has_postflight_review_work(
   398→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   399→    *,
   400→    policy,
   401→) -> bool:
   402→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   403→    has_review_like_issue = any(
   404→        isinstance(issue, dict)
   405→        and issue.get("status") == "open"
   406→        and issue.get("detector") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
   407→        for issue in issues.values()
   408→    )
   409→    if has_review_like_issue:
   410→        return True
   411→    return bool(policy.stale_ids or policy.under_target_ids)
   412→
   413→
   414→def _has_postflight_workflow_items(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
   415→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   416→    return any(
   417→        item_id in order
   418→        for item_id in (
   419→            "workflow::import-scores",
   420→            "workflow::communicate-score",
   421→            "workflow::score-checkpoint",
   422→            "workflow::create-plan",
   423→        )
   424→    )
   425→
   426→
   427→def _has_triage_items(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
   428→    return any(
   429→        isinstance(item_id, str) and item_id.startswith("triage::")
   430→        for item_id in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   431→    )
   432→
   433→
   434→def _sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log(
   435→    plan: dict[str, object],
   436→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   437→) -> bool:
   438→    phase = coarse_phase_name(queue_context(state, plan=plan).snapshot.phase)
   439→    changed = set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
   440→    if changed:
   441→        append_log_entry(
   442→            plan,
   443→            "sync_lifecycle_phase",
   444→            actor="system",
   445→            detail={"phase": phase},
   446→        )
   447→    return changed
   448→
   449→
   450→def _sync_post_scan_without_policy(
   451→    *,
   452→    plan: dict[str, object],
   453→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   454→) -> bool:
   455→    """Run post-scan sync steps that do not require subjective policy context."""
   456→    return bool(_apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, state, reconcile_plan_after_scan))
   457→
   458→
   459→def _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   460→    """Return True when a plan cycle is active and queue items remain.
   461→
   462→    Extends ``is_mid_cycle`` (which checks ``plan_start_scores``) with an
   463→    additional queue-items guard — even if a cycle is nominally active, we
   464→    only skip destructive operations when work actually remains.
   465→
   466→    Mid-cycle scans (via --force-rescan or PHASE_TRANSITION gate) must NOT
   467→    regenerate clusters or inject triage stages — doing so wipes triage
   468→    state and reorders the queue, undoing prioritisation work.
   469→    """
   470→    if not is_mid_cycle(plan):
   471→        return False
   472→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   473→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   474→    return any(item not in skipped for item in order)
   475→
   476→
   477→def _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   478→    *,
   479→    plan: dict[str, object],
   480→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   481→    target_strict: float,
   482→    policy,
   483→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   484→    force_rescan: bool = False,
   485→) -> bool:
   486→    """Run post-scan sync steps that require policy/cycle context.
   487→
   488→    When running mid-cycle (plan_start_scores set, queue non-empty) or
   489→    via --force-rescan, skip auto-clustering and triage injection.  These
   490→    steps regenerate queue structure and issue IDs, which wipes triage
   491→    state and reorders the queue.  They only run at cycle boundaries
   492→    (pre-flight / post-flight).
   493→    """
   494→    dirty = False
   495→    mid_cycle = _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state) or force_rescan
   496→
   497→    if _sync_subjective_and_log(
   498→        plan,
   499→        state,
   500→        policy=policy,
   501→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   502→    ):
   503→        dirty = True
   504→    if not mid_cycle:
   505→        if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   506→            plan,
   507→            state,
   508→            target_strict=target_strict,
   509→            policy=policy,
   510→        ):
   511→            dirty = True
   512→    else:
   513→        print(
   514→            colorize(
   515→                "  Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration to "
   516→                "preserve queue state.",
   517→                "dim",
   518→            )
   519→        )
   520→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   521→        dirty = True
   522→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   523→        dirty = True
   524→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   525→        dirty = True
   526→    if not force_rescan:
   527→        if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, state):
   528→            dirty = True
   529→        if _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, state):
   530→            dirty = True
   531→    if _sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log(plan, state):
   532→        dirty = True
   533→    return dirty
   534→
   535→
   536→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   537→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   538→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   539→    try:
   540→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   541→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   542→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   543→        return
   544→
   545→    force_rescan = getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False)
   546→
   547→    # Force-rescan: clear all cycle state first.  The user explicitly chose
   548→    # to start over, so triage stages, workflow items, subjective dimensions,
   549→    # and plan-start scores from the old cycle are all stale.
   550→    dirty = _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan) if force_rescan else False
   551→
   552→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan=plan, state=runtime.state) or dirty
   553→
   554→    # Policy must be computed after reconciliation, which mutates plan
   555→    # (supersede/prune resolved issues) before policy reads it.
   556→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   557→        runtime,
   558→        plan,
   559→    )
   560→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   561→        plan=plan,
   562→        state=runtime.state,
   563→        target_strict=target_strict,
   564→        policy=policy,
   565→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   566→        force_rescan=getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False),
   567→    ) or dirty
   568→
   569→    if dirty:
   570→        try:
   571→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   572→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   573→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   574→
```

   207→    )
   208→    return _ImportSyncInputs(
   209→        has_review_issue_delta=_review_delta_present(diff),
   210→        assessment_keys=frozenset(assessment_keys),
   211→        covered_ids=tuple(
   212→            f"subjective::{dim_key}"
   213→            for dim_key in sorted(assessment_keys)
   214→        ),
   215→    )
   216→
   217→
   218→def _record_workflow_change(
   219→    mutations: _PlanImportMutations,
   220→    result,
   221→    *,
   222→    workflow_id: str,
   223→    injected: bool = True,
   224→) -> None:
   225→    if not result.changes:
   226→        return
   227→    mutations.dirty = True
   228→    if injected:
   229→        mutations.workflow_injected_ids.append(workflow_id)
   230→
   231→
   232→def _sync_review_delta(
   233→    mutations: _PlanImportMutations,
   234→    plan: dict,
   235→    state: dict,
   236→    *,
   237→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility,
   238→    sync_inputs: _ImportSyncInputs,
   239→) -> None:
   240→    if not sync_inputs.has_review_issue_delta:
   241→        return
   242→    mutations.import_result = sync_plan_after_review_import(
   243→        plan,
   244→        state,
   245→        policy=policy,
   246→    )
   247→    if mutations.import_result is not None:
   248→        mutations.dirty = True
   249→
   250→
   251→def _sync_subjective_queue_after_import(
   252→    mutations: _PlanImportMutations,
   253→    plan: dict,
   254→    state: dict,
   255→    *,
   256→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility,
   257→    target_strict: float,
   258→    sync_inputs: _ImportSyncInputs,
   259→) -> None:
   260→    if not (sync_inputs.has_review_issue_delta or sync_inputs.assessment_keys):
   261→        return
   262→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   263→    mutations.stale_sync_result = sync_subjective_dimensions(
   264→        plan,
   265→        state,
   266→        policy=policy,
   267→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   268→    )
   269→    if mutations.stale_sync_result.changes:
   270→        mutations.dirty = True
   271→
   272→    mutations.auto_cluster_changes = int(auto_cluster_issues(
   273→        plan,
   274→        state,
   275→        target_strict=target_strict,
   276→        policy=policy,
   277→    ))
   278→    if mutations.auto_cluster_changes:
   279→        mutations.dirty = True
   280→
   281→
   282→def _append_workflow_log_entries(
   283→    plan: dict,
   284→    *,
   285→    communicate_result,
   286→    import_scores_result,
   287→    create_plan_result,
   288→) -> None:
   289→    if communicate_result.changes:
   290→        append_log_entry(
   291→            plan,
   292→            "sync_communicate_score",
   293→            actor="system",
   294→            detail={"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True},
   295→        )
   296→    if import_scores_result.changes:
   297→        injected = bool(getattr(import_scores_result, "injected", ()))
   298→        pruned = list(getattr(import_scores_result, "pruned", ()))
   299→        append_log_entry(
   300→            plan,
   301→            "sync_import_scores",
   302→            actor="system",
   303→            detail={
   304→                "trigger": "review_import",
   305→                "injected": injected,
   306→                "pruned": pruned,
   307→            },
   308→        )
   309→    if create_plan_result.changes:
   310→        append_log_entry(
   311→            plan,
   312→            "sync_create_plan",
   313→            actor="system",
   314→            detail={"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True},
   315→        )
   316→
   317→
   318→def _append_review_import_sync_log(
   319→    plan: dict,
   320→    diff: dict,
   321→    mutations: _PlanImportMutations,
   322→    *,
   323→    covered_ids: tuple[str, ...],
   324→) -> None:
   325→    if not (
   326→        mutations.import_result is not None
   327→        or mutations.workflow_injected_ids
   328→        or covered_ids
   329→    ):
   330→        return
   331→    import_result = mutations.import_result
   332→    stale_sync_result = mutations.stale_sync_result
   333→    append_log_entry(
   334→        plan,
   335→        "review_import_sync",
   336→        actor="system",
   337→        detail={
   338→            "trigger": "review_import",
   339→            "new_ids": sorted(import_result.new_ids) if import_result is not None else [],
   340→            "added_to_queue": (
   341→                import_result.added_to_queue if import_result is not None else []
   342→            ),
   343→            "workflow_injected_ids": mutations.workflow_injected_ids,
   344→            "triage_injected": (
   345→                import_result.triage_injected if import_result is not None else False
   346→            ),
   347→            "triage_injected_ids": (
   348→                import_result.triage_injected_ids if import_result is not None else []
   349→            ),
   350→            "triage_deferred": (
   351→                import_result.triage_deferred if import_result is not None else False
   352→            ),
   353→            "diff_new": diff.get("new", 0),
   354→            "diff_reopened": diff.get("reopened", 0),
   355→            "diff_auto_resolved": diff.get("auto_resolved", 0),
   356→            "stale_pruned_from_queue": (
   357→                import_result.stale_pruned_from_queue if import_result is not None else []
   358→            ),
   359→            "covered_subjective": list(covered_ids),
   360→            "stale_sync_injected": (
   361→                sorted(stale_sync_result.injected)
   362→                if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   363→            ),
   364→            "stale_sync_pruned": (
   365→                sorted(stale_sync_result.pruned)
   366→                if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   367→            ),
   368→            "auto_cluster_changes": mutations.auto_cluster_changes,
   369→        },
   370→    )
   371→
   372→
   373→def sync_plan_after_import(
   374→    state: dict,
   375→    diff: dict,
   376→    assessment_mode: str,
   377→    *,
   378→    request: PlanImportSyncRequest | None = None,
   379→) -> None:
   380→    """Apply issue/workflow syncs after import in one load/save cycle."""
   381→    try:
   382→        state_file = request.state_file if request is not None else None
   383→        config = request.config if request is not None else None
   384→        import_file = request.import_file if request is not None else None
   385→        import_payload = request.import_payload if request is not None else None
   386→
   387→        plan_path = None
   388→        target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   389→        if state_file is not None:
   390→            plan_path = plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
   391→        if not has_living_plan(plan_path):
   392→            return
   393→
   394→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   395→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   396→            state,
   397→            target_strict=target_strict,
   398→            plan=plan,
   399→        )
   400→        snapshot = score_snapshot(state)
   401→        current_scores = ScoreSnapshot(
   402→            strict=snapshot.strict,
   403→            overall=snapshot.overall,
   404→            objective=snapshot.objective,
   405→            verified=snapshot.verified,
   406→        )
   407→        trusted_score_import = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
   408→        communicate_result = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   409→            plan,
   410→            state,
   411→            policy=policy,
   412→            scores_just_imported=trusted_score_import,
   413→            current_scores=current_scores,
   414→        )
   415→        import_scores_result = sync_import_scores_needed(
   416→            plan,
   417→            state,
   418→            assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
   419→            import_file=import_file,
   420→            import_payload=import_payload,
   421→        )
   422→        create_plan_result = sync_create_plan_needed(
   423→            plan,
   424→            state,
   425→            policy=policy,
   426→        )
   427→
   428→        sync_inputs = _build_import_sync_inputs(diff, import_payload)
   429→        mutations = _PlanImportMutations()
   430→        _record_workflow_change(
   431→            mutations,
   432→            communicate_result,
   433→            workflow_id="workflow::communicate-score",
   434→        )
   435→        _record_workflow_change(
   436→            mutations,
   437→            import_scores_result,
   438→            workflow_id="workflow::import-scores",
   439→            injected=bool(getattr(import_scores_result, "injected", ())),
   440→        )
   441→        _record_workflow_change(
   442→            mutations,
   443→            create_plan_result,
   444→            workflow_id="workflow::create-plan",
   445→        )
   446→        _sync_review_delta(
   447→            mutations,
   448→            plan,
   449→            state,
   450→            policy=policy,
   451→            sync_inputs=sync_inputs,
   452→        )
   453→        _sync_subjective_queue_after_import(
   454→            mutations,
   455→            plan,
   456→            state,
   457→            policy=policy,
   458→            target_strict=target_strict,
   459→            sync_inputs=sync_inputs,
   460→        )
   461→
   462→        if mutations.dirty:
   463→            if _sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import(plan, state, policy=policy):
   464→                mutations.dirty = True
   465→            _append_workflow_log_entries(
   466→                plan,
   467→                communicate_result=communicate_result,
   468→                import_scores_result=import_scores_result,
   469→                create_plan_result=create_plan_result,
   470→            )
   471→            _append_review_import_sync_log(
   472→                plan,
   473→                diff,
   474→                mutations,
   475→                covered_ids=sync_inputs.covered_ids,
   476→            )
   477→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   478→
   479→        if mutations.import_result is not None:
   480→            _print_review_import_sync(
   481→                state,
   482→                mutations.import_result,
   483→                workflow_injected=bool(mutations.workflow_injected_ids),
   484→            )
   485→        _print_workflow_injected_message(mutations.workflow_injected_ids)
   486→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   487→        print(
   488→            colorize(
   489→                f"  Note: skipped plan sync after review import ({exc}).",
   490→                "dim",
   491→            )
   492→        )
   493→
   494→
   495→__all__ = ["PlanImportSyncRequest", "sync_plan_after_import"]
   496→
```

    43→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    44→class PendingImportScoresMeta:
    45→    """Normalized contract for the queued score-import workflow."""
    46→
    47→    timestamp: str = ""
    48→    import_file: str = ""
    49→    normalized_import_file: str = ""
    50→    packet_sha256: str = ""
    51→
    52→    @classmethod
    53→    def from_mapping(cls, raw: object) -> PendingImportScoresMeta | None:
    54→        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
    55→            return None
    56→        meta = cls(
    57→            timestamp=str(raw.get("timestamp", "")).strip(),
    58→            import_file=str(raw.get("import_file", "")).strip(),
    59→            normalized_import_file=str(raw.get("normalized_import_file", "")).strip(),
    60→            packet_sha256=str(raw.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip(),
    61→        )
    62→        return meta if any((meta.timestamp, meta.import_file, meta.packet_sha256)) else None
    63→
    64→    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, str]:
    65→        return {
    66→            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
    67→            "import_file": self.import_file,
    68→            "normalized_import_file": self.normalized_import_file,
    69→            "packet_sha256": self.packet_sha256,
    70→        }

    72→
    73→def _normalize_match_path(raw_path: object) -> str | None:
    74→    if not isinstance(raw_path, str) or not raw_path.strip():
    75→        return None
    76→    return str(Path(raw_path).expanduser().resolve(strict=False))

    78→
    79→def _latest_assessment_audit(
    80→    state: StateModel,
    81→    *,
    82→    modes: set[str],
    83→) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    84→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    85→    if not isinstance(audit, list):
    86→        return None
    87→    for entry in reversed(audit):
    88→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    89→            continue
    90→        mode = entry.get("mode")
    91→        if isinstance(mode, str) and mode in modes:
    92→            return entry
    93→    return None

    96→def _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
    97→    *,
    98→    import_file: str | None,
    99→    import_payload: dict[str, Any] | None,
   100→    issues_only_audit: dict[str, Any] | None,

   102→    provenance = {}
   103→    if isinstance(import_payload, dict):
   104→        raw_provenance = import_payload.get("provenance")
   105→        if isinstance(raw_provenance, dict):
   106→            provenance = raw_provenance
   107→    recorded_file = (
   108→        str(import_file).strip()
   109→        if isinstance(import_file, str) and import_file.strip()
   110→        else str(issues_only_audit.get("import_file", "")).strip()
   111→        if isinstance(issues_only_audit, dict)
   112→        else ""
   113→    )
   114→    timestamp = ""
   115→    if isinstance(issues_only_audit, dict):
   116→        timestamp = str(issues_only_audit.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   117→    return PendingImportScoresMeta(
   118→        timestamp=timestamp,
   119→        import_file=recorded_file,
   120→        normalized_import_file=_normalize_match_path(recorded_file) or "",
   121→        packet_sha256=str(provenance.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip(),
   122→    )
   123→
   124→
   125→def pending_import_scores_meta(
   126→    plan: PlanModel,
   127→    state: StateModel,
   128→) -> PendingImportScoresMeta | None:
   129→    """Return queued score-import metadata without mutating the plan."""
   130→    refresh_state = _get_refresh_state(plan)
   131→    if refresh_state is not None:
   132→        meta = PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(
   133→            refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   134→        )
   135→        if meta is not None:
   136→            return meta
   137→    issues_only_audit = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   138→    if issues_only_audit is None:
   139→        return None
   140→    return _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
   141→        import_file=str(issues_only_audit.get("import_file", "")),
   142→        import_payload=None,
   143→        issues_only_audit=issues_only_audit,
   144→    )
   145→
   146→
   147→def import_scores_meta_matches(
   148→    meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | dict[str, Any] | None,
   149→    *,
   150→    import_file: str,
   151→    import_payload: dict[str, Any],
   152→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   153→    """Return (matches, reason) for whether the import matches the pending batch.
   154→
   155→    Checks packet_sha256 first (strongest signal), falls back to normalized
   156→    file path.  Returns a single human-readable reason on mismatch.
   157→    """
   158→    normalized_meta = (
   159→        meta
   160→        if isinstance(meta, PendingImportScoresMeta)
   161→        else PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(meta)
   162→    )
   163→    if normalized_meta is None:
   164→        return True, ""
   165→
   166→    provenance = import_payload.get("provenance")
   167→    provenance_dict = provenance if isinstance(provenance, dict) else {}
   168→
   169→    expected_hash = normalized_meta.packet_sha256
   170→    current_hash = str(provenance_dict.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip()
   171→    if expected_hash and current_hash:
   172→        if current_hash == expected_hash:
   173→            return True, ""
   174→        return False, f"expected packet_sha256 {expected_hash}, got {current_hash}"
   175→
   176→    expected_file = normalized_meta.normalized_import_file
   177→    current_file = _normalize_match_path(import_file) or ""
   178→    if expected_file and current_file and current_file != expected_file:
   179→        return False, f"expected import file {normalized_meta.import_file}, got {import_file}"
   180→
   181→    return True, ""
   182→

   184→def _clear_pending_import_scores(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   185→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   186→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   187→        order[:] = [item for item in order if item != WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID]
   188→    refresh_state = _get_refresh_state(plan)
   189→    if refresh_state is not None:
   190→        refresh_state.pop(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY, None)
   191→
   192→
   193→def _pending_compare_timestamp(
   194→    pending_meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | None,
   195→    latest_issues_only: dict[str, Any],
   196→) -> str:
   197→    if pending_meta is not None and pending_meta.timestamp:
   198→        return pending_meta.timestamp
   199→    return str(latest_issues_only.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   200→
   201→
   202→def _pending_import_scores_stale(
   203→    *,
   204→    order: list[str],
   205→    pending_meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | None,
   206→    latest_issues_only: dict[str, Any] | None,
   207→    latest_trusted: dict[str, Any] | None,
   208→) -> bool:
   209→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in order:
   210→        return False
   211→    if latest_issues_only is None:
   212→        return True
   213→    if latest_trusted is None:
   214→        return False
   215→
   216→    latest_trusted_ts = str(latest_trusted.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   217→    compare_ts = _pending_compare_timestamp(pending_meta, latest_issues_only)
   218→    return bool(compare_ts and latest_trusted_ts and latest_trusted_ts >= compare_ts)

   235→def _no_unscored(
   236→    state: StateModel,
   237→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   238→) -> bool:
   239→    """Return True when no unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain."""
   240→    if policy is not None:

   242→    return not stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
   243→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,

   247→def _inject(plan: PlanModel, item_id: str) -> QueueSyncResult:
   248→    """Inject *item_id* into the workflow prefix and clear stale skip entries."""
   249→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   250→    if item_id not in order:
   251→        order.append(item_id)
   252→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
   253→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   254→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
   255→        skipped.pop(item_id, None)
   256→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=[item_id])
   257→
   258→
   259→_EMPTY = QueueSyncResult
   260→
   261→
   262→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   263→    plan: PlanModel,
   264→    state: StateModel,
   265→    *,
   266→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   267→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   268→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   269→
   270→    Injects when:
   271→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   272→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   273→
   274→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   275→    """
   276→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   277→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   278→
   279→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   280→        return _EMPTY()
   281→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   282→        return _EMPTY()
   283→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   284→
   285→
   286→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   287→    plan: PlanModel,
   288→    state: StateModel,
   289→    *,
   290→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   291→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   292→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   293→
   294→    Only injects when:
   295→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   296→    - At least one objective issue exists
   297→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   298→    - No triage stages are pending
   299→
   300→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   301→    """
   302→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   303→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   304→
   305→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   306→        return _EMPTY()
   307→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   308→        return _EMPTY()
   309→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   310→        return _EMPTY()
   311→
   312→    if not has_objective_backlog(state, policy):
   313→        return _EMPTY()
   314→
   315→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   316→
   317→
   318→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   319→    plan: PlanModel,
   320→    state: StateModel,
   321→    *,
   322→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   323→    import_file: str | None = None,
   324→    import_payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   325→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   326→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   327→
   328→    Only injects when:
   329→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   330→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   331→
   332→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   333→    """
   334→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   335→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   336→    refresh_state = _ensure_refresh_state(plan)
   337→    pending_meta = PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(
   338→        refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   339→    )
   340→    latest_issues_only = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   341→    latest_trusted = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes=_TRUSTED_ASSESSMENT_MODES)
   342→
   343→    if _pending_import_scores_stale(
   344→        order=order,
   345→        pending_meta=pending_meta,
   346→        latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   347→        latest_trusted=latest_trusted,
   348→    ):
   349→        _clear_pending_import_scores(plan)
   350→        return QueueSyncResult(pruned=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   351→
   352→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   353→        if assessment_mode == "issues_only":
   354→            _record_pending_import_scores(
   355→                refresh_state,
   356→                import_file=import_file,
   357→                import_payload=import_payload,
   358→                latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   359→            )
   360→            return QueueSyncResult(resurfaced=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   361→        return _EMPTY()
   362→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   363→        return _EMPTY()
   364→    result = _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   365→    _record_pending_import_scores(
   366→        refresh_state,
   367→        import_file=import_file,
   368→        import_payload=import_payload,
   369→        latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   370→    )
   371→    return result
   372→
   373→
   374→class ScoreSnapshot:
   375→    """Minimal score snapshot for rebaseline — avoids importing state.py."""
   376→
   377→    __slots__ = ("strict", "overall", "objective", "verified")
   378→
   379→    def __init__(
   380→        self,
   381→        *,
   382→        strict: float | None,
   383→        overall: float | None,
   384→        objective: float | None,
   385→        verified: float | None,
   386→    ) -> None:
   387→        self.strict = strict
   388→        self.overall = overall
   389→        self.objective = objective
   390→        self.verified = verified
   391→
   392→
   393→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   394→    plan: PlanModel,
   395→    state: StateModel,
   396→    *,
   397→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   398→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   399→    current_scores: ScoreSnapshot | None = None,
   400→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   401→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` and rebaseline scores.
   402→
   403→    Injects when:
   404→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dims), OR
   405→      scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   406→    - ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue
   407→    - Score has not already been communicated this cycle
   408→      (``previous_plan_start_scores`` absent), unless a trusted score import
   409→      explicitly refreshed the live score mid-cycle
   410→
   411→    When injected and *current_scores* is provided, ``plan_start_scores``
   412→    is rebaselined to the current score so the score display unfreezes at
   413→    the new value.  The previous baseline is preserved in
   414→    ``previous_plan_start_scores`` so the communicate-score queue item can
   415→    show the old → new delta — and so mid-cycle scans know not to
   416→    re-inject.
   417→    """
   418→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   419→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   420→
   421→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order:
   422→        return _EMPTY()
   423→    # Already communicated this cycle — previous_plan_start_scores is set
   424→    # at injection time and cleared at cycle boundaries.
   425→    if "previous_plan_start_scores" in plan and not scores_just_imported:
   426→        return _EMPTY()
   427→    if not scores_just_imported and not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   428→        return _EMPTY()
   429→
   430→    if current_scores is not None:
   431→        _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(plan, current_scores)
   432→    # Set sentinel even when rebaseline was a no-op (no plan_start_scores
   433→    # to rebaseline) so mid-cycle scans don't re-inject.
   434→    if not plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores"):
   435→        plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = {}
   436→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   437→
   438→
   439→def _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(
   440→    plan: PlanModel,
   441→    scores: ScoreSnapshot,
   442→) -> None:
   443→    """Snapshot the current score as the new baseline, preserving the old one."""
   444→    old_start = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   445→    if not isinstance(old_start, dict) or not old_start:
   446→        return
   447→    if scores.strict is None:
   448→        return
   449→
   450→    plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = dict(old_start)
   451→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   452→        "strict": scores.strict,
   453→        "overall": scores.overall,
   454→        "objective": scores.objective,
   455→        "verified": scores.verified,
   456→    }
   457→
   458→
   459→__all__ = [
   460→    "PendingImportScoresMeta",
   461→    "ScoreSnapshot",
   462→    "import_scores_meta_matches",
   463→    "pending_import_scores_meta",
   464→    "sync_communicate_score_needed",
   465→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   466→    "sync_import_scores_needed",
   467→    "sync_score_checkpoint_needed",
   468→]
   469→

1→"""Triage sync — inject/prune triage stage IDs based on review issue changes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (

    34→
    35→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
    36→    state: StateModel,
    37→    meta: dict,
    38→) -> set[str]:
    39→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
    40→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    41→    active_ids = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
    42→    known_ids = triaged_ids | active_ids
    43→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state) - known_ids if known_ids else set()
    44→
    45→
    46→def _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta: dict) -> set[str]:
    47→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    48→    active_ids = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
    49→    return triaged_ids | active_ids
    50→
    51→
    52→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
    53→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
    54→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    55→        while sid in order:
    56→            order.remove(sid)
    57→
    58→
    59→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
    60→    order: list[str],
    61→    confirmed: set[str],
    62→    *,
    63→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    64→) -> list[str]:
    65→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
    66→
    67→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
    68→    Returns list of injected stage IDs.
    69→    """
    70→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
    71→    existing = set(order)
    72→    injected: list[str] = []
    73→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
    74→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
    75→            if skipped is not None:
    76→                skipped.pop(sid, None)
    77→            order.append(sid)
    78→            injected.append(sid)
    79→            existing.add(sid)
    80→    return injected
    81→
    82→
    83→def _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta: dict) -> None:
    84→    """Clear defer metadata once triage is no longer deferred."""
    85→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
    86→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
    87→
    88→
    89→def _store_triage_meta(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
    90→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
    91→
    92→
    93→def _inject_triage_result(
    94→    order: list[str],
    95→    confirmed: set[str],
    96→    *,
    97→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    98→    normalize: bool = False,
    99→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   100→    injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, confirmed, skipped=skipped)
   101→    if not injected:
   102→        return QueueSyncResult()
   103→    if normalize:
   104→        normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
   105→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=injected)

   107→
   108→def _mark_triage_deferred(
   109→    plan: PlanModel,
   110→    meta: dict,
   111→    *,
   112→    defer_state: dict,
   113→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   114→    meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   115→    meta["triage_recommended"] = True
   116→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
   117→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   118→    return QueueSyncResult(deferred=True)
   119→
   120→
   121→def _mark_triage_escalated(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   122→    meta[_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY] = True
   123→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   124→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   125→
   126→
   127→def _mark_triage_ready(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   128→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   129→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   130→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   131→
   132→
   133→def _prune_stale_present_stages(
   134→    *,
   135→    plan: PlanModel,
   136→    state: StateModel,
   137→    order: list[str],
   138→    meta: dict,
   139→    last_hash: str,
   140→    confirmed: set[str],
   141→    recorded_unconfirmed: set[str],
   142→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   143→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   144→    if not last_hash or confirmed or recorded_unconfirmed:
   145→        return result
   146→    if not _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta) and stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state):
   147→        return result
   148→    new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   149→    if new_since_triage:
   150→        return result
   151→    _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   152→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   153→    current_hash = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   154→    if current_hash:
   155→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   156→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   157→    result.pruned = list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
   158→    return result
   159→
   160→
   161→def _backfill_partial_triage_snapshot(
   162→    *,
   163→    plan: PlanModel,
   164→    state: StateModel,
   165→    meta: dict,
   166→    last_hash: str,
   167→) -> None:
   168→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   169→    has_completed_stage = any(
   170→        isinstance(v, dict) and v.get("confirmed_at")
   171→        for v in stages.values()
   172→    )
   173→    if not has_completed_stage or meta.get("triaged_ids") or last_hash:
   174→        return
   175→    current_review = sorted(stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state))
   176→    if current_review:
   177→        meta["triaged_ids"] = current_review
   178→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   179→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   180→
   181→
   182→def _defer_or_inject_triage(
   183→    *,
   184→    plan: PlanModel,
   185→    state: StateModel,
   186→    order: list[str],
   187→    meta: dict,
   188→    confirmed: set[str],
   189→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   190→    new_since_triage: set[str],
   191→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   192→    decision = decide_triage_start(
   193→        plan,
   194→        state,
   195→        policy=policy,
   196→        explicit_start=False,
   197→        attested_override=False,
   198→    )
   199→    if decision.action == "defer":
   200→        defer_state = update_defer_state(
   201→            meta.get(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY),
   202→            state=state,
   203→            deferred_ids=new_since_triage,
   204→            options=DeferUpdateOptions(
   205→                deferred_ids_field=_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   206→            ),
   207→        )
   208→        meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   209→        escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   210→            defer_state,
   211→            state=state,
   212→            options=DeferEscalationOptions(
   213→                deferred_ids_field=_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   214→            ),
   215→        )
   216→        if not escalated:
   217→            return _mark_triage_deferred(plan, meta, defer_state=defer_state)
   218→        _mark_triage_escalated(plan, meta)
   219→        return _inject_triage_result(
   220→            order,
   221→            confirmed,
   222→            skipped=plan.get("skipped", {}),
   223→            normalize=True,
   224→        )
   225→
   226→    _mark_triage_ready(plan, meta)
   227→    return _inject_triage_result(
   228→        order,
   229→        confirmed,
   230→        skipped=plan.get("skipped", {}),
   231→    )
   232→
   233→
   234→def _sync_hash_change(
   235→    *,
   236→    plan: PlanModel,
   237→    state: StateModel,
   238→    order: list[str],
   239→    meta: dict,
   240→    confirmed: set[str],
   241→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   242→    current_hash: str,
   243→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   244→    new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   245→    if not new_since_triage and not _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta):
   246→        new_since_triage = stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state)
   247→    if new_since_triage:
   248→        return _defer_or_inject_triage(
   249→            plan=plan,
   250→            state=state,
   251→            order=order,
   252→            meta=meta,
   253→            confirmed=confirmed,
   254→            policy=policy,
   255→            new_since_triage=new_since_triage,
   256→        )
   257→
   258→    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   259→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   260→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   261→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   262→    return QueueSyncResult()

   264→
   265→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   266→# Public API
   267→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   268→
   269→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   270→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   271→
   272→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   273→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   274→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   275→    resolved since then.
   276→
   277→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   278→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   279→    through the plan.
   280→    """
   281→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   282→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state)
   283→
   284→
   285→def compute_open_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   286→    """Return the set of currently-open review/concerns issue IDs."""
   287→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state)
   288→
   289→
   290→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   291→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   292→
   293→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   294→    """
   295→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   296→
   297→
   298→def sync_triage_needed(
   299→    plan: PlanModel,
   300→    state: StateModel,
   301→    *,
   302→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   303→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   304→    """Append triage stage IDs to back of queue when review issues change.
   305→
   306→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   307→
   308→    **Mid-cycle guard**: when the objective backlog still has work, triage
   309→    stages are NOT injected.  Instead, ``epic_triage_meta["triage_recommended"]``
   310→    is set so the UI can show a non-blocking banner.  Stages are injected
   311→    once the objective backlog drains (or on manual ``plan triage``). If
   312→    deferral repeats for multiple scans/days, triage escalates and stages are
   313→    injected in workflow/triage priority order with a forced-visibility marker.
   314→
   315→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   316→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   317→
   318→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   319→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   320→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   321→    """
   322→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   323→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   324→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   325→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   326→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   327→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   328→
   329→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   330→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   331→
   332→    current_hash = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   333→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   334→
   335→    if already_present:
   336→        return _prune_stale_present_stages(
   337→            plan=plan,
   338→            state=state,
   339→            order=order,
   340→            meta=meta,
   341→            last_hash=last_hash,
   342→            confirmed=confirmed,
   343→            recorded_unconfirmed=recorded_unconfirmed,
   344→        )
   345→
   346→    _backfill_partial_triage_snapshot(
   347→        plan=plan,
   348→        state=state,
   349→        meta=meta,
   350→        last_hash=last_hash,
   351→    )
   352→
   353→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   354→        return _sync_hash_change(
   355→            plan=plan,
   356→            state=state,
   357→            order=order,
   358→            meta=meta,
   359→            confirmed=confirmed,
   360→            policy=policy,
   361→            current_hash=current_hash,
   362→        )
   363→
   364→    return result
   365→
   366→
   367→__all__ = [
   368→    "compute_open_issue_ids",
   369→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   370→    "is_triage_stale",
   371→    "sync_triage_needed",
   372→]
   373→

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Single-pass sync for all three subjective categories:
     4→
     5→- **unscored** — placeholder dimensions never yet reviewed.  Injected at cycle
     6→  boundaries only; pruned mid-cycle.
     7→- **stale** — dimensions whose assessment needs a review refresh.
     8→- **under_target** — scored below target but not stale.
     9→
    10→Stale and under-target IDs share injection rules: injected when no objective
    11→backlog exists, evicted when it does, force-promoted after repeated deferrals.
    12→
    13→Invariant: new items are always appended — sync never reorders existing queue
    14→(except escalation promotion, which moves deferred IDs ahead of objective work).
    15→"""

    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
    21→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, QueueSyncResult
    22→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    23→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    24→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel

    27→from .defer_policy import (
    28→    DeferEscalationOptions,
    29→    DeferUpdateOptions,
    30→    should_escalate_defer_state,
    31→    update_defer_state,
    32→)
    33→
    34→
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→# Defer-meta keys (shared with work_queue readers via plan dict)
    37→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    38→
    39→[REDACTED]
    40→_DEFER_IDS_FIELD = "deferred_review_ids"
    41→[REDACTED]
    42→
    43→
    44→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    45→# ID helpers
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→
    48→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    49→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    50→
    51→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    52→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    53→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    54→    """
    55→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    56→        state,
    57→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→def current_under_target_ids(
    62→    state: StateModel,
    63→    *,
    64→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    65→) -> set[str]:
    66→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
    67→
    68→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
    69→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
    70→    """
    71→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
    72→        state,
    73→        target_strict=target_strict,
    74→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    75→    )
    76→
    77→
    78→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    79→# Helpers
    80→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    81→
    82→def _prune_subjective_ids(
    83→    order: list[str],
    84→    *,
    85→    keep_ids: set[str],
    86→    pruned: list[str],
    87→) -> None:
    88→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
    89→    to_remove = [
    90→        fid for fid in order
    91→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    92→        and fid not in keep_ids
    93→    ]
    94→    for fid in to_remove:
    95→        order.remove(fid)
    96→        pruned.append(fid)
    97→
    98→
    99→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> set[str]:
   100→    """Return subjective IDs that are currently skipped in the plan."""
   101→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   102→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
   103→        return set()
   104→    return {
   105→        str(fid)
   106→        for fid in skipped
   107→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   108→    }
   109→
   110→
   111→def _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(
   112→    order: list[str],
   113→    *,
   114→    skipped_ids: set[str],
   115→    pruned: list[str],
   116→) -> None:
   117→    """Repair invalid overlap by removing skipped subjective IDs from queue order."""
   118→    if not skipped_ids:
   119→        return
   120→    to_remove = [fid for fid in order if fid in skipped_ids]
   121→    for fid in to_remove:
   122→        order.remove(fid)
   123→        pruned.append(fid)
   124→
   125→
   126→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   127→    order: list[str],
   128→    *,
   129→    inject_ids: set[str],
   130→    injected: list[str],
   131→) -> None:
   132→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   133→
   134→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   135→    """
   136→    existing = set(order)
   137→    for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   138→        if sid not in existing:
   139→            order.append(sid)
   140→            injected.append(sid)
   141→
   142→
   143→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   144→# Deferral tracking and escalation
   145→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   146→
   147→def _clear_defer_meta(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   148→    plan.pop(_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
   149→
   150→
   151→def _promote_subjective_ids(order: list[str], ids: list[str]) -> int:
   152→    """Move subjective IDs ahead of objective backlog while preserving order.
   153→
   154→    Inserts after any workflow/triage items at the front of the queue.
   155→    """
   156→    target_ids: list[str] = []
   157→    seen: set[str] = set()
   158→    for fid in ids:
   159→        sid = str(fid).strip()
   160→        if not sid or sid in seen:
   161→            continue
   162→        target_ids.append(sid)
   163→        seen.add(sid)
   164→    if not target_ids:
   165→        return 0
   166→
   167→    insert_at = 0
   168→    while insert_at < len(order):
   169→        current = str(order[insert_at])
   170→        if not (
   171→            current.startswith("workflow::") or current.startswith("triage::")
   172→        ):
   173→            break
   174→        insert_at += 1
   175→
   176→    changes = 0
   177→    for sid in target_ids:
   178→        existing_idx = order.index(sid) if sid in order else None
   179→        target_idx = min(insert_at, len(order))
   180→        if existing_idx is not None:
   181→            if existing_idx == target_idx:
   182→                insert_at += 1
   183→                continue
   184→            order.pop(existing_idx)
   185→            if existing_idx < target_idx:
   186→                target_idx -= 1
   187→        order.insert(target_idx, sid)
   188→        insert_at = target_idx + 1
   189→        changes += 1
   190→    return changes
   191→
   192→
   193→def _update_deferral(
   194→    plan: PlanModel,
   195→    state: StateModel,
   196→    *,
   197→    injectable_ids: set[str],
   198→    stale_ids: set[str],
   199→    under_target_ids: set[str],
   200→    skipped_ids: set[str],
   201→) -> bool:
   202→    """Update defer-meta and return True if escalation threshold is reached."""
   203→    stale_deferred = sorted(stale_ids - skipped_ids)
   204→    under_target_deferred = sorted(under_target_ids - skipped_ids)
   205→
   206→    defer_state = update_defer_state(
   207→        plan.get(_DEFER_META_KEY),
   208→        state=state,
   209→        deferred_ids=injectable_ids,
   210→        options=DeferUpdateOptions(deferred_ids_field=_DEFER_IDS_FIELD),
   211→    )
   212→    defer_state["deferred_stale_ids"] = stale_deferred
   213→    defer_state["deferred_under_target_ids"] = under_target_deferred
   214→
   215→    escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   216→        defer_state,
   217→        state=state,
   218→        options=DeferEscalationOptions(deferred_ids_field=_DEFER_IDS_FIELD),
   219→    )
   220→
   221→    if escalated:
   222→        defer_state[_FORCE_IDS_KEY] = sorted(injectable_ids)
   223→    else:
   224→        defer_state.pop(_FORCE_IDS_KEY, None)
   225→
   226→    plan[_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   227→    return escalated
   228→
   229→
   230→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   231→# Unified subjective dimension sync
   232→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   233→
   234→def sync_subjective_dimensions(
   235→    plan: PlanModel,
   236→    state: StateModel,
   237→    *,
   238→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   239→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   240→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   241→    """Single-pass sync for all subjective dimensions in the plan queue.
   242→
   243→    Handles three categories with unified prune/inject logic:
   244→
   245→    - **unscored**: placeholder dimensions not yet reviewed.  Injected at cycle
   246→      boundaries; pruned mid-cycle.
   247→    - **stale**: dimensions needing review refresh.
   248→    - **under_target**: scored below target but not stale.
   249→
   250→    Stale/under-target injection is gated by objective backlog:
   251→
   252→    1. **No backlog** (or cycle just completed) — inject to back of queue.
   253→    2. **Backlog, not escalated** — evict from queue.  Update deferral counter.
   254→    3. **Backlog, escalated** — force-promote to front (after workflow/triage).
   255→
   256→    Unscored injection is unconditional at cycle boundaries, independent of
   257→    backlog state.
   258→    """
   259→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   260→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   261→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   262→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   263→
   264→    # --- Compute all ID sets once -----------------------------------------
   265→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   266→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   267→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   268→    )
   269→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   270→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   271→    injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids
   272→
   273→    # --- Resurface: clear skips for never-reviewed dimensions -------------
   274→    if not mid_cycle:
   275→        skipped_dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   276→        if isinstance(skipped_dict, dict):
   277→            for sid in sorted(unscored_ids & skipped_ids):
   278→                skipped_dict.pop(sid, None)
   279→                result.resurfaced.append(sid)
   280→            skipped_ids -= unscored_ids
   281→
   282→    # --- Repair: remove skipped IDs from queue_order ----------------------
   283→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   284→
   285→    # --- Backlog and deferral ---------------------------------------------
   286→    objective_backlog = has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   287→    should_defer = objective_backlog and not cycle_just_completed
   288→    escalated = False
   289→    if should_defer and injectable_ids:
   290→        escalated = _update_deferral(
   291→            plan, state,
   292→            injectable_ids=injectable_ids,
   293→            stale_ids=stale_ids,
   294→            under_target_ids=under_target_ids,
   295→            skipped_ids=skipped_ids,
   296→        )
   297→    else:
   298→        _clear_defer_meta(plan)
   299→
   300→    # --- Single prune pass ------------------------------------------------
   301→    evicting = should_defer and not escalated
   302→    if evicting:
   303→        keep_ids = set() if mid_cycle else unscored_ids
   304→    else:
   305→        keep_ids = injectable_ids if mid_cycle else (unscored_ids | injectable_ids)
   306→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=keep_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   307→
   308→    # --- Inject unscored (cycle boundaries only) --------------------------
   309→    if not mid_cycle:
   310→        _inject_subjective_ids(
   311→            order,
   312→            inject_ids=unscored_ids - skipped_ids,
   313→            injected=result.injected,
   314→        )
   315→
   316→    # --- Inject or promote stale/under_target -----------------------------
   317→    if escalated:
   318→        _promote_subjective_ids(order, sorted(injectable_ids))
   319→    elif not evicting and injectable_ids:
   320→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   321→
   322→    return result
   323→
   324→
   325→__all__ = [
   326→    "current_under_target_ids",
   327→    "current_unscored_ids",
   328→    "sync_subjective_dimensions",
   329→]
   330→
```

1→"""Helpers for the persisted queue lifecycle phase.
     2→
     3→The persisted phase is the normal source of truth for CLI/debugging, but it is
     4→not trusted blindly. Queue assembly still re-resolves the active phase from the
     5→currently visible items as a safety net so stale saved state cannot strand the
     6→user in the wrong phase after out-of-band changes.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from typing import Iterable
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→
    16→[REDACTED]
    17→_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY = "lifecycle_phase"
    18→
    19→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    20→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW = "review"
    21→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW = "workflow"
    22→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE = "triage"
    23→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    24→VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES = frozenset(
    25→    {
    26→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    27→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    28→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    29→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    30→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    31→    }
    32→)
    33→
    34→
    35→def _refresh_state(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, object]:
    36→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    37→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    38→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    39→        refresh_state = {}
    40→        plan["refresh_state"] = refresh_state
    41→    return refresh_state
    42→
    43→
    44→def _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    45→    return not any(str(issue_id).startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
    46→
    47→
    48→def current_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel) -> str | None:
    49→    """Return the persisted lifecycle phase, falling back for legacy plans."""
    50→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    51→    if isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    52→        phase = refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY)
    53→        if isinstance(phase, str) and phase in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    54→            return phase
    55→    if postflight_scan_pending(plan):
    56→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
    57→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    58→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
    59→    return None
    60→
    61→
    62→def set_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel, phase: str) -> bool:
    63→    """Persist the current queue lifecycle phase."""
    64→    if phase not in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    65→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported lifecycle phase: {phase}")
    66→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
    67→    if refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY) == phase:
    68→        return False
    69→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = phase
    70→    return True
    71→
    72→
    73→def sync_lifecycle_phase(
    74→    plan: PlanModel,
    75→    *,
    76→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
    77→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
    78→    has_postflight_review: bool,
    79→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
    80→    has_triage: bool,
    81→    has_deferred: bool,
    82→) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    83→    """Resolve and persist the current lifecycle phase from queue-state facts."""
    84→    phase = resolve_lifecycle_phase(
    85→        plan,
    86→        has_initial_reviews=has_initial_reviews,
    87→        has_objective_backlog=has_objective_backlog,
    88→        has_postflight_review=has_postflight_review,
    89→        has_postflight_workflow=has_postflight_workflow,
    90→        has_triage=has_triage,
    91→        has_deferred=has_deferred,
    92→    )
    93→    return phase, set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
    94→
    95→
    96→def resolve_lifecycle_phase(
    97→    plan: PlanModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
   100→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
   101→    has_postflight_review: bool,
   102→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
   103→    has_triage: bool,
   104→    has_deferred: bool,
   105→) -> str:
   106→    """Resolve the lifecycle phase from explicit queue-state facts."""
   107→    if has_initial_reviews:
   108→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   109→    if has_objective_backlog:
   110→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   111→    if has_deferred or postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   112→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   113→    if has_postflight_review:
   114→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   115→    if has_postflight_workflow:
   116→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW
   117→    if has_triage:
   118→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE
   119→    persisted = current_lifecycle_phase(plan)
   120→    if persisted is not None:
   121→        return persisted
   122→    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   123→
   124→
   125→def postflight_scan_pending(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
   126→    """Return True when the current empty-queue boundary still needs a scan."""
   127→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
   128→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
   129→        return True
   130→    return not isinstance(refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY), int)
   131→
   132→
   133→def mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   134→    plan: PlanModel,
   135→    *,
   136→    scan_count: int | None,
   137→) -> bool:
   138→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current refresh cycle."""
   139→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   140→    try:
   141→        normalized_scan_count = int(scan_count or 0)
   142→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   143→        normalized_scan_count = 0
   144→    if refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY) == normalized_scan_count:
   145→        return False
   146→    refresh_state[_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY] = normalized_scan_count
   147→    return True
   148→
   149→
   150→def clear_postflight_scan_completion(
   151→    plan: PlanModel,
   152→    *,
   153→    issue_ids: Iterable[str] | None = None,
   154→) -> bool:
   155→    """Require a fresh scan after queue-changing work on real issues."""
   156→    if issue_ids is not None and not any(
   157→        _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids
   158→    ):
   159→        return False
   160→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   161→    if _POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY not in refresh_state:
   162→        return False
   163→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   164→    refresh_state.pop(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY, None)
   165→    return True
   166→
   167→
   168→__all__ = [
   169→    "clear_postflight_scan_completion",
   170→    "current_lifecycle_phase",
   171→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE",
   172→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW",
   173→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN",
   174→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE",
   175→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW",
   176→    "mark_postflight_scan_completed",
   177→    "postflight_scan_pending",
   178→    "resolve_lifecycle_phase",
   179→    "set_lifecycle_phase",
   180→    "sync_lifecycle_phase",
   181→    "VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES",
   182→]
   183→
```

    68→def _option_value(options: object | None, name: str, default: Any) -> Any:
    69→    if options is None:
    70→        return default
    71→    return getattr(options, name, default)
    72→
    73→
    74→def _resolved_scan_path(options: object | None, state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    75→    scan_path = _option_value(options, "scan_path", state.get("scan_path"))
    76→    if hasattr(scan_path, "__class__") and scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState":
    77→        return state.get("scan_path")
    78→    return scan_path
    79→
    80→
    81→def _is_fresh_boundary(plan: dict | None) -> bool:
    82→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    83→        return True
    84→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    85→    if not scores:
    86→        return True
    87→    return isinstance(scores, dict) and bool(scores.get("reset"))
    88→

    90→def _is_objective_item(item: WorkQueueItem, *, skipped_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    91→    return (
    92→        item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
    93→        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    94→        and item.get("id", "") not in skipped_ids
