```
    98→def _review_issue_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    99→    return [
   100→        item for item in items
   101→        if item.get("detector", "") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
   102→    ]
   103→
   104→
   105→def _executable_review_issue_items(
   106→    plan: dict | None,
   107→    state: StateModel,
   108→    review_issue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   109→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   110→    """Hide raw review findings until triage is current for the live issue set."""
   111→    if not review_issue_items or not isinstance(plan, dict):
   112→        return review_issue_items
   113→
   114→    triage_snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(plan, state)
   115→    if triage_snapshot.has_triage_in_queue:
   116→        return []
   117→    if triage_snapshot.is_triage_stale:
   118→        return []
   119→    if not triage_snapshot.triage_has_run:
   120→        return []
   121→    return review_issue_items
   122→
   123→
   124→def _subjective_partitions(
   125→    state: StateModel,
   126→    *,
   127→    scoped_issues: dict[str, dict],
   128→    threshold: float,
   129→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   130→    candidates = build_subjective_items(state, scoped_issues, threshold=threshold)
   131→    initial = [item for item in candidates if item.get("initial_review")]
   132→    postflight = [item for item in candidates if not item.get("initial_review")]
   133→    return initial, postflight
   134→
   135→
   136→def _workflow_partitions(
   137→    plan: dict | None,
   138→    state: StateModel,
   139→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   140→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   141→        return [], [], []
   142→    scan_items = [
   143→        item
   144→        for item in (
   145→            build_deferred_disposition_item(plan),
   146→            build_run_scan_item(plan),
   147→        )
   148→        if item is not None
   149→    ]
   150→    postflight_workflow = [
   151→        item
   152→        for item in (
   153→            build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state),
   154→            build_import_scores_item(plan, state),
   155→            build_communicate_score_item(plan, state),
   156→            build_create_plan_item(plan),
   157→        )
   158→        if item is not None
   159→    ]
   160→    triage_items = build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)
   161→    return scan_items, postflight_workflow, triage_items
   162→
   163→
   164→def _phase_for_snapshot(
   165→    *,
   166→    fresh_boundary: bool,
   167→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   168→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   169→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   170→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   171→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   172→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   173→) -> str:
   174→    if fresh_boundary and initial_review_items:
   175→        return PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   176→    if objective_items:
   177→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   178→    if scan_items:
   179→        return PHASE_SCAN
   180→    if postflight_review_items:
   181→        return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   182→    if postflight_workflow_items:
   183→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   184→    if triage_items:
   185→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   186→    return PHASE_SCAN
   187→
   188→
   189→def _execution_items_for_phase(
   190→    phase: str,
   191→    *,
   192→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   193→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   194→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   195→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   196→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   197→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   198→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   199→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL:
   200→        return initial_review_items
   201→    if phase == PHASE_EXECUTE:
   202→        return objective_items
   203→    if phase == PHASE_SCAN:
   204→        deferred_items = [
   205→            item for item in scan_items
   206→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID
   207→        ]
   208→        if deferred_items:
   209→            return deferred_items
   210→        return [
   211→            item for item in scan_items
   212→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
   213→        ]
   214→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   215→        return postflight_review_items
   216→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   217→        return postflight_workflow_items
   218→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   219→        return triage_items
   220→    return []
   221→
   222→
   223→def build_queue_snapshot(
   224→    state: StateModel,
   225→    *,
   226→    options: object | None = None,
   227→    plan: dict | None = None,
   228→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   229→) -> QueueSnapshot:
   230→    """Build the canonical queue snapshot for the current state."""
   231→    context = _option_value(options, "context", None)
   232→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else (
   233→        plan if plan is not None else _option_value(options, "plan", None)
   234→    )
   235→    scan_path = _resolved_scan_path(options, state)
   236→    skipped_ids = set((effective_plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   237→    scoped_issues = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   238→    scope = _option_value(options, "scope", None)
   239→    chronic = bool(_option_value(options, "chronic", False))
   240→
   241→    all_issue_items = build_issue_items(
   242→        state,
   243→        scan_path=scan_path,
   244→        status_filter="open",
   245→        scope=scope,
   246→        chronic=chronic,
   247→    )
   248→    objective_items = [
   249→        item for item in all_issue_items
   250→        if _is_objective_item(item, skipped_ids=skipped_ids)
   251→    ]
   252→    planned_ids = _planned_objective_ids(
   253→        {item.get("id", "") for item in objective_items},
   254→        effective_plan,
   255→    )
   256→    planned_objective_items = [
   257→        item for item in objective_items
   258→        if item.get("id", "") in planned_ids
   259→    ]
   260→    review_issue_items = _review_issue_items(all_issue_items)
   261→    executable_review_items = _executable_review_issue_items(
   262→        effective_plan,
   263→        state,
   264→        review_issue_items,
   265→    )
   266→    initial_review_items, subjective_postflight_items = _subjective_partitions(
   267→        state,
   268→        scoped_issues=scoped_issues,
   269→        threshold=target_strict,
   270→    )
   271→    postflight_review_items = [*subjective_postflight_items, *executable_review_items]
   272→    scan_items, postflight_workflow_items, triage_items = _workflow_partitions(
   273→        effective_plan,
   274→        state,
   275→    )
   276→
   277→    fresh_boundary = _is_fresh_boundary(effective_plan)
   278→    phase = _phase_for_snapshot(
   279→        fresh_boundary=fresh_boundary,
   280→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   281→        objective_items=planned_objective_items,
   282→        scan_items=scan_items,
   283→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   284→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   285→        triage_items=triage_items,
   286→    )
   287→    execution_items = _execution_items_for_phase(
   288→        phase,
   289→        objective_items=planned_objective_items,
   290→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   291→        scan_items=scan_items,
   292→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   293→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   294→        triage_items=triage_items,
   295→    )
   296→
   297→    execution_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in execution_items}
   298→    backlog_items = [
   299→        item for item in (
   300→            [
   301→                *planned_objective_items,
   302→                *initial_review_items,
   303→                *subjective_postflight_items,
   304→                *review_issue_items,
   305→                *scan_items,
   306→                *postflight_workflow_items,
   307→                *triage_items,
   308→            ]
   309→        )
   310→        if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   311→    ]
   312→    objective_backlog_count = sum(
   313→        1 for item in planned_objective_items if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   314→    )
   315→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers = len(planned_objective_items) < len(objective_items)
   316→
   317→    return QueueSnapshot(
   318→        phase=phase,
   319→        all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items),
   320→        all_initial_review_items=tuple(initial_review_items),
   321→        all_postflight_review_items=tuple(postflight_review_items),
   322→        all_scan_items=tuple(scan_items),
   323→        all_postflight_workflow_items=tuple(postflight_workflow_items),
   324→        all_postflight_triage_items=tuple(triage_items),
   325→        execution_items=tuple(execution_items),
   326→        backlog_items=tuple(backlog_items),
   327→        objective_in_scope_count=len(objective_items),
   328→        planned_objective_count=len(planned_objective_items),
   329→        objective_execution_count=sum(
   330→            1
   331→            for item in execution_items
   332→            if item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   333→            and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   334→        ),
   335→        objective_backlog_count=objective_backlog_count,
   336→        subjective_initial_count=len(initial_review_items),
   337→        subjective_postflight_count=len(postflight_review_items),
   338→        workflow_postflight_count=len(postflight_workflow_items),
   339→        triage_pending_count=len(triage_items),
   340→        has_unplanned_objective_blockers=has_unplanned_objective_blockers,
   341→    )
   342→
   343→
   344→def coarse_phase_name(phase: str) -> str:
   345→    """Map internal queue phases to the persisted coarse lifecycle value."""
   346→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL or phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   347→        return "review"
   348→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   349→        return "workflow"
   350→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   351→        return "triage"
   352→    return phase
   353→
   354→
   355→__all__ = [
   356→    "PHASE_EXECUTE",
   357→    "PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL",
   358→    "PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT",
   359→    "PHASE_SCAN",
   360→    "PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT",
   361→    "PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT",
   362→    "QueueSnapshot",
   363→    "build_queue_snapshot",
   364→    "coarse_phase_name",
   365→]
   366→
```

```
1→"""Shared helpers for queue-sync decisions.
     2→
     3→Every sync module (``sync_triage``, ``sync_dimensions``, ``sync_workflow``,
     4→``auto_cluster_sync``) needs to answer two questions:
     5→
     6→1. **Is there an objective backlog?**  (Should we defer optional items?)
     7→2. **Are we mid-cycle?**  (Has a queue cycle started and not yet completed?)
     8→
     9→This module provides a single definition of each so the logic stays
    10→consistent and changes propagate everywhere at once.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    17→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→def has_objective_backlog(
    26→    state_or_issues: StateModel | dict,
    27→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    28→) -> bool:
    29→    """Return whether a planned objective backlog exists.
    30→
    31→    Prefers the pre-computed *policy* snapshot when available (plan-aware:
    32→    only counts planned objectives post-triage).  Falls back to scanning
    33→    *state_or_issues* directly (counts all open non-subjective issues).
    34→    """
    35→    if policy is not None:
    36→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
    37→
    38→    # Accept either full state payload (`{"issues": ...}`) or raw issues dict.
    39→    issues = state_or_issues
    40→    if isinstance(state_or_issues, dict):
    41→        maybe_issues = state_or_issues.get("issues")
    42→        if isinstance(maybe_issues, dict):
    43→            issues = maybe_issues
    44→    return any(
    45→        f.get("status") == "open"
    46→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    47→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    48→        for f in issues.values()
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def is_mid_cycle(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    53→    """True when a queue cycle is in progress.
    54→
    55→    A cycle is active when ``plan_start_scores`` is set to real score
    56→    values.  The ``{"reset": True}`` sentinel (set by lifecycle reset)
    57→    does NOT count as mid-cycle — it means "seed real scores on next scan".
    58→    """
    59→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    60→    return bool(scores) and not (isinstance(scores, dict) and scores.get("reset"))
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = ["has_objective_backlog", "is_mid_cycle"]
    64→
```

```
1→"""Unified queue-resolution context.
     2→
     3→A frozen ``QueueContext`` computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``plan`` / ``target_strict`` / ``policy`` threading through function chains.
     5→Callers build one context and pass it everywhere — makes the wrong thing
     6→impossible.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass
    12→
    13→from desloppify.base.config import (
    14→    DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    15→    target_strict_score_from_config,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.persistence import (
    18→    has_living_plan,
    19→    load_plan,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanLoadStatus
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import QueueSnapshot, build_queue_snapshot
    24→
    25→
    26→class _PlanAutoLoad:
    27→    """Sentinel type: auto-load plan from disk."""
    28→
    29→
    30→# Sentinel: "auto-load plan from disk" (the default).
    31→_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD = _PlanAutoLoad()
    32→PlanOption = dict | None | _PlanAutoLoad
    33→
    34→
    35→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    36→class QueueContext:
    37→    """Immutable snapshot of resolved queue parameters.
    38→
    39→    Built once via :func:`queue_context`, then threaded through queue
    40→    builders and command helpers so every call site agrees on plan,
    41→    target, and canonical queue facts.
    42→    """
    43→
    44→    plan: dict | None
    45→    target_strict: float
    46→    plan_load_status: PlanLoadStatus
    47→    snapshot: QueueSnapshot
    48→
    49→
    50→def resolve_plan_load_status(
    51→    *,
    52→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    53→) -> PlanLoadStatus:
    54→    """Resolve plan loading and explicitly report degraded mode."""
    55→    if not isinstance(plan, _PlanAutoLoad):
    56→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    57→
    58→    if not has_living_plan():
    59→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=None, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    60→
    61→    try:
    62→        resolved_plan = load_plan()
    63→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=resolved_plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    64→    except OSError as exc:
    65→        return PlanLoadStatus(
    66→            plan=None,
    67→            degraded=True,
    68→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
    69→        )
    70→    except (
    71→        ValueError,
    72→        UnicodeDecodeError,
    73→    ) as exc:
    74→        return PlanLoadStatus(
    75→            plan=None,
    76→            degraded=True,
    77→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
    78→        )
    79→
    80→
    81→def queue_context(
    82→    state: StateModel,
    83→    *,
    84→    config: dict | None = None,
    85→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    86→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    87→) -> QueueContext:
    88→    """Build a :class:`QueueContext` with all parameters resolved.
    89→
    90→    Resolution order:
    91→
    92→    1. **plan** — explicit value wins; sentinel ``_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD`` triggers
    93→       ``load_plan()`` (guarded by ``PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS``).
    94→    2. **target_strict** — explicit float wins; ``None`` reads from *config*
    95→       via ``target_strict_score_from_config``; final fallback is ``95.0``.
    96→    3. **snapshot** — canonical queue phase and partitions derived from the
    97→       resolved plan and target_strict.
    98→    """
    99→    # --- resolve plan ---
   100→    plan_load_status = resolve_plan_load_status(plan=plan)
   101→    resolved_plan = plan_load_status.plan
   102→
   103→    # --- resolve target_strict ---
   104→    if target_strict is not None:
   105→        resolved_target = target_strict
   106→    elif config is not None:
   107→        resolved_target = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   108→    else:
   109→        resolved_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
   110→
   111→    snapshot = build_queue_snapshot(
   112→        state,
   113→        plan=resolved_plan,
   114→        target_strict=resolved_target,
   115→    )
   116→
   117→    return QueueContext(
   118→        plan=resolved_plan,
   119→        target_strict=resolved_target,
   120→        plan_load_status=plan_load_status,
   121→        snapshot=snapshot,
   122→    )
   123→
   124→
   125→__all__ = [
   126→    "PlanLoadStatus",
   127→    "QueueContext",
   128→    "queue_context",
   129→    "resolve_plan_load_status",
   130→]
   131→
```

```
    44→class PendingImportScoresMeta:
    45→    """Normalized contract for the queued score-import workflow."""
    46→
    47→    timestamp: str = ""

    49→    normalized_import_file: str = ""

    52→    @classmethod
    53→    def from_mapping(cls, raw: object) -> PendingImportScoresMeta | None:
    54→        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
    55→            return None
    56→        meta = cls(
    57→            timestamp=str(raw.get("timestamp", "")).strip(),
    58→            import_file=str(raw.get("import_file", "")).strip(),
    59→            normalized_import_file=str(raw.get("normalized_import_file", "")).strip(),

    79→def _latest_assessment_audit(

    84→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    85→    if not isinstance(audit, list):

    87→    for entry in reversed(audit):

   120→        normalized_import_file=_normalize_match_path(recorded_file) or "",
   121→        packet_sha256=str(provenance.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip(),
   122→    )

   124→
   125→def pending_import_scores_meta(
   126→    plan: PlanModel,
   127→    state: StateModel,
   128→) -> PendingImportScoresMeta | None:
   129→    """Return queued score-import metadata without mutating the plan."""
   130→    refresh_state = _get_refresh_state(plan)
   131→    if refresh_state is not None:
   132→        meta = PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(
   133→            refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   134→        )
   135→        if meta is not None:
   136→            return meta
   137→    issues_only_audit = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   138→    if issues_only_audit is None:
   139→        return None
   140→    return _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
   141→        import_file=str(issues_only_audit.get("import_file", "")),
   142→        import_payload=None,
   143→        issues_only_audit=issues_only_audit,
   144→    )

   146→
   147→def import_scores_meta_matches(
   148→    meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | dict[str, Any] | None,
   149→    *,
   150→    import_file: str,
   151→    import_payload: dict[str, Any],
   152→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   153→    """Return (matches, reason) for whether the import matches the pending batch.
   154→
   155→    Checks packet_sha256 first (strongest signal), falls back to normalized
   156→    file path.  Returns a single human-readable reason on mismatch.
   157→    """
   158→    normalized_meta = (
   159→        meta
   160→        if isinstance(meta, PendingImportScoresMeta)
   161→        else PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(meta)
   162→    )
   163→    if normalized_meta is None:
   164→        return True, ""
   165→
   166→    provenance = import_payload.get("provenance")
   167→    provenance_dict = provenance if isinstance(provenance, dict) else {}
   168→
   169→    expected_hash = normalized_meta.packet_sha256
   170→    current_hash = str(provenance_dict.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip()
   171→    if expected_hash and current_hash:
   172→        if current_hash == expected_hash:
   173→            return True, ""
   174→        return False, f"expected packet_sha256 {expected_hash}, got {current_hash}"
   175→
   176→    expected_file = normalized_meta.normalized_import_file
   177→    current_file = _normalize_match_path(import_file) or ""
   178→    if expected_file and current_file and current_file != expected_file:
   179→        return False, f"expected import file {normalized_meta.import_file}, got {import_file}"
   180→
   181→    return True, ""
   182→

   184→def _clear_pending_import_scores(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   185→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   186→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   187→        order[:] = [item for item in order if item != WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID]
   188→    refresh_state = _get_refresh_state(plan)
   189→    if refresh_state is not None:
   190→        refresh_state.pop(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY, None)
   191→
   192→
   193→def _pending_compare_timestamp(
   194→    pending_meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | None,
   195→    latest_issues_only: dict[str, Any],
   196→) -> str:
   197→    if pending_meta is not None and pending_meta.timestamp:
   198→        return pending_meta.timestamp
   199→    return str(latest_issues_only.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   200→
   201→
   202→def _pending_import_scores_stale(
   203→    *,
   204→    order: list[str],
   205→    pending_meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | None,
   206→    latest_issues_only: dict[str, Any] | None,
   207→    latest_trusted: dict[str, Any] | None,
   208→) -> bool:
   209→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in order:
   210→        return False
   211→    if latest_issues_only is None:
   212→        return True
   213→    if latest_trusted is None:
   214→        return False
   215→
   216→    latest_trusted_ts = str(latest_trusted.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   217→    compare_ts = _pending_compare_timestamp(pending_meta, latest_issues_only)
   218→    return bool(compare_ts and latest_trusted_ts and latest_trusted_ts >= compare_ts)
   219→
   220→
   221→def _record_pending_import_scores(
   222→    refresh_state: dict[str, Any],
   223→    *,
   224→    import_file: str | None,
   225→    import_payload: dict[str, Any] | None,
   226→    latest_issues_only: dict[str, Any] | None,
   227→) -> None:
   228→    refresh_state[_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY] = _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
   229→        import_file=import_file,
   230→        import_payload=import_payload,
   231→        issues_only_audit=latest_issues_only,
   232→    ).to_dict()

   235→def _no_unscored(
   236→    state: StateModel,
   237→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   238→) -> bool:
   239→    """Return True when no unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain."""
   240→    if policy is not None:
   241→        return not policy.unscored_ids
   242→    return not stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
   243→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   244→    )

   247→def _inject(plan: PlanModel, item_id: str) -> QueueSyncResult:
   248→    """Inject *item_id* into the workflow prefix and clear stale skip entries."""
   249→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   250→    if item_id not in order:
   251→        order.append(item_id)
   252→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
   253→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   254→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
   255→        skipped.pop(item_id, None)
   256→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=[item_id])
   257→
   258→
   259→_EMPTY = QueueSyncResult
   260→
   261→
   262→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   263→    plan: PlanModel,
   264→    state: StateModel,
   265→    *,
   266→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   267→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   268→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   269→
   270→    Injects when:
   271→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   272→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   273→
   274→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   275→    """
   276→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   277→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   278→
   279→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   280→        return _EMPTY()
   281→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   282→        return _EMPTY()
   283→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   284→
   285→
   286→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   287→    plan: PlanModel,
   288→    state: StateModel,
   289→    *,
   290→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   291→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   292→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   293→
   294→    Only injects when:
   295→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   296→    - At least one objective issue exists
   297→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   298→    - No triage stages are pending
   299→
   300→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   301→    """
   302→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   303→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   304→
   305→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   306→        return _EMPTY()
   307→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   308→        return _EMPTY()
   309→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   310→        return _EMPTY()
   311→
   312→    if not has_objective_backlog(state, policy):
   313→        return _EMPTY()
   314→
   315→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   316→
   317→
   318→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   319→    plan: PlanModel,
   320→    state: StateModel,
   321→    *,
   322→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   323→    import_file: str | None = None,
   324→    import_payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   325→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   326→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   327→
   328→    Only injects when:
   329→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   330→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   331→
   332→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   333→    """
   334→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   335→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   336→    refresh_state = _ensure_refresh_state(plan)
   337→    pending_meta = PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(
   338→        refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   339→    )
   340→    latest_issues_only = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   341→    latest_trusted = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes=_TRUSTED_ASSESSMENT_MODES)
   342→
   343→    if _pending_import_scores_stale(
   344→        order=order,
   345→        pending_meta=pending_meta,
   346→        latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   347→        latest_trusted=latest_trusted,
   348→    ):
   349→        _clear_pending_import_scores(plan)
   350→        return QueueSyncResult(pruned=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   351→
   352→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   353→        if assessment_mode == "issues_only":
   354→            _record_pending_import_scores(
   355→                refresh_state,
   356→                import_file=import_file,
   357→                import_payload=import_payload,
   358→                latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   359→            )
   360→            return QueueSyncResult(resurfaced=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   361→        return _EMPTY()
   362→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   363→        return _EMPTY()
   364→    result = _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   365→    _record_pending_import_scores(
   366→        refresh_state,
   367→        import_file=import_file,
   368→        import_payload=import_payload,
   369→        latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   370→    )
   371→    return result
   372→
   373→
   374→class ScoreSnapshot:
   375→    """Minimal score snapshot for rebaseline — avoids importing state.py."""
   376→
   377→    __slots__ = ("strict", "overall", "objective", "verified")
   378→
   379→    def __init__(
   380→        self,
   381→        *,
   382→        strict: float | None,
   383→        overall: float | None,
   384→        objective: float | None,
   385→        verified: float | None,
   386→    ) -> None:
   387→        self.strict = strict
   388→        self.overall = overall
   389→        self.objective = objective
   390→        self.verified = verified
   391→
   392→
   393→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   394→    plan: PlanModel,
   395→    state: StateModel,
   396→    *,
   397→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   398→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   399→    current_scores: ScoreSnapshot | None = None,
   400→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   401→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` and rebaseline scores.
   402→
   403→    Injects when:
   404→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dims), OR
   405→      scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   406→    - ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue
   407→    - Score has not already been communicated this cycle
   408→      (``previous_plan_start_scores`` absent), unless a trusted score import
   409→      explicitly refreshed the live score mid-cycle
   410→
   411→    When injected and *current_scores* is provided, ``plan_start_scores``
   412→    is rebaselined to the current score so the score display unfreezes at
   413→    the new value.  The previous baseline is preserved in
   414→    ``previous_plan_start_scores`` so the communicate-score queue item can
   415→    show the old → new delta — and so mid-cycle scans know not to
   416→    re-inject.
   417→    """
   418→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   419→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   420→
   421→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order:
   422→        return _EMPTY()
   423→    # Already communicated this cycle — previous_plan_start_scores is set
   424→    # at injection time and cleared at cycle boundaries.
   425→    if "previous_plan_start_scores" in plan and not scores_just_imported:
   426→        return _EMPTY()
   427→    if not scores_just_imported and not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   428→        return _EMPTY()
   429→
   430→    if current_scores is not None:
   431→        _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(plan, current_scores)
   432→    # Set sentinel even when rebaseline was a no-op (no plan_start_scores
   433→    # to rebaseline) so mid-cycle scans don't re-inject.
   434→    if not plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores"):
   435→        plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = {}
   436→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   437→
   438→
   439→def _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(
   440→    plan: PlanModel,
   441→    scores: ScoreSnapshot,
   442→) -> None:
   443→    """Snapshot the current score as the new baseline, preserving the old one."""
   444→    old_start = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   445→    if not isinstance(old_start, dict) or not old_start:
   446→        return
   447→    if scores.strict is None:
   448→        return
   449→
   450→    plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = dict(old_start)
   451→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   452→        "strict": scores.strict,
   453→        "overall": scores.overall,
   454→        "objective": scores.objective,
   455→        "verified": scores.verified,
   456→    }
   457→
   458→
   459→__all__ = [
   460→    "PendingImportScoresMeta",
   461→    "ScoreSnapshot",
   462→    "import_scores_meta_matches",
   463→    "pending_import_scores_meta",
   464→    "sync_communicate_score_needed",
   465→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   466→    "sync_import_scores_needed",
   467→    "sync_score_checkpoint_needed",
   468→]
   469→
```

## Implementation Plan: Unified Postflight Pipeline

### 1. Analysis of Current State

**Two orchestrators with overlapping pipelines:**

| Step | `plan_reconcile._sync_post_scan_with_policy()` | `plan_sync.sync_plan_after_import()` |
|---|---|---|
| Subjective dims | `sync_subjective_dimensions()` | `sync_subjective_dimensions()` |
| Auto-cluster | `auto_cluster_issues()` (skip if mid-cycle) | `auto_cluster_issues()` |
| Communicate-score | `sync_communicate_score_needed()` | `sync_communicate_score_needed()` (with `scores_just_imported`) |
| Import-scores | -- | `sync_import_scores_needed()` |
| Create-plan | `sync_create_plan_needed()` | `sync_create_plan_needed()` |
| Triage | `sync_triage_needed()` | (via `sync_plan_after_review_import` which itself calls triage) |
| Plan-start scores | `_sync_plan_start_scores_and_log()` | -- |
| Postflight scan complete | `_sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log()` | -- |
| Lifecycle phase | `coarse_phase_name(queue_context(...).snapshot.phase)` via `set_lifecycle_phase` | `sync_lifecycle_phase()` with manually-computed booleans |

**Key differences between the two callers:**

1. **plan_reconcile** has scan-specific steps: force-rescan reset, plan reconciliation (supersede/prune), plan-start score seeding/clearing, postflight scan completion marking, mid-cycle guards.
2. **plan_sync** has import-specific steps: `sync_import_scores_needed`, `sync_plan_after_review_import` (inject new review IDs), `scores_just_imported` flag.
3. **Lifecycle resolution** uses two completely different mechanisms.

### 2. Design Decisions

**Q1: Where should the centralized function live?**

New file: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/pipeline.py`

This sits alongside the gate modules it orchestrates. It belongs in the `engine/_plan/sync/` package because it is pure plan-mutation logic, not app-layer display. The import direction rule (engine imports from base only, never app) is preserved -- the pipeline returns structured results and the callers in `app/` handle display.

**Q2: Result type**

```python
@dataclass
class PipelineResult:
    """Structured result from the postflight gate pipeline."""
    subjective_sync: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    auto_cluster_changes: int = 0
    communicate_score_sync: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    import_scores_sync: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    create_plan_sync: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    triage_sync: QueueSyncResult | None = None
    plan_start_scores_changed: bool = False
    postflight_scan_completed: bool = False
    lifecycle_phase: str | None = None
    lifecycle_phase_changed: bool = False

    @property
    def dirty(self) -> bool:
        return any([
            self.subjective_sync and self.subjective_sync.changes,
            self.auto_cluster_changes > 0,
            self.communicate_score_sync and self.communicate_score_sync.changes,
            self.import_scores_sync and self.import_scores_sync.changes,
            self.create_plan_sync and self.create_plan_sync.changes,
            self.triage_sync and self.triage_sync.changes,
            self.plan_start_scores_changed,
            self.postflight_scan_completed,
            self.lifecycle_phase_changed,
        ])
```

This lets callers inspect individual gate results for logging and display without the pipeline needing to know about `print()` or `colorize()`.

**Q3: Lifecycle phase resolution -- unified inside the pipeline**

Use the `queue_context` / `coarse_phase_name` approach (plan_reconcile's method). It is strictly more correct -- it derives the phase from the actual queue snapshot rather than manually computing boolean flags. The `sync_lifecycle_phase()` function in `refresh_lifecycle.py` with its 6 boolean parameters is the inferior approach and was created as a workaround when `plan_sync` didn't have access to `queue_context`.

The pipeline function will:
1. Build a `queue_context(state, plan=plan)` after all gates have run.
2. Call `coarse_phase_name(snapshot.phase)` to get the persisted phase.
3. Call `set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)` to persist it.

This eliminates the manual boolean computation in `plan_sync._sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import()` entirely.

**Q4: `_has_postflight_review_work()` location**

Move it to `engine/_plan/sync/context.py` alongside `has_objective_backlog()` and `is_mid_cycle()`. Both copies are identical. However, with the unified lifecycle resolution via `queue_context`, this helper becomes dead code -- neither caller needs it anymore. So it can be deleted from both files.

Actually, looking more carefully: `_has_postflight_review_work` is only used in `plan_sync._sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import` which computes boolean flags for `sync_lifecycle_phase()`. Since we're replacing that with the `queue_context` approach, this function is no longer needed at all. The `_has_postflight_workflow_items` and `_has_triage_items` helpers in `plan_reconcile` are also only used for the now-unnecessary boolean approach -- they too become dead code.

**Q5: Logging/display**

The pipeline returns `PipelineResult` and does NOT print or log. Both callers handle display themselves -- they have very different display needs (plan_reconcile shows inline scan output; plan_sync shows a structured import summary). Logging via `append_log_entry` stays in the callers too, since the log entry format and detail fields differ between scan and import contexts.

Wait -- actually the `append_log_entry` calls are *almost* identical between the two. Let me reconsider. The pipeline could optionally call `append_log_entry` for each gate change with a neutral `trigger` field, and callers can add their own domain-specific log entries on top. But this adds complexity. Simpler: keep logging in callers. The pipeline is pure mutation + result reporting.

**Q6: Auto-clustering**

Include it in the pipeline with a `skip_auto_cluster: bool = False` parameter. plan_reconcile passes `skip_auto_cluster=mid_cycle`; plan_sync passes `skip_auto_cluster=False` (or rather, only calls it when there was a review delta -- but the gate function `auto_cluster_issues` is idempotent, so calling it unconditionally is safe and simpler).

Actually, looking at `plan_sync._sync_subjective_queue_after_import`, auto-clustering is only run when there's a review issue delta or assessment keys. This is an optimization (skip work when nothing changed), not a correctness guard. The `auto_cluster_issues` function is idempotent. So calling it unconditionally in the pipeline is fine -- it will return 0 changes when nothing changed. Simpler pipeline, same result.

**Q7: Plan-start scores seeding/clearing**

Include in the pipeline with `skip_plan_start_scores: bool = False`. plan_reconcile passes `skip_plan_start_scores=force_rescan`; plan_sync passes `skip_plan_start_scores=True` (it never does this work). This keeps the pipeline complete.

**Q8: Edge cases**

- **Mid-cycle scan guard**: Stays as a parameter. `plan_reconcile` computes `mid_cycle` from its own `_is_mid_cycle_scan()` (which adds a queue-items check on top of `is_mid_cycle`). This gets passed as `skip_auto_cluster`.
- **Force-rescan**: `_reset_cycle_for_force_rescan` stays in plan_reconcile as a pre-pipeline step. It's scan-specific and runs before the pipeline.
- **Import-specific flow**: `sync_plan_after_review_import` (inject new review IDs) stays in plan_sync as a pre-pipeline step. The `sync_import_scores_needed` gate gets wired through via the pipeline parameters.
- **Postflight scan completion**: Stays in the pipeline since it's a standard gate.

### 3. Step-by-Step Implementation

**Step 1: Create `engine/_plan/sync/pipeline.py`**

Define `PipelineResult` dataclass and `run_postflight_gates()` function:

```python
def run_postflight_gates(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility,
    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
    current_scores: ScoreSnapshot | None = None,
    # Import-specific gate params (no-op when None)
    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
    import_file: str | None = None,
    import_payload: dict | None = None,
    # Caller controls
    skip_auto_cluster: bool = False,
    skip_plan_start_scores: bool = False,
    skip_postflight_scan_completion: bool = False,
) -> PipelineResult:
```

Pipeline order:
1. `sync_subjective_dimensions(plan, state, policy=policy, cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed)`
2. `auto_cluster_issues(plan, state, target_strict=..., policy=policy)` (unless `skip_auto_cluster`)
3. `sync_communicate_score_needed(plan, state, policy=policy, scores_just_imported=scores_just_imported, current_scores=current_scores)`
4. `sync_import_scores_needed(plan, state, assessment_mode=assessment_mode, import_file=import_file, import_payload=import_payload)` (only when `assessment_mode is not None`)
5. `sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)`
6. `sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)`
7. `_seed_or_clear_plan_start_scores(plan, state)` (unless `skip_plan_start_scores`) -- move from plan_reconcile
8. `_mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(state, plan)` (unless `skip_postflight_scan_completion`) -- move from plan_reconcile
9. Lifecycle phase: `coarse_phase_name(queue_context(state, plan=plan).snapshot.phase)` then `set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)`

The plan-start-scores logic (`_seed_plan_start_scores`, `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty`, `_has_objective_cycle`) needs to move from `plan_reconcile.py` into the pipeline module since it's pure engine logic.

Similarly `_mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready` should move.

**Step 2: Refactor `plan_reconcile.py`**

The `_sync_post_scan_with_policy()` function shrinks to:

```python
def _sync_post_scan_with_policy(*, plan, state, target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed, force_rescan=False):
    mid_cycle = _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state) or force_rescan
    result = run_postflight_gates(
        plan, state,
        policy=policy,
        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
        skip_auto_cluster=mid_cycle,
        skip_plan_start_scores=force_rescan,
        skip_postflight_scan_completion=force_rescan,
    )
    # Display + logging
    _display_subjective_sync(result.subjective_sync)
    if mid_cycle and not result.auto_cluster_changes:
        print(colorize("  Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration...", "dim"))
    _log_gate_results(plan, result)
    return result.dirty
```

All the `_sync_*_and_log` helper functions collapse. The display functions (`_sync_subjective_dimensions_display` etc.) can be simplified to read from `PipelineResult` fields.

**Step 3: Refactor `plan_sync.py`**

The `sync_plan_after_import()` function shrinks to:

```python
def sync_plan_after_import(state, diff, assessment_mode, *, request=None):
    # ... load plan, compute policy, build sync_inputs ...
    
    # Domain-specific: inject review delta
    mutations = _PlanImportMutations()
    _sync_review_delta(mutations, plan, state, policy=policy, sync_inputs=sync_inputs)
    
    # Shared pipeline
    trusted = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    result = run_postflight_gates(
        plan, state,
        policy=policy,
        scores_just_imported=trusted,
        current_scores=current_scores,
        assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
        import_file=import_file,
        import_payload=import_payload,
        skip_plan_start_scores=True,
        skip_postflight_scan_completion=True,
    )
    
    dirty = mutations.dirty or result.dirty
    if dirty:
        # logging + save
        save_plan(plan, plan_path)
    # display
```

The manually-computed lifecycle boolean approach (`_sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import`) is deleted entirely. `_has_postflight_review_work`, `_has_deferred_disposition_work` are deleted.

## Detailed Implementation Plan

### Step 1: Create `desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/pipeline.py`

This new file contains:

**A. `PipelineResult` dataclass** -- structured result with per-gate results and a `dirty` property.

**B. Pure helper functions moved from `plan_reconcile.py`** (plan-start score seeding/clearing, postflight scan completion):
- `_seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state) -> bool`
- `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan) -> bool`  
- `_has_objective_cycle(state, plan) -> bool | None`
- `_mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(state, plan) -> bool`

These are pure plan-mutation logic with no display dependencies. They import from `engine/` and `base/` only, preserving the import direction rule.

Note: `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty` currently has a lazy import of `plan_aware_queue_breakdown` and `ScoreDisplayMode` from `app.commands.helpers.queue_progress`. This is an app-layer import inside an engine-layer module -- a layering violation. For the initial refactor, keep the lazy import as-is (it's already a lazy import, so the dependency is soft). A follow-up could push the "is queue empty" logic into the engine layer.

**C. `run_postflight_gates()` function** with signature:

```python
def run_postflight_gates(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility,
    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
    current_scores: ScoreSnapshot | None = None,
    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
    import_file: str | None = None,
    import_payload: dict | None = None,
    skip_auto_cluster: bool = False,
    skip_plan_start_scores: bool = False,
    skip_postflight_scan_completion: bool = False,
) -> PipelineResult:
```

**Remove:**

- `_log_pipeline_results(plan, result: PipelineResult)` -- calls `append_log_entry` for each gate that changed.

**Rewrite** `_sync_post_scan_with_policy` to:
1. Compute `mid_cycle`.
2. Call `run_postflight_gates(plan, state, policy=..., ..., skip_auto_cluster=mid_cycle, skip_plan_start_scores=force_rescan, skip_postflight_scan_completion=force_rescan)`.
3. Call `_display_pipeline_results(result, mid_cycle=mid_cycle)`.
4. Call `_log_pipeline_results(plan, result)`.

### Step 3: Refactor `plan_sync.py`

**Remove:**

- `_has_deferred_disposition_work` -- no longer needed

2. Domain-specific: `_sync_review_delta(mutations, plan, state, ...)` (unchanged).
3. Call `run_postflight_gates(plan, state, policy=..., scores_just_imported=..., current_scores=..., assessment_mode=..., import_file=..., import_payload=..., skip_plan_start_scores=True, skip_postflight_scan_completion=True)`.
4. Merge `mutations.dirty or result.dirty` to decide save.

No behavioral changes -- all tests should pass with the same assertions.

### Step 6: Add direct tests for `run_postflight_gates`

Create `desloppify/tests/plan/test_postflight_pipeline.py` with focused tests:
- Pipeline runs all gates in order
- `skip_*` flags correctly bypass gates
- Import-specific gates only run when `assessment_mode` is provided
- Lifecycle phase is resolved and persisted
- `PipelineResult.dirty` aggregates correctly

### Sequencing and Dependencies

1. **Step 1** (create pipeline.py) -- no dependencies, can be done first
2. **Step 2** (refactor plan_reconcile) -- depends on Step 1
3. **Step 3** (refactor plan_sync) -- depends on Step 1, independent of Step 2
4. **Step 4** (init update) -- trivial, do with Step 1
5. **Step 5** (test updates) -- do alongside Steps 2-3
6. **Step 6** (new tests) -- do after Step 1

Steps 2 and 3 can be done in parallel since they're independent callers.

### Potential Challenges

1. **The lazy `app.commands.helpers.queue_progress` import** in `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty`. This breaks the engine-never-imports-app rule. For now, keep the lazy import. Long-term, the "is frozen score mode" check should move to engine.

2. **`_sync_review_delta` calls `sync_plan_after_review_import`** which internally calls `sync_triage_needed`. The pipeline also calls `sync_triage_needed`. Both calls are idempotent (second call is a no-op), but it's wasteful. Could skip triage in the pipeline when review delta was present. Add `skip_triage: bool = False` parameter.

3. **Display differences**: plan_reconcile prints inline during scan; plan_sync prints a structured summary at the end. The pipeline-returns-results approach handles this naturally -- each caller formats results differently.

4. **The `workflow_gates.py` re-export shim** could be updated to also export the pipeline function, or callers can import from `pipeline` directly.

```
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.triage import (
    10→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    11→    compute_open_issue_ids,
    12→    sync_triage_needed,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass
    18→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
    19→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
    20→
    21→    new_ids: set[str]
    22→    added_to_queue: list[str]
    23→    triage_injected: bool
    24→    stale_pruned_from_queue: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    25→    triage_injected_ids: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    26→    triage_deferred: bool = False
    27→
    28→
    29→def _has_triage_baseline(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    30→    """Return True when triage has recorded at least one baseline issue ID."""
    31→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    32→    triaged_ids = meta.get("triaged_ids", [])
    33→    return bool(triaged_ids)
    34→
    35→
    36→def _review_issue_ids_for_import_sync(
    37→    plan: PlanModel,
    38→    state: StateModel,
    39→    *,
    40→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    41→) -> set[str]:
    42→    """Return review IDs that should be synced into queue_order after import.
    43→
    44→    No triage baseline yet: include all currently-open review IDs so the
    45→    first import cannot drop follow-up work.
    46→    Existing triage baseline: include only IDs that are new since triage.
    47→    """
    48→    if _has_triage_baseline(plan):
    49→        return compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
    50→    return set(open_review_ids) if open_review_ids is not None else compute_open_issue_ids(state)
    51→
    52→
    53→def _is_review_queue_id(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    54→    """Return True for queue IDs representing review/concerns issues."""
    55→    return issue_id.startswith("review::") or issue_id.startswith("concerns::")
    56→
    57→
    58→def _prune_stale_triage_meta(
    59→    plan: PlanModel,
    60→    *,
    61→    stale_ids: set[str],
    62→) -> None:
    63→    """Drop stale review IDs from triage recovery metadata."""
    64→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta")
    65→    if not isinstance(meta, dict) or not stale_ids:
    66→        return
    67→
    68→    active_ids = meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids")
    69→    if isinstance(active_ids, list):
    70→        filtered_active = [
    71→            issue_id for issue_id in active_ids
    72→            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id not in stale_ids
    73→        ]
    74→        if filtered_active:
    75→            meta["active_triage_issue_ids"] = filtered_active
    76→        else:
    77→            meta.pop("active_triage_issue_ids", None)
    78→
    79→    undispositioned_ids = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_ids")
    80→    if isinstance(undispositioned_ids, list):
    81→        filtered_undispositioned = [
    82→            issue_id for issue_id in undispositioned_ids
    83→            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id not in stale_ids
    84→        ]
    85→        if filtered_undispositioned:
    86→            meta["undispositioned_issue_ids"] = filtered_undispositioned
    87→            meta["undispositioned_issue_count"] = len(filtered_undispositioned)
    88→        else:
    89→            meta.pop("undispositioned_issue_ids", None)
    90→            meta.pop("undispositioned_issue_count", None)
    91→
    92→
    93→def _prune_stale_review_ids_from_plan(
    94→    plan: PlanModel,
    95→    *,
    96→    live_open_review_ids: set[str],
    97→) -> list[str]:
    98→    """Remove stale review IDs from queue-related plan containers.
    99→
   100→    Review imports can auto-resolve prior review IDs. These IDs must be removed
   101→    from queue_order (and linked queue metadata) or the queue can drift from
   102→    state reality.
   103→    """
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→    stale_ids = sorted(
   106→        {
   107→            issue_id
   108→            for issue_id in order
   109→            if _is_review_queue_id(issue_id) and issue_id not in live_open_review_ids
   110→        }
   111→    )
   112→    if not stale_ids:
   113→        return []
   114→
   115→    stale_set = set(stale_ids)
   116→    order[:] = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in stale_set]
   117→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, stale_set)
   118→    _prune_stale_triage_meta(plan, stale_ids=stale_set)
   119→
   120→    deferred = plan.get("deferred")
   121→    if isinstance(deferred, list):
   122→        plan["deferred"] = [issue_id for issue_id in deferred if issue_id not in stale_set]
   123→
   124→    skipped = plan.get("skipped")
   125→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
   126→        for issue_id in stale_set:
   127→            skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   128→
   129→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   130→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids")
   131→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   132→            continue
   133→        cluster["issue_ids"] = [
   134→            issue_id for issue_id in issue_ids if issue_id not in stale_set
   135→        ]
   136→
   137→    return stale_ids
   138→
   139→
   140→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   141→    plan: PlanModel,
   142→    state: StateModel,
   143→    *,
   144→    policy=None,
   145→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   146→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   147→
   148→    Appends new issue IDs to queue_order, prunes stale review IDs from queue
   149→    containers, and injects triage stages if needed (respects mid-cycle guard
   150→    — defers when objective work remains). Returns ``None`` when no queue
   151→    changes are required.
   152→    """
   153→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   154→    open_review_ids = compute_open_issue_ids(state)
   155→    stale_pruned_from_queue = _prune_stale_review_ids_from_plan(
   156→        plan,
   157→        live_open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   158→    )
   159→    new_ids = _review_issue_ids_for_import_sync(
   160→        plan,
   161→        state,
   162→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   163→    )
   164→    if not new_ids and not stale_pruned_from_queue:
   165→        return None
   166→
   167→    # Add new issue IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   168→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   169→    existing = set(order)
   170→    added: list[str] = []
   171→    for issue_id in sorted(new_ids):
   172→        if issue_id not in existing:
   173→            order.append(issue_id)
   174→            added.append(issue_id)
   175→
   176→    # Inject triage stages if needed (policy enables mid-cycle guard)
   177→    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   178→    triage_injected_ids = list(getattr(triage_result, "injected", []) or [])
   179→    triage_injected = bool(triage_injected_ids)
   180→    triage_deferred = bool(triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "deferred", False))
   181→
   182→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   183→        new_ids=new_ids,
   184→        added_to_queue=added,
   185→        stale_pruned_from_queue=stale_pruned_from_queue,
   186→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
   187→        triage_injected_ids=triage_injected_ids,
   188→        triage_deferred=triage_deferred,
   189→    )
   190→
   191→
   192→__all__ = ["ReviewImportSyncResult", "sync_plan_after_review_import"]
   193→
```

```
    71→def _has_postflight_review_work(state: dict, *, policy) -> bool:

    87→    return any(

   318→def _append_review_import_sync_log(
   319→    plan: dict,
   320→    diff: dict,
   321→    mutations: _PlanImportMutations,
   322→    *,
   323→    covered_ids: tuple[str, ...],
   324→) -> None:

   326→        mutations.import_result is not None
   327→        or mutations.workflow_injected_ids
   328→        or covered_ids
   329→    ):
   330→        return
   331→    import_result = mutations.import_result
   332→    stale_sync_result = mutations.stale_sync_result
   333→    append_log_entry(
   334→        plan,
   335→        "review_import_sync",
   336→        actor="system",
   337→        detail={
   338→            "trigger": "review_import",
   339→            "new_ids": sorted(import_result.new_ids) if import_result is not None else [],
   340→            "added_to_queue": (
   341→                import_result.added_to_queue if import_result is not None else []
   342→            ),
   343→            "workflow_injected_ids": mutations.workflow_injected_ids,

   349→            ),

   352→            ),

   354→            "diff_reopened": diff.get("reopened", 0),
   355→            "diff_auto_resolved": diff.get("auto_resolved", 0),
   356→            "stale_pruned_from_queue": (
   357→                import_result.stale_pruned_from_queue if import_result is not None else []
   358→            ),
   359→            "covered_subjective": list(covered_ids),
   360→            "stale_sync_injected": (
   361→                sorted(stale_sync_result.injected)
   362→                if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   363→            ),
   364→            "stale_sync_pruned": (
   365→                sorted(stale_sync_result.pruned)
   366→                if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   367→            ),
   368→            "auto_cluster_changes": mutations.auto_cluster_changes,
   369→        },
   370→    )
   371→
   372→
   373→def sync_plan_after_import(
   374→    state: dict,
   375→    diff: dict,
   376→    assessment_mode: str,
   377→    *,
   378→    request: PlanImportSyncRequest | None = None,
   379→) -> None:
   380→    """Apply issue/workflow syncs after import in one load/save cycle."""
   381→    try:
   382→        state_file = request.state_file if request is not None else None
   383→        config = request.config if request is not None else None
   384→        import_file = request.import_file if request is not None else None
   385→        import_payload = request.import_payload if request is not None else None
   386→
   387→        plan_path = None
   388→        target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   389→        if state_file is not None:
   390→            plan_path = plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
   391→        if not has_living_plan(plan_path):
   392→            return
   393→
   394→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   395→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   396→            state,
   397→            target_strict=target_strict,
   398→            plan=plan,
   399→        )
   400→        snapshot = score_snapshot(state)
   401→        current_scores = ScoreSnapshot(
   402→            strict=snapshot.strict,
   403→            overall=snapshot.overall,
   404→            objective=snapshot.objective,
   405→            verified=snapshot.verified,
   406→        )
   407→        trusted_score_import = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
   408→        communicate_result = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   409→            plan,
   410→            state,
   411→            policy=policy,
   412→            scores_just_imported=trusted_score_import,
   413→            current_scores=current_scores,
   414→        )
   415→        import_scores_result = sync_import_scores_needed(
   416→            plan,
   417→            state,
   418→            assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
   419→            import_file=import_file,
   420→            import_payload=import_payload,
   421→        )
   422→        create_plan_result = sync_create_plan_needed(
   423→            plan,
   424→            state,
   425→            policy=policy,
   426→        )
   427→
   428→        sync_inputs = _build_import_sync_inputs(diff, import_payload)
   429→        mutations = _PlanImportMutations()
   430→        _record_workflow_change(
   431→            mutations,
   432→            communicate_result,
   433→            workflow_id="workflow::communicate-score",
   434→        )
   435→        _record_workflow_change(
   436→            mutations,
   437→            import_scores_result,
   438→            workflow_id="workflow::import-scores",
   439→            injected=bool(getattr(import_scores_result, "injected", ())),
   440→        )
   441→        _record_workflow_change(
   442→            mutations,
   443→            create_plan_result,
   444→            workflow_id="workflow::create-plan",
   445→        )
   446→        _sync_review_delta(
   447→            mutations,
   448→            plan,
   449→            state,
   450→            policy=policy,
   451→            sync_inputs=sync_inputs,
   452→        )
   453→        _sync_subjective_queue_after_import(
   454→            mutations,
   455→            plan,
   456→            state,
   457→            policy=policy,
   458→            target_strict=target_strict,
   459→            sync_inputs=sync_inputs,
   460→        )
   461→
   462→        if mutations.dirty:
   463→            if _sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import(plan, state, policy=policy):
   464→                mutations.dirty = True
   465→            _append_workflow_log_entries(
   466→                plan,
   467→                communicate_result=communicate_result,
   468→                import_scores_result=import_scores_result,
   469→                create_plan_result=create_plan_result,
   470→            )
   471→            _append_review_import_sync_log(
   472→                plan,
   473→                diff,
   474→                mutations,
   475→                covered_ids=sync_inputs.covered_ids,
   476→            )
   477→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   478→
   479→        if mutations.import_result is not None:
   480→            _print_review_import_sync(
   481→                state,
   482→                mutations.import_result,
   483→                workflow_injected=bool(mutations.workflow_injected_ids),
   484→            )
   485→        _print_workflow_injected_message(mutations.workflow_injected_ids)
   486→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   487→        print(
   488→            colorize(
   489→                f"  Note: skipped plan sync after review import ({exc}).",
   490→                "dim",
   491→            )
   492→        )
   493→
   494→
   495→__all__ = ["PlanImportSyncRequest", "sync_plan_after_import"]
   496→
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle issue churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import get_issue_note
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.lifecycle import clear_focus_if_cluster_empty
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.meta import append_log_entry
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.skip import resurface_stale_skips
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.reconcile_review_import import (
    15→    ReviewImportSyncResult,
    16→    sync_plan_after_review_import,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (

    20→    PlanModel,

    22→    ensure_plan_defaults,

    24→from desloppify.engine._plan.skip_policy import skip_kind_state_status
    25→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    26→
    27→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    28→
    29→
    30→@dataclass
    31→class ReconcileResult:
    32→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    33→
    34→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    35→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    36→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    37→    clusters_completed: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    38→    changes: int = 0
    39→
    40→
    41→def _find_candidates(
    42→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    43→) -> list[str]:
    44→    """Find alive issues that could be remaps for a disappeared issue."""
    45→    candidates: list[str] = []
    46→    for fid, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items():
    47→        if issue.get("status") not in _ALIVE_STATUSES:
    48→            continue
    49→        if issue.get("detector") == detector and issue.get("file") == file:
    50→            candidates.append(fid)
    51→    return candidates
    52→
    53→
    54→_ALIVE_STATUSES = frozenset({"open", "deferred", "triaged_out"})
    55→
    56→
    57→def _is_issue_alive(state: StateModel, issue_id: str) -> bool:
    58→    """Return True if the issue exists and is actionable (open/deferred/triaged_out)."""
    59→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    60→    if issue is None:
    61→        return False
    62→    return issue.get("status") in _ALIVE_STATUSES
    63→
    64→
    65→def _supersede_id(
    66→    plan: PlanModel,
    67→    state: StateModel,
    68→    issue_id: str,
    69→    now: str,
    70→) -> bool:
    71→    """Move a disappeared issue to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    72→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    73→    detector = ""
    74→    file = ""
    75→    summary = ""
    76→    if issue:
    77→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    78→        file = issue.get("file", "")
    79→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
    80→
    81→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    82→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    83→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != issue_id]
    84→
    85→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    86→        "original_id": issue_id,
    87→        "original_detector": detector,
    88→        "original_file": file,
    89→        "original_summary": summary,
    90→        "status": "superseded",
    91→        "superseded_at": now,
    92→        "remapped_to": None,
    93→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    94→    }
    95→
    96→    # Preserve any existing override note
    97→    override_note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    98→    if override_note:
    99→        entry["note"] = override_note
   100→
   101→    plan["superseded"][issue_id] = entry
   102→
   103→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster issue_ids
   104→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   105→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   106→    if issue_id in order:
   107→        order.remove(issue_id)
   108→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   109→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, {issue_id})
   110→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   111→        ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   112→        if issue_id in ids:
   113→            ids.remove(issue_id)
   114→
   115→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
   116→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(issue_id)
   117→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   118→        override["cluster"] = None
   119→        override["updated_at"] = now
   120→
   121→    return True
   122→
   123→
   124→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   125→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   126→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   127→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   128→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   129→
   130→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   131→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   132→        try:
   133→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   134→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   135→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   136→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   137→                to_prune.append(fid)
   138→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   139→            to_prune.append(fid)
   140→
   141→    for fid in to_prune:
   142→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   143→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   144→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   145→
   146→    return to_prune
   147→
   148→
   149→def _referenced_plan_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> set[str]:
   150→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   151→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   152→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   153→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("overrides", {}).keys())
   154→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   155→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   156→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   157→    return {
   158→        fid for fid in referenced_ids - already_superseded
   159→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   160→    }
   161→
   162→
   163→def _supersede_dead_references(
   164→    plan: PlanModel,
   165→    state: StateModel,
   166→    *,
   167→    referenced_ids: set[str],
   168→    now: str,
   169→    result: ReconcileResult,
   170→) -> None:
   171→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   172→        if _is_issue_alive(state, fid):
   173→            continue
   174→        if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   175→            result.superseded.append(fid)
   176→            result.changes += 1
   177→
   178→
   179→def _complete_empty_manual_clusters(
   180→    plan: PlanModel,
   181→    *,
   182→    pre_sizes: dict[str, int],
   183→    result: ReconcileResult,
   184→) -> None:
   185→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   186→    for name, prev_size in pre_sizes.items():
   187→        if prev_size == 0:
   188→            continue
   189→        cluster = clusters.get(name)
   190→        if cluster is None or len(cluster.get("issue_ids", [])) != 0:
   191→            continue
   192→        result.clusters_completed.append(name)
   193→        append_log_entry(
   194→            plan,
   195→            "cluster_done",
   196→            issue_ids=[],
   197→            cluster_name=name,
   198→            actor="system",
   199→            detail={"reason": "cluster members no longer actionable in state"},
   200→        )
   201→        result.changes += 1
   202→
   203→
   204→def _reconcile_epic_clusters(
   205→    plan: PlanModel,
   206→    state: StateModel,
   207→    *,
   208→    result: ReconcileResult,
   209→) -> None:
   210→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   211→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   212→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   213→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   214→            continue
   215→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   216→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if _is_issue_alive(state, fid)]
   217→        if alive_ids != issue_ids:
   218→            cluster["issue_ids"] = alive_ids
   219→            result.changes += 1
   220→        if not alive_ids:
   221→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   222→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   223→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   224→        result.changes += 1
   225→
   226→
   227→def _sync_skipped_issue_statuses(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> None:
   228→    """Sync state status for skipped issues that are still 'open'.
   229→
   230→    Ensures state is authoritative: temporary → deferred, triaged_out → triaged_out.
   231→    Runs on every reconcile so existing data gets migrated on next scan.
   232→    """
   233→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   234→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   235→    for fid, entry in skipped.items():
   236→        issue = issues.get(fid)
   237→        if issue is None or issue.get("status") != "open":
   238→            continue
   239→        kind = str(entry.get("kind", ""))
   240→        target_status = skip_kind_state_status(kind)
   241→        if target_status and target_status != "open":
   242→            issue["status"] = target_status
   243→
   244→
   245→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   246→    plan: PlanModel,
   247→    state: StateModel,
   248→) -> ReconcileResult:
   249→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   250→
   251→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   252→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   253→    """
   254→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   255→    result = ReconcileResult()
   256→    now = utc_now()
   257→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   258→
   259→    referenced_ids = _referenced_plan_issue_ids(plan)
   260→
   261→    # Snapshot non-epic cluster sizes before superseding so we can detect
   262→    # clusters that become empty after state reconciliation.
   263→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   264→    pre_sizes: dict[str, int] = {
   265→        name: len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   266→        for name, cluster in clusters.items()
   267→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   268→    }
   269→
   270→    # Sync state status for issues in plan.skipped that are still "open" in state.
   271→    # This migrates existing data: temporary skips → deferred, triaged_out skips → triaged_out.
   272→    _sync_skipped_issue_statuses(plan, state)
   273→
   274→    _supersede_dead_references(
   275→        plan,
   276→        state,
   277→        referenced_ids=referenced_ids,
   278→        now=now,
   279→        result=result,
   280→    )
   281→    _complete_empty_manual_clusters(plan, pre_sizes=pre_sizes, result=result)
   282→    _reconcile_epic_clusters(plan, state, result=result)
   283→
   284→    clear_focus_if_cluster_empty(plan)
   285→
   286→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   287→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   288→
   289→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   290→    if resurfaced:
   291→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   292→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   293→        # Reopen resurfaced issues in state (they were deferred)
   294→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   295→        for fid in resurfaced:
   296→            issue = issues.get(fid)
   297→            if issue and issue.get("status") == "deferred":
   298→                issue["status"] = "open"
   299→
   300→    # Prune old superseded entries
   301→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   302→    result.pruned = pruned
   303→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   304→
   305→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   306→    if result.changes > 0:
   307→        append_log_entry(
   308→            plan,
   309→            "reconcile",
   310→            issue_ids=result.superseded,
   311→            actor="system",
   312→            detail={
   313→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   314→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   315→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   316→                "clusters_completed_count": len(result.clusters_completed),
   317→            },
   318→        )
   319→
   320→    return result
   321→
   322→__all__ = [
   323→    "ReconcileResult",
   324→    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
   325→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   326→    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
   327→]
   328→
```

```
1→"""Triage sync — inject/prune triage stage IDs based on review issue changes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
     7→    TRIAGE_IDS,
     8→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
     9→    QueueSyncResult,
    10→    confirmed_triage_stage_names,
    11→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix,
    12→    recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→
    18→from .defer_policy import (
    19→    DeferEscalationOptions,
    20→    DeferUpdateOptions,
    21→    should_escalate_defer_state,
    22→    update_defer_state,
    23→)
    24→from .triage_start_policy import decide_triage_start
```
